# arXiv Daily · 2026-10-14 · 232 papers

### Real-Space Interference from Quantum Metric
- **arXiv**: [2610.14048](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14048)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14048.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Real-Space Interference from Quantum Metric
- **Authors**: Bo-Qing Wu, Xing-Lei Ma, Jin-Xin Hu, Jinchao Zhao, K. T. Law
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, cond-mat.supr-con, quant-ph
- **TL;DR**: 在晶体孤立能带中，量子度规（quantum metric）在动量空间的热点分布可通过局域束缚态的倏逝尾产生实空间干涉；两者间存在直接对应关系，并在近平坦带中尤为显著。

Real-space interference of wave packets is governed by their central momentum separation and momentum width, which set the oscillation wavelength and spatial envelope, respectively. Here, we show that in crystalline systems, quantum metric distribution in an isolated band can similarly give rise to real-space interference through the evanescent tails of a localized bound state. The location and width of a quantum metric hot spot in the momentum space set a complex wavevector as one evanescent plane wave component of a bound state. Consequently, with multiple quantum metric hot spots, the density distribution of a bound state or the total density of two spatially overlapped bound states exhibits an interference pattern, where the oscillation period and long-range decay length are determined by the momentum-space separation of the hot spots and the width of the narrowest hot spots, respectively. This quantum-geometric interference is particularly pronounced in (nearly) flat bands, where competing scales from band dispersion are suppressed. Furthermore, we demonstrate that using a Josephson STM the real-space interference can be experimentally observed through the tip-position dependence of the Josephson critical current. Our results reveal a direct correspondence between momentum-space quantum geometry and real-space interference, showing how bound-state profiles encode the momentum-resolved quantum metric.

### Hierarchical Multi-Agent Reasoning Systems for Knowledge-Grounded Material Substitution
- **arXiv**: [2610.14123](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14123)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14123.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Hierarchical Multi-Agent Reasoning Systems for Knowledge-Grounded Material Substitution
- **Authors**: Tarjei Paule Hage, Subhadeep Pal, Wei Lu, Yu-Chuan Hsu, Gayla Lyon, Jiezhu Jin, Markus J. Buehler
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cond-mat.mes-hall, cs.LG
- **TL;DR**: 提出分层多智能体推理系统MARS，用于科学约束下的材料替代，并以PFAS替代为案例验证，整体性能优于对照且固定骨干模型后仍有提升。

AI-assisted scientific discovery increasingly seeks to move beyond generating plausible ideas toward reproducible, constraint-aware workflows that can support practical engineering decisions. We present MARS, a hierarchical Multi-Agent Reasoning System for knowledge-grounded material substitution under scientific and manufacturing constraints. We apply MARS to replace per- and polyfluoroalkyl substances (PFAS), a demanding materials-design problem in which substitutes must satisfy functional performance, environmental, experimental, and processing requirements. MARS decomposes substitution into a closed-loop workflow comprising requirement analysis, grounded candidate generation, and manufacturability assessment with process design. The system integrates retrieval-augmented generation, knowledge-graph reasoning, a database of lab-available materials, persistent memory of intermediate results, and rejection-aware iterative feedback. In a PFAS-substitution case study, MARS generates structured material requirements, identifies an evidence-grounded substitute candidate, and develops a lab-scale manufacturing route. In a multi-seed ablation evaluation, MARS achieves the highest overall performance across all three subsystems, with gains that persist when the backbone model is held fixed. These results suggest that hierarchical decomposition, knowledge grounding, and manufacturability-aware refinement improve material-substitution reasoning beyond model capability alone.

### IceCube data for astrophysical neutrinos exhibit q-statistical behavior
- **arXiv**: [2610.14129](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14129)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14129.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: IceCube data for astrophysical neutrinos exhibit q-statistical behavior
- **Authors**: Constantino Tsallis, Ugur Tirnakli
- **Categories**: cond-mat.stat-mech, astro-ph.HE, hep-ph
- **TL;DR**: 用q-高斯拟合IceCube天体物理中微子能谱，得到非可加指数q≈1.6，显著高于宇宙线(≈1.2)和LHC(≈1.1)，暗示超长程时空关联。

Relevant IceCube recent data for astrophysical neutrinos are analyzed. Similarly to cosmic rays and proton-proton LHC collisions, the corresponding spectra are satisfactorily described, for the entire interval of measured energies, through q-Gaussians. This fact reinforces the already available evidence that nonadditive entropies (either of the $S_q$ class or of the $S_{delta}$ one, or even alternative ones) appear to play a crucial role in systems such as black holes and related cosmological phenomena (e.g., dark energy). The IceCube data yield sensibly high values for the momenta-distribution nonadditive index (q \simeq 1.6), in contrast with the cosmic rays values (q \simeq 1.2) and the LHC values (q \simeq 1.1). This fact neatly indicates very-long-ranged space-time correlations. The detailed associated physical mechanisms remain, at the present stage, as an instigating open issue.

### Electric-dipole-induced nuclear electric resonance in silicon
- **arXiv**: [2610.14139](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14139)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14139.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Electric-dipole-induced nuclear electric resonance in silicon
- **Authors**: Jiyun Su, Shihang Zhang, Peihao Huang
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, quant-ph
- **TL;DR**: 该文理论提出利用超精细相互作用与磁场梯度实现快速的电偶极诱导核电共振（EDNER），在特定工作点下提升抗电噪声能力，为硅基可扩展量子计算提供全电学、可寻址的核自旋控制方案。

The nuclear spin of a phosphorus atom in silicon is considered as a promising quantum information carrier for quantum computing due to its long coherence time. Coherent control of the nuclear spin is typically realized using nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR). In this work, we theoretically propose that with the aid of hyperfine interactions and magnetic field gradients, fast electric-dipole-induced nuclear electric resonance (EDNER) can be achieved. The stronger EDNER (compared with NMR) arises from the combined effects of hyperfine interaction between the nuclear and electron spins and coupling between the electron spin and charge degrees of freedom mediated by magnetic field gradients in this scheme. By operating at specific working points, the nuclear-spin splitting becomes less sensitive to electrical noise, enabling long coherence times and high-fidelity quantum operations. All elctrical, addressable control of nuclear spins paves the way for scalable silicon-based quantum computing.

### In-Situ TEM Observation of Solid-State Phase Evolution in High-Entropy Alloy Nanoparticles
- **arXiv**: [2610.14147](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14147)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14147.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: In-Situ TEM Observation of Solid-State Phase Evolution in High-Entropy Alloy Nanoparticles
- **Authors**: Nirmal Kumar Katiyar, Noorbasha Bhavani Sai, Guilherme Gurian Dariani, Douglas S. Galvao, Pulickel M. Ajayan, Pedro Alves da Silva Autreto, Sriram Vijayan, Chandra Sekhar Tiwary
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 本文利用原位HRTEM观察CoCrFeMnNi高熵合金纳米团簇在室温、550°C和850°C下的相变与原子迁移行为，并用分子动力学模拟加以验证。

The emergence of new materials has gone beyond conventional design principles. Among them are high entropy alloys (HEAs), in which multiple metallic elements coexist within a single unit cell. In this case, each atomic position in a solid material has a different chemical environment, leading to configurational entropy as high as that in the fully random or melted state, which has been frozen to a solid solution. However, the behaviour of such alloy systems at pre-melting temperatures is largely unknown, where atoms gain sufficient mobility and time to rearrange, leading to an enthalpic rather than an entropic interplay. This study demonstrated the phase transformation of HEA nanoclusters (CoCrFeMnNi) under high-resolution transmission electron microscopy (HRTEM) at different temperatures, including room temperature (RT), 550 °C, and 850 °C. The clustered nanoparticles coarsen into larger clusters, accompanied by compositional heterogeneity due to surface and lattice diffusion. This experimental finding of atomic mobility was further corroborated using molecular dynamics, which provides a detailed mechanistic description.

### Exact SU($N$) symmetry resolution of bosonic Hubbard models: general boson-fermion mappings and flat-band singlet-cluster states
- **arXiv**: [2610.14183](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14183)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14183.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Exact SU($N$) symmetry resolution of bosonic Hubbard models: general boson-fermion mappings and flat-band singlet-cluster states
- **Authors**: Loïc Herviou
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.quant-gas, math-ph
- **TL;DR**: 利用U(L)–U(N) Howe对偶，可在各个SU(N)不可约表示中分别对SU(N)对称Bose-Hubbard模型做精确对角化，得到三条普适结构结论及若干应用。

We show how to perform exact diagonalization of SU($N$)-symmetric Bose-Hubbard models separately in each irreducible representation of SU($N$), by exploiting the Howe duality between U($L$) and U($N$). Three structural consequences follow for any lattice and any Hamiltonian built from the U($L$) generators: (i) the spectrum in a given orbital sector is independent of $N$ and of the particle statistics, (ii) the canonical partition functions for bosons and fermions obey the exact identity $Z_{\mathrm{b}}(-N)=(-1)^{N_\mathrm{b}}Z_{\mathrm{f}}(N)$, (iii) with unfrustrated hopping the groundstate is always a color ferromagnet, so that the color-resolved method we introduce is useful to study excited sectors, frustrated lattices and finite temperatures. As applications, we first derive the finite-temperature phase diagram of attractive SU($N$) bosons on few-site rings at large $N$ for arbitrary filling. We then show the emergence of singlet-cluster states from frustrated hopping, the counterpart of fermionic ferromagnetism, with the example of the sawtooth and kagome lattice.

### Precursor stoichiometry controls dopant structure, defect chemistry, and valence in Mn-doped ZnGa$_2$O$_4$ nanocrystals
- **arXiv**: [2610.14195](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14195)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14195.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Precursor stoichiometry controls dopant structure, defect chemistry, and valence in Mn-doped ZnGa$_2$O$_4$ nanocrystals
- **Authors**: Mátyás M. Rudolf, Gábor Bortel, Nikoletta Jegenyés, Árpád Jakab, László Bencs, Ferenc Simon, Adam Gali, Dávid Beke
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, physics.chem-ph, physics.optics
- **TL;DR**: 通过调节Zn/Ga比与退火温度，发现宿主阳离子比是决定Mn掺杂ZnGa2O4纳米晶发光的关键变量，最亮组分并非化学计量比，而取决于过量组分在趋近化学计量过程中被消耗的组成。

The luminescence of doped nanocrystals is usually optimised through the activator; here we show that the host cation ratio is an equally decisive, and largely overlooked, design variable. Mn-doped ZnGa$_2$O$_4$ nanocrystals (Mn:Zn = $4\times10^{-4}$) were grown hydrothermally at nominal Zn/Ga ratios between 0.30 and 0.75 (stoichiometric: 0.50) and post-annealed at 400--1100$^\circ$C, and the resulting composition--temperature matrix was mapped by X-ray- and UV-excited luminescence, EPR, Rietveld-refined X-ray diffraction, Raman spectroscopy and SEM. The as-grown crystals are phase-pure spinel at every composition and accommodate their off-stoichiometry internally as a point-defect inventory, reflected in a 0.4\% variation of the lattice parameter; on annealing, the excess gallium is exsolved as $β$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ in amounts set by the solution composition. The Mn$^{2+}$ emission separates into two optima, near-stoichiometric crystals annealed at 400--600$^\circ$C and Zn-excess crystals annealed at 1100$^\circ$C, the latter being twice as bright under X-ray excitation. Under X-ray and 365~nm excitation the two optima are separated by a minimum at 800$^\circ$C that coincides with a collapse of the Mn$^{2+}$ EPR population and has no counterpart in the Raman linewidths, identifying transient, defect-compensated oxidation of the dopant rather than lattice degradation as its origin. The most Zn-rich composition shows neither Mn$^{2+}$ nor Mn$^{4+}$ emission at any annealing stage, whereas its trace Cr$^{3+}$ emission is strong, indicating that Mn is rejected already during growth. Solution stoichiometry therefore acts on two levels, dopant capture and native-defect inventory, while annealing controls dopant valence and lattice order. The brightest high-temperature phosphor is thus not the nominally stoichiometric one, but the composition whose excess is consumed on its way to stoichiometry.

### Anisotropic pressure response of thermal conductivity in crystalline polytetrafluoroethylene
- **arXiv**: [2610.14220](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14220)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14220.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Anisotropic pressure response of thermal conductivity in crystalline polytetrafluoroethylene
- **Authors**: Maissa Adi, Jamal Abou Haibeh, Samuel Huberman
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 本文用第一性原理计算探究正交晶Form III PTFE在1.503、3.452、5.056 GPa加压下热导率的各向异性响应，发现沿链方向热导率随压力持续下降，而横向分量在高压阶段回升。

Compression can change thermal conductivity differently along polymer chains and across them by altering phonon velocities and lifetimes. We examine this directional response in orthorhombic Form III polytetrafluoroethylene (PTFE) at calculated pressures of 1.503, 3.452, and 5.056 GPa, using first-principles harmonic and cubic force constants within the single-mode relaxation-time approximation (SMRTA). At 100, 200, and 300 K, the chain-axis conductivity decreases at every pressure step. Between 3.452 and 5.056 GPa, both transverse components recover: at 300 K they increase by 23.7% and 13.7%, while the longitudinal conductivity decreases by 14.5%. An exact factorization separates the conductivity into a heat-capacity--velocity factor and a weighted effective lifetime. The transverse recovery follows from larger heat-capacity-weighted squared velocities, consistent with stiffer interchain restoring forces. Along the chains, shorter effective lifetimes dominate, and both conductivity-weighted Normal and Umklapp rates increase at 300 K. Comparing the same cubic force-constant blocks at all three pressures, we find that interchain coupling is already substantial at 1.503 GPa and increases under compression.

### Emergence of quantum transport in SnTe films upon structural refinement
- **arXiv**: [2610.14233](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14233)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14233.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Emergence of quantum transport in SnTe films upon structural refinement
- **Authors**: Shrinkhala Sharma, Rabindra Basnet, Thomas Parker, Afolabi Akinfaderin, Alexander Samokhvalov, Rajeswari Kolagani, Ramesh C. Budhani
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 该文研究Bi2Te3缓冲层上脉冲激光烧蚀生长的SnTe薄膜，通过退火调控织构与化学计量比，并用磁输运（WAL/HLN分析）表征表面态的相干输运。

Topological crystalline insulators are distinguished from the conventional Z2 topological insulators by crystalline symmetry protection of the surface states. Quantitative characterization of these states requires magneto-transport measurements from which the phase coherence length and number of independent coherent channels can be extracted. Here we report such measurements on textured thin films of SnTe grown on Bi2Te3 buffered c-plane sapphire (Al2O3) by pulsed laser ablation. A post deposition annealing protocol was implemented to control the crystallographic texture, which reveals improved crystallinity and enhanced epitaxial alignment along the [111] direction in films annealed at elevated temperatures. The higher carrier density and lower resistivity seen in films annealed at 100 oC are suggestive of Sn vacancies which act as native acceptor defects, while films annealed at 200 oC approach the 1:1 stoichiometry. The magnetoresistance of all samples at T &lt; 10 K characterized by a cusp centered around zero-field is suggestive of weak antilocalization and interpreted in the framework of the Hikami-Larkin-Nagaoka theory in the strong spin-orbit coupling limit. At T &gt; 20 K, the MR is small and positive, and deviates from conventional Kohler scaling. An approximate collapse of the MR curves between 50 and 250 K is obtained using an empirical temperature-dependent scaling factor, indicating that changes in the Hall-derived carrier density alone cannot account for the observed scaling behavior. These results demonstrate that post growth annealing strongly influences the interplay between stoichiometry, crystallinity, and coherent transport in SnTe thin films.

### Establishing the role of thickness in LaOBr; an alternative gate dielectric for vdW heterostructures
- **arXiv**: [2610.14272](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14272)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14272.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Establishing the role of thickness in LaOBr; an alternative gate dielectric for vdW heterostructures
- **Authors**: Isaac Antoine Lagaud, Justin Boddison-Chouinard, Antoine Labbé, Ayse Melis Aygar, Alex Bogan, Li-Lin Tay, Takashi Taniguchi, Kenji Watanabe, Aljoscha Söll, Zdeněk Sofer, Adina Luican-Mayer, Louis Gaudreau
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 该论文实验验证层状介电材料 LaOBr 作为二维范德华异质结构栅介质的潜力：体介电常数实测为 12.2（理论值 12.5），击穿场与 hBN 相当而介电常数高 2–3 倍，但存在界面死层效应。

In electronic devices, the quality of the dielectric material is determined by figures of merit such as the band gap, leakage onset field, breakdown field and the number of built-in defects, which can all limit device performance. An additional and important metric to consider is the strength of the dielectric constant. Hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) is the benchmark gate dielectric for two-dimensional van der Waals heterostructures, owing to its wide bandgap, and atomically flat interface. At the same time, its dielectric constant (2--4) highlights the opportunity to explore alternative higher-permittivity van der Waals dielectrics that provide stronger electrostatic control. Previous work established lanthanum oxybromide (LaOBr), a layered dielectric with a predicted dielectric constant of 12.5, as a potential dielectric for van der Waals heterostructures. Using graphene field-effect devices with LaOBr, we show that the leakage-onset and breakdown fields decrease systematically with thickness. Dual-gate capacitance measurements combined with a dead-layer model reveal a bulk dielectric constant of $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{bulk}} = 12.2$, in agreement with theory, while interfacial dead-layers cause the effective dielectric constant to vary from 6 to 11 across multiple thicknesses. Benchmarked against established gate dielectrics, LaOBr combines breakdown fields comparable to hBN with dielectric constants 2 to 3 times larger. By establishing the operating regime of devices with various thicknesses, we further confirm the advantage of LaOBr over hBN for high-capacitance gate-dielectric operation. These results validate LaOBr as a promising dielectric for integration into two-dimensional heterostructures, and establish thickness-dependent benchmarking as essential for evaluating emerging layered dielectric materials.

### Benchmarking machine-learning interatomic potentials using classical force fields
- **arXiv**: [2610.14239](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14239)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14239.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Benchmarking machine-learning interatomic potentials using classical force fields
- **Authors**: Johannes Sandberg, Geneviève Dusson, Julien Lam
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 本文用Lennard-Jones和Stillinger-Weber等经典势作为基准，系统比较线性原子团簇展开、Behler-Parrinello高维神经网络势和基于图神经网络的MACE势在拟合能力、抗噪性、碳足迹及径向分布函数重现上的表现。

We propose a rational comparison between several well-known machine-learning interatomic potentials (linear atomic cluster expansion, the Behler-Parrinello high-dimensional neural network potential, and the graph neural network-based MACE potential) by testing their performance for fitting classical potentials such as the Lennard-Jones and Stillinger-Weber potential. With this we assess their advantages and limitations regarding several aspects: varying training dataset size, robustness to noise, carbon footprint, as well as the ability to reproduce the radial distribution function as an example observable. Overall the MACE models are shown to most accurately reproduce the potential energy surface, with little sign of overfitting, but at a significant computational cost, highlighting important questions regarding the choice of models in computational materials science.

### Direct Observation of Chirality-Dependent Berry Curvature Dipole via Cross-Axial Nonlinear Hall Effect in Trigonal Te
- **arXiv**: [2610.14367](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14367)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14367.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Direct Observation of Chirality-Dependent Berry Curvature Dipole via Cross-Axial Nonlinear Hall Effect in Trigonal Te
- **Authors**: Jaehyun Lee, Junhee Shin, Daryll J. C. Dalayoan, Miju Park, Changhee Sohn, Gang Qiu, Noejung Park, Binghai Yan, Tony Low, Hosub Jin, Seon Namgung
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 三角碲(Te)通过跨轴非线性霍尔效应测得对映体依赖的Berry曲率偶极(BCD),可作为手性控制量子几何的对称性选择探针。

Quantum geometric quantities, such as Berry curvature (BC) and quantum metric, are known to govern the physical and topological properties of materials, making access to these quantities critically important for the comprehensive understanding of the materials. Chiral crystals offer a natural platform in which structural handedness can control quantum geometry and nonlinear transport, but direct experimental access to the quantum geometric quantities has remained elusive. Here, we show that trigonal tellurium (Te) hosts an enantiomer-dependent Berry curvature dipole (BCD) that can be accessed through the measurement of cross-axial nonlinear Hall effect (NHE), unlike previously studied in-plane configurations where the BCD contribution vanishes. Angle-resolved measurements on left- and right-handed Te devices reveal a second-order transverse response whose sign reverses with crystal chirality. The extracted cross-axial nonlinear susceptibility, $χ_{yxz}$, exceeds the in-plane susceptibility, $χ_{xxx}$, by more than two-folds, indicating a strong BCD-driven contribution. First-principles calculations attribute this behavior to a topologically nontrivial hedgehog-like BC texture near the H points and predict a BCD that reverses sign between opposite enantiomers, consistent with experiment. These results establish cross-axial nonlinear Hall transport as a symmetry-selective probe of chirality-controlled quantum geometry in tellurium and related chiral materials.

### Mn--Cr dopant association in dilute co-doped $\mathrm{ZnAl_2O_4}$ revealed by combined EPR and optical spectroscopy
- **arXiv**: [2610.14262](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14262)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14262.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Mn--Cr dopant association in dilute co-doped $\mathrm{ZnAl_2O_4}$ revealed by combined EPR and optical spectroscopy
- **Authors**: Apoko S. Omondi, Mátyás M. Rudolf, Nikoletta Jegenyés, Árpád Jakab, Gábor Bortel, Bence G. Márkus, Adam Gali, Dávid Beke
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, physics.chem-ph
- **TL;DR**: 通过EPR自旋计数与多种光谱手段，在ZnAl2O4:Mn/Cr中揭示Cr–Mn边共享交换耦合缺陷复合物的形成，其按质量作用定律调控，是共掺荧光粉增亮的真正机制而非能量传递。

Efficient and durable phosphors made from abundant elements underpin energy-saving solid-state lighting, near-infrared light sources for sensing, and X-ray and biomedical imaging. Co-doping is a standard route to brighter phosphors, yet the fate of the co-dopants is rarely established. We combine quantitative electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR) spin counting with photoluminescence, photoluminescence excitation, and X-ray-excited spectroscopy across two dilute Mn/Cr co-doping series of hydrothermally grown $\mathrm{ZnAl_2O_4}$ nanoparticles and show that the two ions can form an exchange-coupled, edge-sharing Cr--Mn defect complex, with $\mathrm{Mn^{2+/4+}}$ entering an octahedral site next to $\mathrm{Cr^{3+}}$. The octahedral Mn lifts the inversion symmetry of the $\mathrm{Cr^{3+}}$ site and strengthens its parity-forbidden absorption, enhancing the $\mathrm{Cr^{3+}}$ emission without energy transfer. The population of the defect complex follows the law of mass action, and above a threshold Mn concentration, Mn re-partitions into EPR-silent octahedral configurations that quench both emissions. Concentration optima in co-doped phosphors can thus reflect defect speciation, not only energy-transfer efficiency, which opens a structural design handle for brighter emitters.

### Transport kinetics in excitonic Mott insulator
- **arXiv**: [2610.14426](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14426)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14426.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Transport kinetics in excitonic Mott insulator
- **Authors**: W. J. Brunner, Zhiwen Zhou, Luc M. Nguyen, C. A. Lozano, G. Mercado, L. H. Fowler-Gerace, L. V. Butov
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 该文在MoSe₂/WSe₂异质结中研究间接激子的输运动力学，通过超高的激子迁移率排除无序局域化，并观察到激子云随密度增加先膨胀后收缩的现象，直接指示Mott绝缘体相的出现。

Indirect excitons (IXs) in a periodic moiré potential in a van der Waals heterostructure form an experimental platform for exploring Bose-Hubbard physics, including the superfluid state and the Mott insulator state. The realization of these collective states requires low-disorder materials. In particular, the localization due to the Mott insulator must be distinguished from disorder-induced localization. We studied the transport kinetics of IXs in a MoSe$_2$/WSe$_2$ van der Waals heterostructure with an anomalously high IX mobility, consistent with superfluidity. This high-mobility transport demonstrates that disorder-induced localization is negligible. We found a remarkable suppression of this efficient IX transport, directly indicating the Mott insulator. As the IX density increases during the excitation pulse, the IX cloud initially expands and then contracts upon entering the Mott insulator phase. The emergence of IX cloud contraction is accompanied by a peak in the IX spatial distribution at the origin. Our simulations reproduce the observed non-monotonic IX transport kinetics, including the temporal expansion and contraction of the IX cloud, as well as the peak in its spatial distribution.

### A Solution-Processed Ti/Pt/NiOx/Ag RRAM Device with Multiple Forming-Free Non-Volatile Intermediate States
- **arXiv**: [2610.14322](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14322)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14322.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: A Solution-Processed Ti/Pt/NiOx/Ag RRAM Device with Multiple Forming-Free Non-Volatile Intermediate States
- **Authors**: Abhrajit Roy, Chopali Chanchal Sahu, Rudraksh Sangore, Raman Hissariya, Poulomi Chakrabarty, Harsh Roniyar, Merlyne De Souza, Debanjan Bhowmik
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, physics.app-ph
- **TL;DR**: 基于溶液法制备的NiO基RRAM器件（Ti/Pt底电极、Ag顶电极）实现免形成（forming-free）的二进制电阻切换与脉冲驱动的LTP/LTD，具有约30个中间态（约5-bit权重分辨率），多数态在1000秒内保持稳定；并用实测LTP-LTD特性校准MLP与CNN，在MNIST与CIFAR-10上完成分类。

Artificial neural networks (ANN) implemented on crossbar arrays of non-volatile synaptic devices are known to consume much less energy compared to that implemented on conventional computing units. Solution-processed resistive random-access memory (RRAM) devices based on transition-metal oxides are suitable as such synaptic devices because of their cheap and easy fabrication method, stability, and suitable memristive properties. Here, in NiO-based RRAM devices with Ti/Pt as the bottom electrode and Ag as the top electrode (NiO layer deposited through solution processing), we experimentally report forming-free binary switching between a high resistance state (HRS) and a low resistance state (LRS) as well as forming-free pulse-driven modulation (long-term potentiation (LTP), long-term depression (LTD)) through 30 intermediate synaptic states between HRS and LRS (around 5-bit synaptic weight resolution). Many of these states are measured to be stable at least up to 1000 seconds confirming their non-volatility and their utility in storage of synaptic weights for ANN crossbar arrays. On the contrary, most prior experimental reports (including reports both on solution-processing-based and sputtering-based RRAM devices) require forming to exhibit non-volatility. Avoiding forming reduces variability, eliminates the need for high voltage pulses, and makes the crossbar array design simpler. Further, we confirm the presence of oxygen vacancies in our materials stack using X-ray photo-electron spectroscopy (XPS). Finally, we show successful data classification on MNIST and CIFAR-10 data sets using a multi-layer perceptron (MLP) model and a convolution neural network (CNN) model respectively, where the synaptic weight updates of these ANN models are calibrated against the experimentally measured LTP-LTD characteristics of the NiO RRAM devices.

### Competitive scaling of small-subsystem-optimization heuristics for LABS
- **arXiv**: [2610.14361](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14361)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14361.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Competitive scaling of small-subsystem-optimization heuristics for LABS
- **Authors**: Ginger E. Lau, Adil A. Gangat
- **Categories**: cond-mat.stat-mech, quant-ph
- **TL;DR**: 通过迭代优化与系统解耦的小子系统（n=4个自旋），在LABS问题上实现了O(1.333^N)的时间缩放，接近经典最优水平，说明小规模专用硬件也具有实用价值。

The low autocorrelation binary sequences (LABS) problem, often formulated as a spin-lattice energy-minimization problem, is a combinatorial optimization problem with a very rugged energy landscape. We demonstrate that iterative optimization of very small subsystems of n spins that are decoupled from the rest of the system achieves global optimization of LABS with a time-to-solution scaling that is competitive with heuristics that optimize the global system of N spins directly. Our isolated-subsystem heuristic with n = 4 achieves a time scaling of O(1.333^N ) on the interval N = [27, 60], while the current state-of-the-art classical and quantum scalings are respectively O(1.34^N ) and O(1.21^N ). Our results suggest that specialized optimization hardware with only small numbers of logical spins may be of practical relevance.

### Magnon Klein paradox
- **arXiv**: [2610.14648](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14648)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14648.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Magnon Klein paradox
- **Authors**: D. Wang
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, physics.app-ph
- **TL;DR**: 该文通过分析自旋波的能量输运指出：与电子Klein佯谬不同，磁振子（magnon）Klein佯谬在反射率意义上并不存在；文献中所报告的磁振子Klein佯谬源于给透射自旋波赋予了错误的符号。

By considering the energy transport of spin wave, we show that, like the electron Klein paradox, there is no magnon Klein paradox for reflectivity. Heuristically, this can be understood according to the fact that, even if the ground state is unstable, any energy-carrying wave cannot have greater-than-one reflectivity off a passive interface, which will violate the conservation of energy. The magnon Klein paradox discussed in the literature is derived from assigning a wrong sign to the transmitted spin wave.

### Atoms that Matter: Largest Materials Datasets Are 99% Redundant, and Local Chemistry Can Guide the Required 1%
- **arXiv**: [2610.14392](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14392)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14392.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Atoms that Matter: Largest Materials Datasets Are 99% Redundant, and Local Chemistry Can Guide the Required 1%
- **Authors**: Evgeny Moerman, Igor Poltavsky, Anton Charkin-Gorbulin, Alexandre Tkatchenko
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 在OMat24上仅用650万原子（不到1%）训练MACE模型，随机、潜空间聚类（DIRECT）与新的无模型环境多样性方法（EnvTok）三种选择方式所得模型均能逼近全量数据模型，表明数据量并非可迁移性的关键，化学环境多样性才是OMat24信息量的决定因素。

General-purpose machine-learning force fields (MLFFs) for materials are now trained on the order of $100$ million structures, assuming transferability requires such scale. We test this premise on OMat24, the largest available materials dataset, by training MACE models on subsets of $6.5$ million atoms, less than $1\%$ of OMat24, selected in three complementary ways: at random, by latent-space clustering of a pretrained MLFF (DIRECT), and by a new model-free method maximizing the diversity of discrete, interpretable local environments (EnvTok). All three models approach the full-data reference MACE-OMAT-0 model to within a few tens of $\mathrm{meV}/\mathring{\mathrm{A}}$ in forces, and remain close to or exceed it on formation energies, elastic moduli, heat capacities, Li$^{+}$ migration barriers, and molecular-dynamics stability up to $3000\,$K and $500\,$GPa. This near-independence from the selection strategy shows that chemical environment diversity, not size, defines OMat24's information content: the remaining $99\%$ of structures are largely redundant. Broad sampling of chemical space thus remains essential, but reference labels are needed only for structurally and chemically distinct configurations. Using atomic positions and species, optionally augmented by magnetic moments, EnvTok identifies these before any labelling or training, yielding compact, architecture-independent datasets. This hundredfold reduction makes hybrid-DFT or higher-level relabelling of general-purpose training sets feasible, and frees computational budget for extending environmental coverage and multi-fidelity learning.

### Pre-pairing and polarization of doped charge carriers in unconventional superconductors
- **arXiv**: [2610.14401](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14401)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14401.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Pre-pairing and polarization of doped charge carriers in unconventional superconductors
- **Authors**: Lixin Zhang, Mengyan Nie, Yan Cheng, Ran Zhao
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con
- **TL;DR**: 通过第一性原理计算发现铜氧化物中掺杂剂倾向形成二聚体并使空穴/电子预配对（Z-pair），基于强反铁磁交换能与掺杂诱导的U值突变，提出Z-pair机制解释非常规超导。

Based on first principles calculations, we show in hole-doped La2-xSrxCuO4, the Sr dopants prefer to form dimers, rather than be isolated as generally believed. Same trend applies to other superconductors such as Na-doped Ca2CuO2Cl2, and electron type Nd2-xCexCuO4. The dimer structure makes the two doped holes (electrons) bound up and form a singlet which is also polarized. Another type of dopant in cuprates is oxygen which behaves similarly. So, we propose that, for all oxide high TC superconductors, the holes (electrons) are pre-paired (Z-pairs) during the preparation of the superconductor. The spontaneously formed Z-pairs are conceptually different from not only the Cooper pairs, but also the Zhang-Rice singlets. The attraction force in a Z-pair originates basically from the strong antiferromagnetic exchange energy. We also found that the on-site Coulomb repulsion (U) is no longer a constant due to doping. Around a doping site, U can reduce its value discontinuously, which plays a key role in stabilizing the Z-pairs against isolated holes (electrons). A Z-pair is a p-wave like localized state which breaks the symmetry of the CuO2 plain and attracts each other along the direction perpendicular to the polarization. The Z-pairs may be popular in all of the unconventional superconductors and the periodic assembly can result in the emergence of coherence states and superconductivity.

### Valley-resolved high harmonics probe symmetry breaking beyond the Dirac cone
- **arXiv**: [2610.15251](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15251)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15251.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Valley-resolved high harmonics probe symmetry breaking beyond the Dirac cone
- **Authors**: Pablo Pais, Alfredo Iorio, Marcelo F. Ciappina
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, hep-th, physics.atom-ph
- **TL;DR**: 利用强反向旋转ω-2ω场和谷分辨高次谐波产生(HHG)，可将石墨烯中的三角扭曲(trigonal warping)作为"紫外对称性破缺修正"探测出来，谐波谷对比度是灵敏探针。

Low-energy electrons in graphene obey an emergent, rotationally invariant Dirac equation, while the honeycomb lattice enters only through higher-order, symmetry-breaking corrections, the leading one being trigonal warping. This provides a unique solid-state arena to address a cornerstone challenge in quantum gravity: how to detect ultraviolet symmetry-breaking granular corrections, suppressed by a fundamental scale, atop an emergent infrared relativistic continuum. We show that intense counter-rotating $ω$-$2ω$ fields and valley-resolved high-harmonic generation (HHG) provide such a probe. Within a family of models that interpolates continuously between the isotropic Dirac cone and nearest-neighbour graphene at fixed Fermi velocity, the valley contrast of the harmonic yield vanishes identically in the symmetric theory, grows linearly with the warping coupling, and its valley-even counterpart appears only at second order, as expected from effective-field-theory power counting. The stronger the laser, the farther it drives electrons from the Dirac points, so the laser intensity plays the role of the collision energy. For the fourth harmonic, the leading warping correction alone accounts for the valley contrast of real graphene up to fundamental intensities of about $10^{11}$ W/cm$^2$, below the damage threshold of monolayer graphene, whereas the fifth harmonic already reveals higher-order lattice corrections at intensities around $10^{10}$ W/cm$^2$. The two harmonics, which rotate in opposite directions, are emitted by opposite valleys. Valley-resolved harmonics are far more sensitive than the residual valley populations, making them a practical detector of lattice-scale physics hidden beneath an emergent Dirac theory.

### Large Out-of-plane Polarization in Two-dimensional Bi2O3 Monolayers Predicted by an AI-driven Inverse-design Workflow
- **arXiv**: [2610.14429](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14429)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14429.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Large Out-of-plane Polarization in Two-dimensional Bi2O3 Monolayers Predicted by an AI-driven Inverse-design Workflow
- **Authors**: Hongmei Tan, Jing Zhang, Shan Gao, Yueyu Zhang, Xiangmei Duan
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 通过逆向结构设计结合机器学习势与第一性原理计算，在Bi2O3体系中发现了两种新型二维极性单层（四方相和正交相），其面外极化分别达34.12和37.15 pC/m，其中o-Bi2O3为已报道二维氧化物中最大的面外极化之一。

Two-dimensional (2D) polar oxides with built-in electric fields offer promising opportunities for nanoscale electronics, optoelectronics, and surface charge engineering. However, their discovery remains challenging due to the enormous structural complexity of nonlayered oxide networks. Here, we combine inverse structural design with a machine-learning interatomic potential and first-principles calculations to explore 2D oxide configurations. Using Bi2O3 as a representative system, we identify two previously unreported polar monolayers with tetragonal and orthorhombic symmetries. Both structures exhibit vertically separated Bi and O sublayers, giving rise to pronounced intrinsic polarity with Berry-phase 2D polarizations of 34.12 and 37.15 pC/m, respectively. Notably, the o-Bi2O3 monolayer exhibits one of the largest reported out-of-plane polarizations among 2D oxides. Both monolayers exhibit dynamical stability, finite-temperature structural integrity, and wide band gaps (~3.8 eV). The large polarization originates from dimensionality-induced reconstruction of the nonlayered Bi-O network, which breaks local coordination symmetry and produces out-of-plane charge redistribution, while the stereochemically active Bi 6s2 lone pair further favors asymmetric Bi-O bonding. These findings suggest dimensionality-induced local-coordination symmetry breaking as a promising design motif for high-polarization 2D oxides, and demonstrate the utility of combining inverse design with machine-learning-assisted screening to explore unconventional polar phases in nonlayered oxide networks.

### Extended-range percolation on lattices with cubic and non-compact neighborhoods in three dimensions
- **arXiv**: [2610.15441](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15441)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15441.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Extended-range percolation on lattices with cubic and non-compact neighborhoods in three dimensions
- **Authors**: Zhe Zhang, Yuxuan Fan, Yang Yang, Yusong Tu, Zhipeng Xun
- **Categories**: cond-mat.stat-mech
- **TL;DR**: 该文用单团簇生长算法的蒙特卡罗模拟研究三维扩展邻域渗流模型（立方与非紧致邻域），获得约100种结构的高精度渗流阈值，并揭示阈值与晶格拓扑（配位数z、第i配位区位点数z_i与距离r_i）的关系。

Using MC simulations based on an efficient single-cluster growth algorithm, we investigate two types of three-dimensional extended-range percolation models, namely cubic and complex (non-compact) neighborhoods. We obtain high-precision percolation thresholds $p_c$ for approximately 100 distinct structures and further explore the relationship between these thresholds and the topology of the lattices, which includes the total coordination number $z$, the number of sites $z_i$ and these sites' distance to the central site $r_i$ of the given neighborhood in the $i$th coordination zone. Both bond and site percolation are analyzed for cubic neighborhoods on the SC lattice. For bond percolation, the asymptotic value of $zp_c$ tends to Bethe-lattice behavior ($zp_c\sim1$), and finite-$z$ correction is found to be consistent with $zp_{c}-1\sim a_{1}z^{-x}$, where $x=(d-1)/d$. For site percolation, the value of $zp_c$ varies non-monotonically, exhibiting an initial increase followed by a decrease with the coordination number $z$. Linear fitting leads to $zp_c\sim2.5987(1)$ for large $z$, predicting a value of continuum percolation threshold of aligned cubes $η_c=0.324838(12)$ using $zp_c=2^{d}η_c$. We then examine site percolation for non-compact neighborhoods extending to the fifth coordination zone on SC, FCC, and BCC lattices. Through a comparison of the three indices of $z$, $ξ=\sum_iz_ir_i^2/i$, and $ζ=\sum_iz_ir_i$, we find that $ζ$ is effective in removing the degeneracy. The results obtained show power-law dependence $p_c\propto(ζ/K)^{-γ}$ with $γ_{\mathrm{SC}}^ζ=0.703(12)$, $γ_{\mathrm{FCC}}^ζ=0.728(10)$, and $γ_{\mathrm{BCC}}^ζ=0.699(12)$ for SC, FCC, and BCC lattices, and $p_c\proptoζ^{-γ}$ with $γ^ζ=0.719(9)$ independent of the underlying lattice. The number $K$ indicates lattice connectivity.

### Fluctuation-based evaluation of thermal conductivity using deviational phonon Monte Carlo simulations
- **arXiv**: [2610.14433](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14433)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14433.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Fluctuation-based evaluation of thermal conductivity using deviational phonon Monte Carlo simulations
- **Authors**: Takuma Hori, Yuzuho Oki
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 提出一种基于涨落的蒙特卡洛方法，在声子BTE框架下由偏差声子能量的均方位移直接计算热导率，无需热库，可在单次模拟中得到多方向热导率。

We develop a fluctuation based Monte Carlo method for evaluating thermal conductivity within the phonon Boltzmann transport equation (BTE) framework. Starting from the energy based variance reduced formulation of the BTE, we derive a relation between the spatial spreading of deviational phonon energy and the macroscopic heat conduction equation. The resulting formulation expresses thermal conductivity in terms of the mean square displacement of deviational phonon particles, without imposing thermal reservoirs. Consequently, thermal conductivity along multiple directions can be evaluated from a single simulation. The method is demonstrated for crystalline silicon at room temperature in bulk, square nanowires, and anisotropic polycrystalline nanostructures. The calculated thermal conductivities agree with theoretical predictions, including the size dependence induced by phonon boundary scattering and the mode-resolved contributions. For anisotropic polycrystalline structures, the method simultaneously resolves the directional thermal conductivities and captures their dependence on grain morphology. These results demonstrate the validity of the proposed fluctuation-based approach for evaluating thermal conductivity across different transport regimes and structural configurations.

### Microscopic foundation for orbitronics: orbital exchange interactions, orbital-dependent transport, and orbital switching
- **arXiv**: [2610.15374](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15374)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15374.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Microscopic foundation for orbitronics: orbital exchange interactions, orbital-dependent transport, and orbital switching
- **Authors**: Hantao Zhang, Ran Cheng
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cond-mat.other, cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 该文将Anderson-Kondo模型推广至包含导带轨道自由度，建立轨道交换相互作用的理论框架，揭示轨道极化如何产生并反过来影响输运与磁动力学，为"轨道电子学"奠定微观基础。

Recent experiments highlighted the essential role of the orbital degrees of freedom underlying current-induced magnetic dynamics. However, a microscopic picture of how orbital angular momenta are transferred between itinerant and localized electrons, and the way it feeds back into transport, remains elusive. Here we develop a theoretical framework for the orbital exchange interactions by generalizing the Anderson-Kondo model to incorporate the orbitals of conduction bands. Illustrating with the $p$-orbitals, we determine the way electrons acquire orbital polarization and its influence on the transport properties, uncovering the orbital counterparts of phenomena analogous to spin filtering and tunnel magnetoresistance. Reciprocally, we demonstrate that orbital-polarized currents can exert torques on localized magnetic moments without having to convert into spin currents, which enables electrical switching of magnetization even when spin polarization becomes inactive. These striking discoveries lay a microscopic foundation for orbitronics and reveal critical mechanisms for orbital manipulations of magnetic order.

### A Fisher-Geometric Approach to Supercell Pair Distribution Function Refinement
- **arXiv**: [2610.14597](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14597)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14597.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: A Fisher-Geometric Approach to Supercell Pair Distribution Function Refinement
- **Authors**: Dawei Wang
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 提出一种无矩阵、Fisher几何方法，将PDF观测似然的信息度量回传到原子坐标，实现大体系超胞的第二阶PDF精修。

Refining atomistic supercells against powder pair distribution functions (PDFs) requires optimizing many coupled atomic coordinates using a much smaller set of radial measurements. Reverse-mode differentiation makes gradients accessible for large systems, but standard first-order optimizers do not explicitly use coupled PDF sensitivities to precondition coordinate updates. We introduce a matrix-free, Fisher-geometric approach that pulls the information metric of the observation likelihood back onto atomic coordinates. The resulting update weights collective displacements according to their PDF response and experimental uncertainty. Under isotropic damping, the update is obtained by solving a damped linear system over $M$ PDF channels, using automatic differentiation to evaluate Jacobian--vector and vector--Jacobian products without constructing the full Jacobian or coordinate curvature matrix. For Gaussian measurement errors, the update recovers classical Levenberg--Marquardt refinement while avoiding dense coordinate-space factorization. The same computational framework accommodates correlated Gaussian errors and robust likelihood-based updates. This approach combines likelihood-aware collective updates with matrix-free backpropagation for direct second-order refinement of large atomistic supercells.

### Ergodicity breaking transition beyond qubits
- **arXiv**: [2610.15546](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15546)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15546.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Ergodicity breaking transition beyond qubits
- **Authors**: Tanay Pathak, Abhik Kumar Saha
- **Categories**: cond-mat.stat-mech, cond-mat.dis-nn, quant-ph
- **TL;DR**: 本文通过引入零维qudit玩具模型，系统研究了局域Hilbert空间维度对遍历性破缺转变的控制作用。利用杂化条件解析得到相互作用临界值对局域维度的依赖关系，数值结果与理论预测一致，并在高自旋模型中验证了临界行为的普适性。

The local Hilbert space dimension is a fundamental parameter in quantum many-body systems, but its role in controlling the ergodicity-breaking transition remains less explored. We systematically study this question by introducing toy models of qudits in zero dimensions that exhibit an ergodicity-breaking transition. Using the hybridization condition, we analytically obtain the dependence of the critical value of the interactions on the local Hilbert space dimension. Numerical studies of spectral correlation measures show agreement with the theoretical predictions, hence confirming the existence of the ergodicity-breaking transition in these models. We further introduce a distinct higher spin version of the model and numerically obtain the same critical behavior, demonstrating that the observed critical point is independent of the microscopic structure of interactions considered.

### Quantum Geometry and Pair Breaking in Intra- and Intervalley Superconductivity of Rhombohedral Graphene
- **arXiv**: [2610.14480](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14480)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14480.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Quantum Geometry and Pair Breaking in Intra- and Intervalley Superconductivity of Rhombohedral Graphene
- **Authors**: Louis Primeau, Yang Zhang, Shi-Zeng Lin
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con, cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 使用DFT拟合的多轨道紧束缚模型研究电子掺杂菱面体四层石墨烯中的Kohn-Luttinger超导，发现Bloch形状因子强烈偏好谷内配对（主导为p_x-ip_y），但三角翘曲导致的能带失配会截断Cooper对数、显著压缩谷内超导区域；主导不稳定性出现在配对Lifshitz转变附近及环状/三瓣费米面重构区。

The observation of superconductivity emerging from a spin- and valley-polarized normal state in rhombohedral graphene raises a basic puzzle: intravalley Cooper pairs involve states that are not generically degenerate because of trigonal warping, and therefore lack the particle--particle nesting underlying the conventional Cooper instability. While recent model studies suggest that nontrivial band geometry can strongly promote chiral pairing, how this geometric enhancement competes with realistic band-structure-induced pair breaking remains unclear. Here we study Kohn--Luttinger superconductivity in electron-doped rhombohedral tetralayer graphene using a DFT-fitted multiorbital tight-binding model, retaining the full Bloch-wave-function form factors in both RPA screening and the projected Cooper interaction. We separate the superconducting instability into three contributions: the symmetry-resolved form-factor-dressed interaction, the Cooper-pair phase space determined by the symmetrized pair dispersion, and the energy mismatch generated by trigonal warping. We find that the Bloch form factors strongly favor intravalley pairing, with a leading predominantly $p_x-ip_y$ instability and a substantially larger maximum $T_c$ than the intervalley reference state. At the same time, trigonal-warping-induced mismatch cuts off the Cooper logarithm and confines intravalley superconductivity to a much narrower region of the phase diagram. The dominant instability occurs near pair-Lifshitz transitions and primarily in reconstructed annular or three-lobed Fermi-surface regimes. Our results show that superconductivity in rhombohedral graphene is controlled by the combined effects of quantum-geometry-dressed interactions, pair fermiology, and trigonal-warping-induced pair breaking.

### A Solvable Model in the Half-filled Landau Level
- **arXiv**: [2610.15471](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15471)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15471.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: A Solvable Model in the Half-filled Landau Level
- **Authors**: Anders Karlhede
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 本文提出了半填充朗道能级中相互作用电子的一种可解微观模型。该模型的低能部分由一组相互独立、空间交错的一维无相互作用费米子偶极系统构成，每个系统具有费米海基态，其总偶极数与电子数相等，费米面态密度与二维费米气体相同。

A solvable microscopic model of interacting electrons in a half-filled Landau level is presented. The Hamiltonian is a well-defined limit of the electron-electron interaction. The low energy sector consists of a set of independent, spatially interlaced, one-dimensional systems of non-interacting fermionic dipoles, each system with a Fermi sea ground state. The total number of dipoles in the set of Fermi seas forming the two-dimensional ground state equals the number of electrons in the system and the density of states at the Fermi level is that of a two-dimensional Fermi gas. The model is obtained by generalizing, or rather adopting, the existing exact solution on a thin, but not too thin, torus to the two-dimensional bulk case.

### Enhancing Quasi-Two-Dimensional Electronic Structure in Bi2O2Se via Chlorine Substitution
- **arXiv**: [2610.14619](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14619)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14619.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Enhancing Quasi-Two-Dimensional Electronic Structure in Bi2O2Se via Chlorine Substitution
- **Authors**: Yong-Zhen Jiang, Qi-Xun Wen, Xiang Xu, Su-Tao Sun, Feng-Fan Ren, Zi-Xuan Zhou, Xing-Yuan You, Yu-Cheng Kan, Zheng Zhang, Jie-Yi Liu, Yu-Zhou Zhao, Zheng-Tai Liu, Mao Ye, Shu-Hua Yao, Jian Zhou, Yan-Bin Chen, Zhong-Kai Liu, Yu-Lin Chen, Yang-Yang Lv, Cheng Chen
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 通过Cl取代在Bi2O2Se中引入范德华间隙，在保留其优异电子特性的同时增强二维性，并实现0.8~3.5 eV宽带隙调控。

Bismuth oxyselenide (Bi2O2Se) has emerged as a leading semiconductor candidate for next-generation electronics and quantum phenomena research. However, its strong ionic interlayer bonding of Se layers fundamentally restricts the full potential of this otherwise promising quasi-two-dimensional (2D) material. In this work, we demonstrate chlorine substitution as an effective strategy to introduce van der Waals gaps and modify the electronic structure of the system. Through comprehensive transport and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) measurements on Bi6O6Se2Cl2 single crystal, an optimal Cl-doping compound, we show that the key electronic features, including indirect band gap, high electron mobility, and carrier effective mass, are well preserved, while the 2D character of the system is sufficiently enhanced. First-principles calculations attribute this selective tunability to the unique spatial separation between conductive Bi-O layers and dopant Se/Cl layers, which enables independent optimization of interlayer coupling and intralayer transport. Remarkably, our theoretical calculations combined with experimental observations project a broad bandgap engineering across a wide range (0.8 ~ 3.5 eV) within the Bi2O2Se system. Our findings not only expand the application potential of Bi2O2Se-based materials in optoelectronics, nanoelectronics, and fundamental research but also highlight spatial decoupling as a viable strategy for modifying detailed electronic structure of quasi-2D quantum systems.

### Developing the Statistical Mechanics of Evolving Assembly Spaces
- **arXiv**: [2610.15695](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15695)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15695.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Developing the Statistical Mechanics of Evolving Assembly Spaces
- **Authors**: Anisha K. Yeddanapudi, Abhishek Sharma, Ian Seet, Leroy Cronin
- **Categories**: cond-mat.stat-mech
- **TL;DR**: 该论文将装配理论（Assembly Theory, AT）与统计力学/随机热力学耦合，提出通过"装配空间"和"装配器"（比特运算表示）来刻画复杂新颖对象的构造过程，并估计其所需功，从而把选择、历史与因果偶然性纳入热力学框架。

A gap exists in connecting biological evolution within the framework of statistical mechanics. It has been hoped that statistical mechanics would provide us with a general predictive principle for the emergence of novelty and complexity. However, the emergence of novel functional features initiated by stochastic processes remains difficult to describe, because these processes commonly arise outside near-equilibrium or linear-response regimes. To understand how environmental influences generate diverse, open-ended, complex objects by driving physical systems to specific regions of phase space over long spatial and temporal scales, a new approach to non-equilibrium thermodynamics is necessary. Here, we present the coupling of assembly theory (AT) with statistical mechanics as a framework that works within the laws of thermodynamics, but can contextualise the processes of novelty, complexity, and evolution within these laws. We map construction processes from physical space to assembly space (AS) and introduce the concept of an assembler, a machine that constructs objects contingent on previous states and is represented by bitwise operations. Using this approach, we apply principles from stochastic thermodynamics with AT to define selection within large combinatorial spaces1 and estimate the work required to create novel and complex objects. This approach allows us to represent complex processes, such as selection, within large combinatorial spaces as a set of rules for a memory-feedback loop followed by the assembler. This framework concretely defines the relationships between thermodynamics and information theory by introducing a novel aspect of physics that uses history and causal contingency to influence the probabilistic time evolution of complex systems.

### Exotic metallic phase in a heterotic Kondo lattice: violation of Luttinger's theorem and absence of Kondo gap
- **arXiv**: [2610.14556](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14556)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14556.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Exotic metallic phase in a heterotic Kondo lattice: violation of Luttinger's theorem and absence of Kondo gap
- **Authors**: Hanting Zhong, Shuxiang Yang, Chao Cao, Xiao-Yong Feng, Jianhui Dai, Hai-Qing Lin
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 本文在顺磁相下研究具有异质层状结构的近藤晶格系统，发现弱近藤耦合时为分数化狄拉克液体（无质量/有质量狄拉克费米子加中性自旋子费米面），强耦合时自旋子与狄拉克费米子杂化形成近平台杂化带及导带/价带重费米子带，产生大电子费米面；其近藤能隙是否存在依赖堆叠构型，系统在半满时仍保持金属性且Luttinger定理在临界点附近被违反。

Fermi surface transformation accompanied by quantum phase transition is widely observed in correlated electron systems including heavy fermion materials and van der Waals quantum heterostructures. The unified Anderson lattice description for this phenomenon suggests that their interacting ground states in the case of electron half filling always destabilize the metallic phase in the strong Kondo-coupling regime. We examine this issue within the paramagnetic phase of a Kondo lattice system with a heterotic layered structure. In the weak Kondo-coupling regime, the system exhibits a fractional Dirac liquid involving the massless or massive Dirac fermions and a charge neutral spinon Fermi surface. In the strong Kondo-coupling regime, the spinons hybridize with the Dirac fermions resulting in an intermediate nearly-flat hybridizing band together with the conduction and valence heavy fermion bands, yielding a large electric Fermi surface. Absence of the Kondo gap between these bands is observed depending on the stacking configurations of the layered structure. In particular, the system remains metallic even at the exact half filling in this regime and Luttinger's theorem is violated across the critical point. Implications of these results on the van der Waals quantum heterostructures are briefly discussed.

### Chemical-Potential-Dependent Superconducting Spectroscopy on Bi2Te3/NbSe2 Heterostructures
- **arXiv**: [2610.14627](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14627)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14627.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Chemical-Potential-Dependent Superconducting Spectroscopy on Bi2Te3/NbSe2 Heterostructures
- **Authors**: Pengyu Hu, Chun Yu Wan, Qiaoyan Yu, Chen Qian, Yisheng Gu, Xian Du, Xuhui Ning, Tingwen Miao, Shi Chen, Zihao Ma, Hao Yang, Hemian Yi, Weijiong Chen, Xiaoxue Liu, Liang Liu, Dandan Guan, Yaoyi Li, Shiyong Wang, Canhua Liu, Sangen Zhao, Fulin Wang, Junwei Liu, Hao Zheng, Jinfeng Jia
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con
- **TL;DR**: 利用稀释制冷温度下的高分辨扫描隧道谱研究Bi2Te3/NbSe2异质结构超导性质，观察到偏离常规单s波谱形的相干峰（内拐点或内峰），证明其源于Bi2Te3体态与表面态被NbSe2单一超导能隙选择性近邻耦合，而非继承NbSe2复杂序参量。

High-resolution scanning tunneling spectroscopy performed at dilution refrigerator temperatures was employed to investigate the superconducting properties of topological insulator/superconductor (Bi2Te3/NbSe2) heterostructures. Coherence peaks with inner kinks or inner peaks, deviating from the conventional single s-wave spectral line shape, were observed in both 5 quintuple-layer (5QL) and 7QL Bi2Te3 thin films. Based on the combination of chemical-potential-dependent spectroscopic measurements and model simulations, we conclude that these spectral features arise from contributions of bulk and surface states in Bi2Te3 by selectively proximity-coupled to a single superconducting gap from NbSe2, rather than inheriting the complicated superconductor's order parameter of NbSe2.

### Approaching Irrational Quantization
- **arXiv**: [2610.15753](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15753)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15753.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Approaching Irrational Quantization
- **Authors**: Zhihong Wu, Zhimou Chen, Siyuan Zhang, Jinyang Chen, L. N. Pfeiffer, K. W. West, Yang Liu, Xi Lin
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 该文展示了一种可实验实现的有限几何网络，其归一化电阻在完全占据朗道能级时收敛到无理数 √3，实测为 1.735±0.001 h/e²，将量子化响应从有理数值推广到无理数值。

The integer and fractional quantum Hall effects reveal that quantized physical responses can take integer and fractional values, respectively. This naturally raises the question of whether quantization can extend beyond rational numbers to an irrational value. Here we show that such a quantized response can emerge from the limiting behavior of a finite, experimentally realizable geometric network. We develop a method based on infinite geometric structures, in which a sequence of finite networks rapidly converges toward an infinite limit. Following this approach, we identify a planar network whose normalized resistance converges to the irrational value $\sqrt{3}$, and realize a finite approximation with high-precision geometric control. The measured normalized resistance is $1.735 \pm 0.001\, h/e^2$ for a fully occupied Landau level in the designed pattern. Our experimental results extend the concept of quantization beyond rational values and demonstrate that an irrational quantized response can be approached through a finite physical system.

### Crossed Electric Spin Generation in Multilayers
- **arXiv**: [2610.14668](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14668)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14668.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Crossed Electric Spin Generation in Multilayers
- **Authors**: Xukun Feng, Wei Du, Jin Cao, Qian Niu, Shengyuan A. Yang, Cong Xiao
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 本文提出"交叉电场自旋产生效应"(crossed electric spin generation)，利用面内电场与栅电场的组合在多层/准二维结构中操控自旋，其机制源于准二维体系特有的混合Berry曲率。结合第一性原理计算，在二维铁磁Fe3GeTe2与交错磁V2Te2O中预测了可观的自旋产生。

We propose a crossed electric spin generation effect in multilayer structures. This effect combines the advantages of in-plane and gate electric fields for manipulating spin, and unveils a nice quantum geometric structure in terms of the underlying hybrid Berry curvatures that are unique to quasi-2D systems and manifest inherent spin-layer-orbital coupling. Combining our theory with first-principles calculations, we predict sizable spin generation in 2D ferromagnetic Fe3GeTe2 and altermagnetic V2Te2O. Our theory opens a new Berry-curvature route for all-electric spin manipulation in 2D materials

### Universal Boolean Computation in Negatively Coupled Circle Map Lattices
- **arXiv**: [2610.16290](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16290)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16290.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Universal Boolean Computation in Negatively Coupled Circle Map Lattices
- **Authors**: Priyanka D. Bhoyar, Prashant M. Gade
- **Categories**: cond-mat.stat-mech, nlin.CD, nlin.PS, physics.ins-det
- **TL;DR**: 研究负耦合圆映射一维格点在行波区实现布尔通用计算。

We investigate the computational capabilities of a one-dimensional lattice of negatively
  coupled circle maps in its traveling wave regime. We interpret propagating waves as information carriers and exploit their interactions to implement Boolean logic. The logical states are encoded by the propagation direction of the traveling waves, while the presence or absence of a wave at a designated output location represents the logical output. Using controlled wave collisions, we demonstrate the realization of NAND and NOR gates along with other basic gates. Since NAND and NOR operations are functionally complete, the results establish that the traveling wave regime of the negatively coupled circle-map lattice supports universal Boolean computation in principle. The demonstrated logical operations remain valid over a finite range of the coupling strength εand map parameter ω, indicating robustness of the computational scheme to parameter variations. Our results therefore demonstrate that computational functionality can emerge from the collective nonlinear dynamics of a coupled map lattice, providing a con- nection between the dynamical properties of traveling wave states and collision based information processing.

### Emergent one-dimensional Bloch oscillations in coupled Ising layers
- **arXiv**: [2610.14857](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14857)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14857.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Emergent one-dimensional Bloch oscillations in coupled Ising layers
- **Authors**: E. S. Ma, Z. Song
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 在不对称纵向场耦合的双层Ising模型中，层间Heisenberg耦合在赝自旋表示下充当横向耦合，使梯级赝自旋结构产生动力学约束，把二维多体动力学限制到一维准粒子子空间；两层纵向场之差为涌现的畴壁激发提供有效线性势，形成Wannier–Stark阶梯，从而在无传统倾斜势的情况下产生Bloch振荡。

Bloch oscillations typically arise in periodic systems subjected to a constant force generated by a potential gradient. Here, we study domain-wall dynamics in coupled Ising layers under asymmetric longitudinal fields. The interlayer coupling is of Heisenberg type and acts as an effective transverse coupling in the emergent pseudospin representation. We show that the rung pseudospin structure gives rise to kinetic constraints, restricting the many-body dynamics to an emergent one-dimensional quasiparticle subspace within the original two-dimensional system. Furthermore, the imbalance between the longitudinal fields of the two layers induces an effective linear potential for the emergent domain-wall excitations, leading to their confinement between two oppositely polarized ferromagnetic domains. Remarkably, the resulting Wannier--Stark ladder of domain-wall excitations gives rise to Bloch oscillations in the absence of a conventional tilted potential, revealing a distinct mechanism for coherent quantum dynamics. Numerical simulations of time-dependent observables confirm the analytical predictions.

### Finite-temperature adiabatic-connection reduced density-matrix functional theory: a unified framework for i-DMFT and TAO-DFT
- **arXiv**: [2610.14706](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14706)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14706.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Finite-temperature adiabatic-connection reduced density-matrix functional theory: a unified framework for i-DMFT and TAO-DFT
- **Authors**: Jingang Han, Huanjing Gong, Kai Luo, Hongming Weng, Xinguo Ren
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, physics.chem-ph, physics.comp-ph
- **TL;DR**: 提出有限温度绝热连接约化密度矩阵泛函理论(FT-AC-RDMFT)，将i-DMFT与TAO-DFT统一为同一框架的两种约化，并证明有限温度占据数可推广至杂化和半局域泛函，显著改进强关联体系描述。

Accurately describing chemical bond breaking requires capturing strong/static electron correlation, a major challenge for single-reference self-consistent-field methods such as Hartree--Fock and Kohn--Sham density-functional theory (DFT). Fractional occupations substantially improve this situation, as demonstrated by information-density-matrix-functional theory (i-DMFT) and thermally-assisted-occupation DFT (TAO-DFT). Although useful, they are formulated within rather different theoretical frameworks, are somewhat semi-empirical, and their relationship remains unclear. Here we introduce finite-temperature adiabatic-connection reduced density-matrix functional theory (FT-AC-RDMFT), in which i-DMFT and TAO-DFT emerge as different reductions of a unified framework. The effective eigenvalue equation for natural orbitals and the Fermi--Dirac occupation numbers follow directly from the variational principle, while a thermal adiabatic connection relates the empirical entropy coefficient of i-DMFT to a characteristic temperature of the auxiliary non-interacting system. Within this framework, i-DMFT is the exact-exchange-based reduction, whereas TAO-DFT results when exact exchange is replaced by a density-based exchange-correlation functional. Numerical calculations confirm that i-DMFT, apart from a constant, coincides with the exact-exchange realization of the framework, and show that the improved dissociation behavior from finite-temperature occupations extends beyond exact exchange to hybrid and semilocal density functionals. For strongly correlated V2O3, finite-temperature occupations substantially reduce the severely overestimated non-magnetic LDA bulk modulus, bringing it close to experiment. These results establish FT-AC-RDMFT as a common theoretical foundation for finite-temperature occupation-based electronic-structure methods in molecular and solid-state systems.

### Probing the wave-vector gap in nanoscale magnonics
- **arXiv**: [2610.15945](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15945)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15945.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Probing the wave-vector gap in nanoscale magnonics
- **Authors**: Ondřej Wojewoda, Samer Kurdi, Toeno van der Sar, Daniel Schick, Sebastian Wintz, Alexei Maznev
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 评述短波长自旋波（约30–300 rad/μm中间波矢区间）测量难题，并系统比较五种新兴表征技术。

Short-wavelength spin waves are central to miniaturizing magnonic devices and to studying exchange-dominated magnetization dynamics. Yet the wave-vector range between roughly 30 and 300 radians per micrometre has remained difficult to access experimentally, posing challenges for both excitation and detection. This Perspective reviews recent advances in emerging measurement techniques and discusses prospects for their future development. Optical and microwave techniques are restricted to the vicinity of the Brillouin-zone centre. Inelastic neutron, electron and resonant hard x-ray scattering cover the full Brillouin zone but lack the energy resolution needed for low-frequency magnons at intermediate wave vectors. This Perspective examines five emerging approaches that are beginning to close this gap: Mie-enhanced Brillouin light scattering, colour-centre magnetometry, magnon momentum microscopy, extreme-ultraviolet transient-grating spectroscopy, and time-resolved scanning transmission x-ray microscopy. For each, we discuss the physical principle, demonstrated capabilities, current limitations and prospects for development. Compared side by side, the techniques give complementary access to magnon frequencies, local stray fields, real-space dynamics and momentum-space distributions, and they differ in their sensitivity to coherent and incoherent excitations. We therefore discuss the main advantages and use cases of each technique and consider the practical requirements for addressing different scientific questions. We highlight opportunities for further development, including higher sensitivity, improved spatial and temporal resolution, and broader frequency and wave-vector coverage. Together, these advances could enable, for example, systematic studies of nonlinear magnon interactions at nanoscale wavelengths and investigations of emerging magnetic materials such as altermagnets.

### Inverse Designing Reconfigurable Piezoelectric Tensors via Architectural and Modal Programming
- **arXiv**: [2610.14761](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14761)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14761.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Inverse Designing Reconfigurable Piezoelectric Tensors via Architectural and Modal Programming
- **Authors**: Anish Satpati, Marco Maurizi, Hyunmoon Cho, Victor Couedel, Nicolas Mule, Xiaoyu, Zheng
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该文提出基于图表示（graph representation）的通用框架，通过架构设计（architecture）实现对完整有效压电张量的按需调控，并揭示频率选择性张量重构现象，进而实现体积缩减90%的多自由度压电致动器。

Piezoelectricity arises from the coupling between electric polarization and mechanical deformation, described by a third-rank tensor whose coefficients are constrained by crystal symmetry. In natural crystals, atomic periodicity and energetic stability restrict accessible symmetries, limited to 20 distinct anisotropies that can exist. Here we establish a general framework, based on graph representation, for controlling the full effective piezoelectric tensor through architecture by resolving how macroscopic symmetry, local polarization and deformation of structural elements act together. This enables each coefficient to be traced to the members that reinforce or cancel it and be controlled through targeted geometric changes. We further uncover frequency-selective tensor reconfiguration, in which excitation frequency switches the dominant piezoelectric coefficients of a single architecture. These principles open a broader class of applications in which a single piezoelectric architecture can deliver programmable sensing, actuation and mode conversion across multiple directions and deformation states. We realize a multi-degree-of-freedom actuator that integrates distinct actuation modes in one block and reduces volume by 90% relative to conventional multi-stack assemblies.

### Mutual linearity from classical response theory
- **arXiv**: [2610.16894](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16894)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16894.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Mutual linearity from classical response theory
- **Authors**: Timur Aslyamov, Massimiliano Esposito
- **Categories**: cond-mat.stat-mech
- **TL;DR**: 本文用经典有限响应理论统一了马尔可夫跳跃过程与化学反应网络中的互线性关系：单一速率改变时稳态概率变化共享公共因子，消除该因子即得概率、可观测量与流之间的精确关系，且在远离平衡时成立。

We unify known mutual-linearity relations for Markov jump processes and chemical reaction networks within a common framework based on classical finite-response theory. In a Markov jump process, an exact finite-response identity shows that all stationary probability changes share a common factor when the rates of one edge change. Eliminating this factor gives the relations between probabilities, observables, and currents, even far from equilibrium. We also explain how the same argument applies when one coefficient of a linear system changes and recovers the known relations in Laplace space and between stationary complex activities in chemical reaction networks.

### Spectral Mapping Theorem between SU(N) and SU(Ns/N) Heisenberg Models and Exact Diagonalization of SU(4) and SU(7) Long-Range Ordered Phases on the Triangular Lattice
- **arXiv**: [2610.15162](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15162)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15162.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Spectral Mapping Theorem between SU(N) and SU(Ns/N) Heisenberg Models and Exact Diagonalization of SU(4) and SU(7) Long-Range Ordered Phases on the Triangular Lattice
- **Authors**: Samuel Gozel, Pierre Nataf
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 本文证明SU(N)海森堡模型与SU(Ns/N)模型在单态扇区的谱互为负值，并基于标准杨表(SYT)提出高效省内存的精确对角化实现，将三角格子上SU(4)和SU(7)模型研究扩展至Ns=28。

We first show that, for the SU(N) Heisenberg model with isotropic two-site interactions on an arbitrary lattice of Ns sites, the full spectrum in its singlet sector (when N divides Ns) is the negative of the full spectrum in its singlet sector of the SU(Ns/N) Heisenberg model defined on the same lattice. This theorem, valid for models with the fundamental irreducible representation (irrep) at each lattice site, follows naturally from a transposition operation in the basis of Standard Young Tableaux (SYTs). Second, this basis -- previously recognized as particularly convenient for exact diagonalization (ED) of SU(N) Heisenberg models -- admits an efficient, memory-saving implementation, thereby enabling large-scale diagonalizations for unprecedented system sizes Ns and numbers of colors N&gt;3. We apply these ideas to an ED study of the SU(4) and SU(7) Heisenberg models on the triangular lattice (TL), extending the calculations up to Ns=28 sites. In particular, starting from the SU(4) case, whose nearest-neighbor ground state remains debated, we confirm that next-nearest-neighbor couplings stabilize four-sublattice long-range order. We further find that increasing the number of colors from N=4 to N=7 provides another route toward a Néel-like ground state, even without such additional couplings. N = 4 to N = 7 provides another route toward a Néel-like ground state, even without such additional couplings.

### Kink boundary-induced polarity revealed by nonreciprocal charge transport in FeTe0.7Se0.3 superconductor
- **arXiv**: [2610.14728](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14728)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14728.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Kink boundary-induced polarity revealed by nonreciprocal charge transport in FeTe0.7Se0.3 superconductor
- **Authors**: Shengyao Li, Ya Deng, Han Yan, Dianyi Hu, Zherui Yang, Xueyan Wang, Ming Yue, Qiong Wu, Zheng Liu, X. Renshaw Wang
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con
- **TL;DR**: 在中心对称超导体FeTe0.7Se0.3中，通过生长过程中扭结带（kink band）边界诱导的应变工程打破反演对称性，实现了显著的非互易电荷输运。

Nonreciprocal charge transport, characterized by direction-dependent electrical response, provides a powerful probe of electronic structure and a platform for rectification. However, its realization is typically governed by intrinsic crystal symmetry, restricting the range of suitable materials. Here, we demonstrate nonreciprocal charge transport in an otherwise centrosymmetric superconductor, FeTe0.7Se0.3 (FTS), through kink boundary-induced strain engineering. The stress relaxation during growth generates a cusp-like kink-band boundary with asymmetric curvature, producing a spatially nonuniform strain field and an effective in-plane polarity that breaks inversion symmetry. Pronounced nonreciprocal transport is observed under out-of-plane magnetic fields, with strong dependence on current and temperature across the superconducting transition, in contrast to the negligible response in flat nanosheets. Our results identify kink boundary-induced symmetry breaking as a general route to engineer nonreciprocal charge transport in centrosymmetric systems.

### Driven Vortex Motion in Periodic Channel Potential Embedded in Superconducting Films
- **arXiv**: [2610.14775](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14775)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14775.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Driven Vortex Motion in Periodic Channel Potential Embedded in Superconducting Films
- **Authors**: A. Al Luhaibi, A. Glatz, Michael Vogl, Hocine Bahlouli, J. B. Ketterson
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con
- **TL;DR**: 通过数值求解TDGL方程研究周期宽度调制超导通道中的涡旋动力学，发现几何调制可有效控制电场谐波含量，特定通道轮廓能抑制高次谐波、逼近纯正弦波形，为超导器件中低谐波微波信号产生及片上低温波形发生器提供途径。

In this paper, we investigate vortex dynamics in periodically width-modulated superconducting channels by numerically solving the time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau (TDGL) equations. The channels are designed with sinusoidally varying widths characterized by their average width and modulation amplitude. The motion of driven vortices generates an oscillatory electric field with a fundamental frequency, $f_0$, set by the channel periodicity, together with higher-order harmonics. In contrast to the situation of vortex lattice dynamics in a periodic pinning landscape of circular holes considered by Al Luhaibi et al. [1], we show that the harmonic content of the generated electric field can be effectively controlled through the channel geometry. In particular, appropriately engineered channel profiles strongly suppress higher-order harmonics, yielding an electric-field response that closely approaches a pure sinusoidal waveform. These results demonstrate that geometric modulation offers a viable route to engineering low-harmonicity microwave signals in superconducting devices and may enable cryogenic on-chip waveform generators for controlling superconducting qubits. Such devices could provide a useful building block for scalable transmon-based quantum computing architectures.
