### High-mobility spin transport mediated by indirect excitons in a van der Waals heterostructure
- **arXiv**: [2610.16089](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16089)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16089.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: High-mobility spin transport mediated by indirect excitons in a van der Waals heterostructure
- **Authors**: Zhiwen Zhou, W. J. Brunner, Luc M. Nguyen, C. A. Lozano, G. Mercado, L. H. Fowler-Gerace, L. V. Butov
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 在TMD范德华异质结中，利用间接激子(IXs)介导自旋输运，抑制粒子散射，实现高达10^5 cm^2/(eV·s)的自旋迁移率，比标准扩散自旋输运高数个量级。

Efficient spin transport with high spin mobility is of both fundamental and practical significance for spintronics. Spin can be transferred by moving particles. However, particle scattering limits spin transfer and prevents high spin mobilities. In this work, we study the kinetics of spin transport mediated by a quantum system of indirect excitons (IXs) in a van der Waals heterostructure composed of atomically thin transition-metal dichalcogenides (TMDs). This spin transfer mechanism allows for the realization of efficient spin transport characterized by a high spin mobility reaching 10$^5$ cm$^2$/(eV$\cdot$s), which is orders of magnitude higher than that achieved via standard diffusive spin transport in TMD materials. The suppression of IX scattering enables high-mobility spin transport.

### Selective faceting induced by Hexagonal Boron Nitride on curved Platinum substrate
- **arXiv**: [2610.14825](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14825)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14825.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Selective faceting induced by Hexagonal Boron Nitride on curved Platinum substrate
- **Authors**: Alaa Mohammed Idris Bakhit, Muhammad Saad Mahmood, Khadiza Ali, Anna A. Makarova, Igor Piš, Federica Bondino, Frederik Schiller, Maria Peressi
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该工作结合实验与第一性原理计算，研究六方氮化硼(hBN)在曲面Pt(331)衬底上的生长，揭示了hBN诱导的选择性刻面化及不同Pt晶面上hBN-衬底相互作用的差异。

This work combines experimental investigations and first-principles calculations to study hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) growth on a curved Pt(331) substrate. Low-energy electron diffraction (LEED) and scanning tunnelling microscopy (STM) were employed to investigate the structural properties, observing that hBN growth led to faceting with several stable facets: Pt(111), Pt(221), Pt(441), Pt(881), and Pt(110). Additionally, the electronic properties were examined by X-ray photoemission spectroscopy (XPS) and near-edge X-ray absorption fine structure (NEXAFS), revealing an increase in the hBN-substrate interaction strength, from the weakest at the Pt(111) to the strongest at the Pt(110). Density functional theory (DFT) calculations confirm that the selective faceting mechanism is induced by hBN, which changes the order of relative stability of the uncovered facets. Although a complete understanding of the process is not achievable using only thermodynamical arguments, the comparison between hBN adhesion energies and clean surface free energies clearly indicates that some facets are stabilized with respect to others by stronger overlayer-substrate interaction, whereas for others the clean surface energetics plays the dominant role.

### Proof of the exact correlation-length exponent $ν= \frac{\sqrt[3]{2}}{2}$ and algebraic relations for $β$ and $γ$ in the 3D Ising model
- **arXiv**: [2610.17797](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17797)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17797.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Proof of the exact correlation-length exponent $ν= \frac{\sqrt[3]{2}}{2}$ and algebraic relations for $β$ and $γ$ in the 3D Ising model
- **Authors**: Aleksandr Liivand
- **Categories**: cond-mat.stat-mech
- **TL;DR**: 本文声称利用群论方法与构造几何命题，证明了3D Ising模型关联长度临界指数ν的精确值为∛2/2，并据此得到γ和β的精确代数表达式，与现有最精确数值估计高度吻合。

Based on our previous results regarding critical exponent estimates for the 3D Ising model and scaling relations [1], we have proven that the exact value of the correlation-length exponent is $\frac{\sqrt[3]{2}}{2}$. This is in very close agreement with the most precise numerical estimates currently available. Using a group theoretical approach and some propositions of constructive geometry, we derived an exact construction of algebraic expressions for the critical exponents $γ$ and $β$. We proved that these expressions depend solely on the exact value of $ν\,$.

### Superlattice-Stabilized Fractional Chern Insulators in a "Berry Basin" Model
- **arXiv**: [2610.15197](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15197)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15197.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Superlattice-Stabilized Fractional Chern Insulators in a "Berry Basin" Model
- **Authors**: Yongxin Zeng, Andrew J. Millis
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 提出"Berry basin"模型，用含色散平底能带、层自由度动量空间缠绕与周期性势的相互作用体系，研究菱面体石墨烯中整数量子与分数量子陈绝缘体的形成条件，发现分数态在所有可达相互作用强度下都需要非零超晶格势。

The discovery of integer and fractional Chern insulators in rhombohedral graphene, following their discovery in the isolated topological flat band in twisted MoTe$_2$, motivates a search for the conditions under which an electronic band can host a topological correlated state. In this Letter we introduce a ``Berry basin" model which captures the essential features of rhombohedral graphene: a dispersive band with a flat bottom, momentum-space winding of a ``layer" degree of freedom, and Coulomb interactions, while also including periodic potentials, enabling controlled studies of the interplay of quantum geometry, superlattice modulation, and electron-electron interactions in producing correlated topological states beyond the isolated topological band paradigm. While integer Chern insulators emerge at strong coupling without a superlattice potential, stabilizing the fractional state requires nonzero potential for all interaction strengths we access. We provide insight into the greater robustness of integer and fractional Chern insulators in rhombohedral graphene when electrons are polarized away from the moiré interface. Our results establish a new class of systems that host robust fractional Chern insulators and provide a controlled numerical platform to test competing theories of interacting topological phases.

### Local Suppression of Superconductivity in Niobium Films by Focused Silicon Ion Implantation
- **arXiv**: [2610.15200](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15200)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15200.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Local Suppression of Superconductivity in Niobium Films by Focused Silicon Ion Implantation
- **Authors**: Rochelle Y. Qu, Shunsuke Nishimura, Max Proepper, Joseph J. Barrera, Alexander Brooks, Joshua Vande Berg, Achim Nadzeyka, Aravind R. Sreeja, Jay C. LeFebvre, Roger K. Lake, Shane A. Cybart
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con
- **TL;DR**: 利用聚焦离子束直接写入硅离子注入，在Nb薄膜中局部抑制超导性以形成"护城河"（moat），无需刻蚀即可阻挡磁通涡旋进入逻辑区域。

Magnetic flux trapped in a superconducting circuit shifts the critical currents of nearby Josephson junctions and disturbs the operation of the circuit. The usual remedy is a moat etched through the ground plane to hold the flux away from the logic. We report direct write silicon ion implantation with a focused ion beam as a way to make moats without etching the film, by locally suppressing superconductivity in niobium (Nb). Single implanted lines were written across Nb electrodes in films of three thicknesses over two orders of magnitude in ion fluence, and the transition temperature, resistance, and critical current density of each line were measured. The transition temperature of the line falls with fluence while the surrounding Nb is unchanged, and the critical current density falls by about two orders of magnitude before the line becomes resistive at 4.2 K. The product of the critical current and the resistance of the lines follows a single power law in reduced temperature. This gives an estimate of the critical current of a line at any temperature from its transition temperature and resistance. A time dependent Ginzburg-Landau simulation indicates that an implanted region can capture and hold a vortex. From these results we suggest a starting ion energy and fluence for implanted moats in a 100 nm Nb ground plane at 4.2 K and estimate the equivalent broad beam fluence.

### Pressure-transferable universal machine-learning potentials: the WBM-MatPES benchmark and Tuga-FF
- **arXiv**: [2610.14891](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14891)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14891.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Pressure-transferable universal machine-learning potentials: the WBM-MatPES benchmark and Tuga-FF
- **Authors**: Pierre-Paul De Breuck, Thalis H. B. da Silva, Antoine Loew, Silvana Botti, Miguel A. L. Marques
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 发布WBM-MatPES数据集（18.2万结构、两条凸包、六种压力测试集至150 GPa）用于系统评测通用机器学习势，并提出Tuga-FF模型，在常压和高压下均取得最优精度。

Universal machine-learning interatomic potentials hold the promise to replace costly ab initio calculations in materials science workflows. However, the accuracy and transferability of current models are limited by the availability of training data. We release WBM-MatPES, a dataset of 182 thousand structures recomputed with consistent MatPES settings, together with two reference convex hulls of different depths and test sets at six pressures up to 150 GPa. These datasets reproduce a realistic discovery workflow and allow potentials to be ranked systematically at this level of theory, similar to the Matbench Discovery leaderboard at the Materials Project settings. In this benchmark, five published MatPES potentials agree within 12 meV/atom at 25 GPa, yet their errors increase dramatically to 0.4-5 eV/atom at 150 GPa. To address this limitation, we develop Tuga-FF, a graph transformer trained on datasets spanning ambient and high-pressure configurations alike. Tuga-FF is the most accurate model in the benchmark at ambient pressure, with an energy error of 13.6 meV/atom, and maintains an error of just 21 meV/atom at 150 GPa. We believe this benchmark will encourage the community to train higher-accuracy models and generate richer datasets, advancing machine-learned interatomic potentials as a whole.

### Chirally pinned flat bands, helical minibands, and bias-controlled topology of Jackiw-Rebbi states in a periodically modulated Dirac mass texture
- **arXiv**: [2610.16183](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16183)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16183.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Chirally pinned flat bands, helical minibands, and bias-controlled topology of Jackiw-Rebbi states in a periodically modulated Dirac mass texture
- **Authors**: Tao Zhou
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cond-mat.quant-gas
- **TL;DR**: 提出一种仅含慢调制狄拉克质量纹理的方格点阵模型，经精确嵌套在每个横向动量ky下化为1+1维变质量狄拉克哈密顿量，从而在一个可精确求解且易于实验实现的平台上统一平带与拓扑束缚态。

Flat bands amplify interactions into correlated phases, and topological bound states are robust by principle; combining the two in a model that is both exactly analyzable and experimentally accessible is therefore especially desirable. Here we show that a square-lattice model with a slowly modulated Dirac mass texture, $m(x)=m+m_0\cos(2πx/L)$ with $L$ up to hundreds of sites, provides such a setting, without engineered geometries, lattice-scale textures, or long-range hoppings. Exact nesting reduces the problem, independently at each transverse momentum $k_y$, to a $1+1$ dimensional Dirac Hamiltonian with a position-dependent mass: every simple zero of $m(x)$ binds Jackiw-Rebbi zero modes, and the two walls of a period form an effective Su-Schrieffer-Heeger ring. The model interpolates between a chirally pinned flat band at large $L$, a helical miniband whose nodes are enforced by a boundary phase of the ring, and a bias-dimerized regime in which the sign of the bias sets a winding number. A central result is that the miniband bandwidth is not a single scale: a max-min measurement conflates three widths of different origin, the helical height $W_{\rm JR}$ exponential in the period, the zone-edge upturn $W_{\rm edge}\propto L^{-2/3}$ dominating at large $L$, and a bias bump exponential in $L$, so the flat band is robust against bias in the thermodynamic limit. We analyze two lattice realizations, a two-orbital model with modulated inter-orbital hybridization and a single-orbital bond-order texture sharing the nodal physics while leaving the van Hove points gapless, and propose experiments on cold-atom superlattices, Kekule-ordered graphene, STM designer lattices, and microwave arrays.

### Spiral Edge States in Topological 2D Sb2Te3 Crystals
- **arXiv**: [2610.14905](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14905)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14905.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Spiral Edge States in Topological 2D Sb2Te3 Crystals
- **Authors**: Timothy Carlson, Daniel Lazega, Scott McGuire, Mihirangi Medahinne, Motahhare Mirhosseini, Gabriel Marcus, ChaoChao Dun, Maria Kolesnik-Gray, Vojislav Krstic, David Carroll
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, quant-ph
- **TL;DR**: 研究孪晶与螺旋状二维Sb2Te3拓扑绝缘体晶体的边缘态导电性，发现两条螺旋与打结的边缘导电通道经拓扑缺陷连通，并在0.155 eV处观察到与带内两个孤立态相关的局域激发。

This work explores the edge state conduction properties of twinned and spiraled, 2D Sb2Te3 topological insulator crystals. We show the structure supports spatially continuous edge states within two spiral and knotted edge conduction channels. The channels are connected through a topological defect. This geometry introduces boundary conditions into the 2D crystal that modifies the edge state dispersion. Using a combination of low loss Electron Energy Loss Spectroscopy, Magnetic Force Microscopy and traditional carrier transport measurements we identify a distinct excitation at 0.155 eV in the spiral that seems to be related to two isolated states within the gap. This state is localized to the crystal edges. The modification of edge state bands using manipulation of a systems quantum geometry may open new avenues for the use of 2D topological systems.

### Organisation and aggregation of calcium carbonate ions in water via molecular simulation
- **arXiv**: [2610.18128](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18128)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18128.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Organisation and aggregation of calcium carbonate ions in water via molecular simulation
- **Authors**: Elizaveta Sidler, Rita S Dias, Raffaela Cabriolu
- **Categories**: cond-mat.stat-mech, physics.chem-ph
- **TL;DR**: 本文以两种互补模拟方法（OPES与∞RETIS）研究CaCO₃首个配位球的形成与离子聚集的关系，发现高/低过饱和下存在不同动力学行为，且配位球通过两条不同路径在预形成团簇中生成。

Crystallisation of calcium carbonate (CaCO$_3$) in aqueous solutions is essential for carbon dioxide sequestration and the sustainable production of calcium carbonate materials, which are widely used in industry and increasingly explored for novel applications. While crystallisation is often analysed within the framework of classical nucleation theory, CaCO$_3$ and other ionic crystals often form through amorphous precursor phases, motivating the need for alternative descriptions. In this work, we investigate the relationship between the formation of the first CaCO$_3$ coordination sphere, a fundamental unit of local ordering, and ionic aggregation using two complementary simulation approaches: on-the-fly probability-enhanced sampling (OPES) and asynchronous and infinite-swap replica exchange transition interface sampling ($\infty$RETIS). OPES was used to explore free-energy landscapes, whereas $\infty$RETIS was used to determine reaction kinetics. We studied systems at two levels of supersaturation, between which the formation of the first coordination sphere exhibited distinct kinetic behaviour. These differences were found to be linked to concentration-dependent ionic aggregation. At lower concentration, aggregation remains limited, whereas higher concentration promotes extensive spinodal-like aggregation. For both concentrations, the first coordination sphere forms within the pre-formed clusters through one of two distinct pathways: either within a cluster close to the minimum size required for its formation, or within larger amorphous aggregates whose size and structure depend on concentration. The coexistence of these pathways at both levels of supersaturation reveals the multifaceted nature of early-stage CaCO$_3$ crystallisation and highlights the importance of differentiating ionic aggregation and organisation.

### Dispersion of low-energy optical excitations in NiPS$_3$ explained by singlet polaron theory
- **arXiv**: [2610.15230](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15230)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15230.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Dispersion of low-energy optical excitations in NiPS$_3$ explained by singlet polaron theory
- **Authors**: I. J. Hamad, C. S. Helman, A. E. Feiguin, L. O. Manuel, A. A. Aligia
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 本文用单态极化子理论（SPT）计算NiPS3中~1.5 eV激子的动量依赖，所得最低能准粒子色散与RIXS测量的激子色散高度吻合（形状与带宽），将光学激发解释为可色散的单态极化子。

Optical and resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS) experiments in the van der Waals antiferromagnet NiPS$_3$ have revealed narrow excitonic resonances near 1.5 eV whose microscopic origin remains under debate. In this work we study the momentum dependence of these excitations using the singlet-polaron theory (SPT) previously developed for NiPS$_3$. Within its framework, we calculate the spectral function of the corresponding effective model and extract the dispersion of the resulting quasiparticles. The theory naturally predicts two singlet excitations whose dynamics arise from their propagation in the zig-zag antiferromagnetic background ($S=1$) coupled to two-magnon processes. We show that the dispersion of the lowest-energy quasiparticle agrees very well with the excitonic dispersion measured by RIXS, reproducing both its overall shape and bandwidth along high-symmetry directions of the Brillouin zone. Our results provide a consistent interpretation of the optical excitations in terms of dispersive singlet polarons, and suggest that similar mechanisms may govern excitonic dynamics in other layered van der Waals antiferromagnets.

### Spatial interference changes the symmetry class of a Josephson element
- **arXiv**: [2610.15746](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15746)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15746.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Spatial interference changes the symmetry class of a Josephson element
- **Authors**: Takayuki Kubo
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con, quant-ph
- **TL;DR**: 空间干涉可在横向均匀、局域能量反演对称的结中破坏有效约瑟夫森势的反演对称性；多谐波相位不相容导致立方相互作用与三波混频，相位反射可翻转奇阶非线性而不改变量子谱，并可由磁场分量反转实现。

Spatial interference can break the inversion symmetry of an effective Josephson potential even in a laterally homogeneous junction with inversion-symmetric local energy. We derive a general criterion based on harmonic-dependent complex spatial form factors: a single local harmonic remains a shifted cosine, whereas multiple harmonics can acquire phases incompatible with any common inversion center, permitting cubic interactions and three-wave mixing. Phase reflection reverses all odd-order expansion coefficients about corresponding minima while preserving the even-order ones. For a capacitively shunted junction at fixed charging energy and arbitrary offset charge, it also preserves the complete quantum spectrum, enabling reversal of the odd nonlinear sector without spectral retuning. A centered cross junction with multiple local harmonics is one concrete example. Within the imposed-field model, a three-component magnetic field can generate an inversion-asymmetric effective potential, and reversing an odd number of field components exactly reflects it without changing the quantum spectrum of the capacitively shunted junction.

### UGME: A python package for non-Markovian protein folding dynamics
- **arXiv**: [2610.14234](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14234)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14234.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: UGME: A python package for non-Markovian protein folding dynamics
- **Authors**: Anthony J. Dominic, Bertram Cham, Andrés Montoya-Castillo
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph
- **TL;DR**: ugme是一个模块化开源Python包，用于构建和验证广义主方程（GME）模型，以显式刻画分子动力学降维投影中出现的非马尔可夫（记忆）效应。

Biomolecular processes, including protein folding, protein-protein association, and protein-DNA recognition, often occur on timescales beyond what individual atomistic molecular dynamics (MD) simulations can reach. To access these longer-timescale dynamics, practitioners commonly combine many parallelized, short trajectories into reduced kinetic models, such as Markov state models (MSMs), that describe how the biomolecules interconvert between a handful of biomolecular configurations (e.g., folded and unfolded states of a protein). Central to MSM construction is the assumption that, at sufficiently long time lags, these projected dynamics become memoryless, such that the future evolution of the system depends only on its current state. Yet, projecting high-dimensional biomolecular dynamics onto a small set of states necessarily discards information about unresolved degrees of freedom, including solvent reorganization, side-chain rearrangements, and intermediate configurations. Because these unresolved motions can continue to influence subsequent dynamics, the resulting dynamics retain memory that is absent from a Markovian description. Here, we present ugme, a modular, open-source Python package for constructing and validating generalized master equations (GME) models that explicitly account for these non-Markovian (memory) effects.

### Pressure-Driven Evolution of the Physical Properties of Layered Y2GaB and Y2InB MAB Phases: A Comparative First-Principles Study
- **arXiv**: [2610.14908](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14908)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14908.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Pressure-Driven Evolution of the Physical Properties of Layered Y2GaB and Y2InB MAB Phases: A Comparative First-Principles Study
- **Authors**: M. Rabi Us Sani Shapon, Md. Asif Afzal, Jubair Hossan Abir, Raihana Shams Islam, Saleh Hasan Naqib
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 本文用第一性原理计算比较了层状MAB相硼化物Y2GaB与Y2InB在0–20 GPa静水压下的结构与性能响应，确认Y2GaB在0–10 GPa力学稳定、Y2InB仅常压稳定，并揭示压力可调控弹性、热输运与光学各向异性。

MAB phases are nanolaminated ternary borides in which strong in-plane M-B bonds and weaker interlayer interactions drive their distinctive stability and functionality. Hydrostatic pressure offers a direct route to tune the in-plane and interlayer interactions that govern the stability and functionality of layered Y2GaB and Y2InB borides. Here, first-principles calculations are used to compare their structural, elastic, thermophysical, electronic, bonding, optical, and vibrational responses from 0 to 20 GPa. Elastic criteria show that Y2GaB is stable at 0, 5, and 10 GPa, whereas Y2InB is stable only at ambient pressure. Within the stable Y2GaB range, compression increases the elastic moduli, acoustic velocities, Debye temperature, and lattice thermal conductivity while reducing the elastic anisotropy. Both compounds are multiband metals and spin-orbit coupling modifies local dispersions without opening a gap. Y2GaB retains nine Fermi-surface sheets from 0 to 10 GPa, whereas Y2InB has eight at ambient pressure, with no pressure-induced topological reconstruction. Charge-density and overlap analyses indicate mixed ionic-covalent Y-B bonding within a delocalized metallic background. Optical parameters are anisotropic. Unit-cell phonon spectra contain positive frequencies at 0 and 10 GPa, although expanded-supercell calculations reveal low-frequency sensitivity. These results establish mechanically viable pressure windows and clarify substitution-driven structure-property relationships for these borides.

### Wilson-loop formulations of the spin polarization and the spin Chern number
- **arXiv**: [2610.16246](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16246)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16246.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Wilson-loop formulations of the spin polarization and the spin Chern number
- **Authors**: Kiminori Hattori, Ryuki Uchida
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 本文基于Wilson loop从动量空间和实空间推导自旋极化与自旋Chern数，并证明其与Z2指标等价。

In this paper, we derive the spin polarization and the spin Chern number in momentum and real spaces in terms of the Wilson loop in each spin sector. For these derivations, the spin matrix in the subspace of occupied or unoccupied states is generalized by the unitary rotation to prevent the spectral gap from closing. In the presence of a finite spin gap, the spin eigenvectors recompose the eigenstates of the Hamiltonian in two spin sectors. A dimensional reduction leads to the charge polarization in each spin sector in terms of the Wilson loop along the effective Brillouin zone (BZ) in one dimension (1D). A linear combination of the charge polarizations defines the spin polarization, which naturally quantifies the spin Chern number in momentum space. It is also shown that the spin Chern number is directly derived from the local Wilson loops on a discrete 2D BZ. The theoretical treatment is further generalized for real space in a supercell framework, which can deal with ordered and disordered systems in a unified manner. In this framework, the spin Chern number in real space is formulated in terms of the Wilson loop around the BZ boundary. These formulations are used to numerically evaluate topological phases in the Bernevig-Hughes-Zhang model. Numerical results show an equivalence of the spin Chern number and the ${\mathbb{Z}_2}$ index.

### Investigation on Thermal Stability of Epi-GeSn Directly on Si (001)
- **arXiv**: [2610.14947](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14947)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14947.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Investigation on Thermal Stability of Epi-GeSn Directly on Si (001)
- **Authors**: Tharundev V, Dushyant Singh, Vigneshswari SR, Subha Maity, Prince Rathor, Krista R Khiangte
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 研究直接在Si(001)上外延的亚稳态Ge1-xSnx(~4.5% Sn)薄层的结构与热稳定性：70nm薄膜热稳定，140nm薄膜在加热至813K时显著退化，热稳定性取决于生长时形成的初始缺陷结构。

In this paper we investigate the structural properties and thermal stability of metastable Ge1-xSnx epilayers (~4.5 at.% Sn) grown directly on Si(001) substrates by high-resolution X-ray diffraction (HRXRD) and Raman spectroscopy. Our results demonstrate that both 70 and 140 nm films are fully relaxed, with the thicker film exhibiting a small tensile strain (+0.09%). The thermal stability of the heterostructures was evaluated up to 813 K using temperature-dependent Raman spectroscopy and scanning electron microscopy. The surface-weighted Raman response (532 nm excitation) and surface morphology of the 70 nm film remain highly stable. In contrast, the 140 nm film undergoes severe degradation, characterized by significant attenuation and broadening of the Ge-Ge longitudinal optical phonon mode, alongside enhanced surface Sn segregation. These findings demonstrate that the thermal stability of direct GeSn/Si heterostructures is governed by the initial defect structure established during epitaxial growth, providing key design rules for understanding the fabrication of GeSn-based optoelectronic devices.

### Molecular-Dynamics Verification of Effective Gravity under Heat Conduction
- **arXiv**: [2610.18250](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18250)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18250.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Molecular-Dynamics Verification of Effective Gravity under Heat Conduction
- **Authors**: Satoshi Uruno, Naoko Nakagawa
- **Categories**: cond-mat.stat-mech
- **TL;DR**: 通过二维Lennard-Jones流体的分子动力学模拟，验证了全局热力学对有效重力的预测：在弱热传导下液-气分层翻转发生在零有效重力处，且与平衡态饱和蒸气压差预测一致。

We test the effective-gravity prediction of global thermodynamics using standard molecular dynamics of a two-dimensional Lennard-Jones fluid. Under weak heat conduction with the lower boundary hotter than the upper boundary, the transition between liquid below gas and liquid above gas shifts from zero to finite downward gravity. Using independently measured equilibrium saturation pressures, we calculate the shift predicted by global thermodynamics and find agreement with the observed transition shift. The transition occurs at zero effective gravity, where the hydrostatic pressure difference balances the saturation-pressure difference between the two boundaries. The hysteresis response under heat conduction also agrees with the equilibrium response when plotted against effective gravity. These results establish the predictive power of global thermodynamics for nonequilibrium structure formation.

### Matrix-product-state GPLHR for non-Hermitian many-body eigenproblems
- **arXiv**: [2610.15387](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15387)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15387.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Matrix-product-state GPLHR for non-Hermitian many-body eigenproblems
- **Authors**: Xingyao Wu, Zhangyu Yang, Yanbin Chen, Yu Wang, Feng Chen
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 将 GPLHR 方法嵌入 MPS 框架，高效计算非厄米多体算符在复平面上给定偏移附近的多个右本征态。

Computing multiple eigenstates of non-Hermitian many-body operators plays an important role in investigating excitation spectra, spectral properties, and relaxation dynamics in interacting and open quantum systems. However, this task is challenging because the eigenvectors are generally nonorthogonal and the Hilbert space dimension grows exponentially with system size. Here, we formulate the generalized preconditioned locally harmonic residual (GPLHR) method in a matrix product state (MPS) framework to efficiently compute multiple targeted right eigenstates whose eigenvalues lie near a specified shift in the complex plane. Successive randomized compression (SRC) is employed to accelerate operator applications. We test the method on transcorrelated and complex Hubbard chains, an interacting non-Hermitian Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chain, and a Liouvillian model. The results demonstrate that GPLHR combined with MPS provides an efficient and accurate approach for computing multiple eigenstates of non-Hermitian many-body operators.

### Pressure Evolution of the Superconducting Order Parameter of Kagome Superconductor CsV3Sb5
- **arXiv**: [2610.16253](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16253)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16253.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Pressure Evolution of the Superconducting Order Parameter of Kagome Superconductor CsV3Sb5
- **Authors**: Haiyan Zuo, Wangju Yang, Zhifan Wu, Yuqing Zhao, Qiuxia Chen, Weiming Lao, Yangpeng Qi, Hechang Lei, Haizi Cong, Cong Ren
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con
- **TL;DR**: 通过软点接触谱(SDPCS)结合电阻测量，研究CsV3Sb5在静水压下的超导能隙结构，发现c轴两个s波能隙、ab面强各向异性能隙，且2Δavg/kBTc在P1-P2压力窗口增强，指示CDW与SC的复杂纠缠。

Kagome superconductors AV3Sb5 (A = K, Rb, Cs) offer an outstanding platform for exploring the interplay among charge-density-wave (CDW) order, superconductivity (SC), and nontrivial topological electronic states. For CsV3Sb5, moderate hydrostatic pressure induces a double-dome superconducting phase diagram while monotonically suppressing the CDW phase, which signals an unconventional coupling between SC and CDW. Nevertheless, the microscopic nature of the pressure-modulated superconducting state and its relationship with CDW order remain poorly understood. Here we employ soft directional point-contact spectroscopy (SDPCS), combined with superconducting resistive measurements, to probe the superconducting order parameter of CsV3Sb5 under hydrostatic compression. Our anisotropic PCAR measurements resolve two distinct s-wave superconducting gaps along the c-axis and a strongly anisotropic gap within the ab plane. Two-band Blonder-Tinkham-Klapwijk (BTK) fits to the c-axis spectra yield a large gap satisfying 2Delta1/kBTc &gt; 3.52 and a small gap with 2Deltas/kBTc &lt; 3.52. The spectral-weight-averaged coupling ratio 2Deltaavg/kBTc is enhanced in the pressure window between P1 and P2, suggesting that pressure-induced modifications of the low-energy electronic structure may contribute to the evolution of superconductivity. For ab-plane junctions, the superconducting gap anisotropy is gradually suppressed above P1, accompanied by the emergence of a pronounced peak-dip-hump structure suggestive of enhanced coupling between superconducting quasiparticles and a bosonic excitation. These results shed new light on the intricate entanglement between CDW and SC in kagome superconductivity.

### Learning Electron Density with Virtual Atoms for Transferable Atomistic Representations
- **arXiv**: [2610.14521](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14521)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14521.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Learning Electron Density with Virtual Atoms for Transferable Atomistic Representations
- **Authors**: Yuzhi Liu, Xinzijian Liu, Chengqian Zhang, Anyang Peng, Duo Zhang, Han Wang
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph
- **TL;DR**: 在DPA3框架中引入"虚拟原子"表示电子密度的查询点，使其与真实原子共享同一编码器，从而统一实现密度、能量与力的多任务预测，并证明电子密度可作为势函数表示的有效监督信号。

Electron density is a fundamental quantity of electronic structure, yet most machine learning density models treat it solely as a reconstruction target and are developed separately from machine learning interatomic potentials. Here, we introduce a virtual-atom formulation within the DPA3 framework, in which density query points are represented as virtual nodes and processed by the same parameterized encoder used for real atoms. An asymmetric neighbor construction allows virtual atoms to receive information from nearby real atoms without altering the real-atom interaction graph. The resulting framework supports density, energy, and force prediction through task-specific readout heads. The proposed model achieves state-of-the-art density reconstruction accuracy on the Materials Project benchmark, matches the best reported performance on nickel-manganese-cobalt battery cathode materials, and is competitive with dedicated density models on QM9. When density labels are used jointly with energy and force supervision, the model improves in-domain accuracy, makes local environment information more accessible under linear probing, and reduces zero-shot force errors on larger QM40 molecules. Density-only checkpoints also provide useful initializations for downstream energy-force learning under the fixed fine-tuning protocol. Together, these results support electron density as a source of electronic-structure supervision for interatomic-potential representations.

### EpNet-Si: Atomic Form Factors Learned from Band Structures
- **arXiv**: [2610.14992](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14992)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14992.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: EpNet-Si: Atomic Form Factors Learned from Band Structures
- **Authors**: Qiang Zhu
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 提出EpNet-Si，用等变网络从局部环境预测原子平面波形因子，把经典经验赝势法扩展为可跨结构迁移的快速能带计算方法。

The empirical pseudopotential method (EPM) computes band structures with one diagonalization per $k$ point and no self-consistency, but its fixed atomic form factors do not transfer between crystal structures. To address this limitation, we introduce EpNet-Si, an EPM for silicon in which an equivariant network predicts each atom's plane-wave form factor from its local environment: a species potential plus monopole, dipole and quadrupole corrections, mixed by a learned router over atomic densities, together with an $s/p$ nonlocal projector. With 6{,}195 parameters, the model is trained through the eigensolver on reference density functional theory (DFT) band energies of only 77 cells. Among 231 distortions of the trained structure types, the 142 gapped cells have mean band and gap errors of 15 and 8\,meV; among 192 unseen structures, the 165 gapped cells have mean errors of 38 and 49\,meV. Physical properties derived from EpNet-Si, including effective masses, gap pressure coefficients, deformation potentials and the density of states at the Fermi level, agree with those obtained from the reference DFT calculations. These results suggest that the classical EPM, fitted to a small set of reference data, can be extended into a fast alternative for modern electronic band-structure calculations.

### Symmetry Condition and Mechanism for the Anomalous Hall Effect in Odd-Parity Magnets
- **arXiv**: [2610.16247](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16247)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16247.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Symmetry Condition and Mechanism for the Anomalous Hall Effect in Odd-Parity Magnets
- **Authors**: Xun-Jiang Luo, Su-Qi Zhang, Yi-Dong Liu, Yong-Ting Shi, Ding-Fu Shao, Mingliang Tian
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 该文确立了奇宇称磁体(OPM)中反常霍尔效应(AHE)的对称性条件与微观机制：AHE仅在磁点群为铁磁型时允许，其微观来源是OPM固有的"隐藏塞曼场"(HZF)与自旋轨道耦合的协同作用。

The anomalous Hall effect (AHE) in unconventional magnets has attracted considerable attention. A giant AHE has recently been observed in the candidate $p$-wave magnet Gd$_3$(Ru$_{1-δ}$Rh$_δ$)$_4$Al$_{12}$ [Nature 646, 837(2025)], while its theoretical interpretation invokes a spontaneous magnetization to break time-reversal symmetry ($\mathcal{T}$), although the magnetic order itself already breaks $\mathcal{T}$. Consequently, the microscopic origin of the AHE in odd-parity magnets (OPMs) remains an open question. Here we establish the symmetry condition and microscopic mechanism for the AHE in OPMs. Since the OPM order is classified by the spin group whereas the AHE is governed by the magnetic group, the AHE is allowed only when the magnetic point group is ferromagnetic; conversely, it is forbidden whenever the effective time reversal $\mathcal{T}τ$ (with $τ$ a fractional translation), which is essential to the realization of the OPM, is preserved. Microscopically, OPMs generically host a hidden Zeeman field (HZF) rooted in this intrinsic $\mathcal{T}$ breaking, and its interplay with spin-orbit coupling (SOC) generates the AHE without any external field. We demonstrate this picture with models on the square, honeycomb, and kagome lattices. In particular, a square-lattice helimagnet with sublattice-resolved SOC maps exactly onto an anisotropic Rashba--Zeeman model, whose Zeeman term is nothing but the HZF and directly yields the AHE. Our results establish the symmetry condition for the AHE in OPMs and identify the HZF--SOC interplay as a general route to realize it.

### DiffGA: Steering Frozen Diffusion Models for Materials Design via Evolutionary Search
- **arXiv**: [2610.14999](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14999)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14999.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: DiffGA: Steering Frozen Diffusion Models for Materials Design via Evolutionary Search
- **Authors**: Shivang Agarwal, Rodrigo Wang, Kevin Ryczko, Aayush R. Singh
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cs.LG
- **TL;DR**: DiffGA：在预训练扩散模型采样轨迹中注入进化搜索，实现免重训练的逆材料设计，并用MLFF表征驱动的GP代理降低黑箱oracle成本。

Steering candidate generation toward user-defined objectives is central to inverse materials design, yet remains difficult when objectives are black-box, changing, or non-differentiable, as in many simulation- and experiment-derived properties. Generative models offer a solution but typically require retraining or fine-tuning for each new objective, demanding labeled data and substantial compute, or rely on differentiable guidance that is brittle to gradient noise and undefined for many practical targets. We present DiffGA, a retraining-free framework that injects evolutionary search into the sampling trajectory of a retrained diffusion model, evolving partially denoised states under an arbitrary black-box oracle. Because oracle evaluation often dominates campaign cost, we couple DiffGA with an on-the-fly Bayesian surrogate that reuses representations from state-of-the-art machine-learned force fields (MLFFs). A Gaussian process is fit during the campaign on the force fields's graph embeddings and, once it demonstrably ranks an unseen generation, replaces expensive property evaluations under an uncertainty-aware acquisition rule, reducing oracle cost with negligible loss of accuracy. Across three crystal-design tasks using a frozen MatterGen backbone, DiffGA outperforms diffusion-only, compute-matched, purely evolutionary, and fine-tuned baselines in oracle-assessed rates of stable, unique, and novel hits. Rather than proposing scientific compounds, we use density functional theory to quantify MLFF oracle fidelity, showing that it is a faithful proxy in key regimes but systematically biased in others, delineating when proxy-driven generative discovery can be trusted. On a representative sample, DiffGA's top hits survive first principles re-evaluation as physically realistic hits, while the surrogate-accelerated pipeline preserves this fidelity at a fraction of the cost.

### Charge-selective topological locking and ultraslow coarsening in magnetoelastic media
- **arXiv**: [2610.18320](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18320)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18320.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Charge-selective topological locking and ultraslow coarsening in magnetoelastic media
- **Authors**: Tirthankar Banerjee, Amaresh Jaiswal
- **Categories**: cond-mat.stat-mech
- **TL;DR**: 磁弹耦合的对称性决定复合缺陷的拓扑组织，并能定性改变相序化动力学：各向同性磁致伸缩给出XY类粗化，而四极磁弹耦合锁定磁/弹缺陷电荷，导致反常慢的涡旋湮灭（密度t^{-2/5}，速度t^{-4/5}，z≈5）。

We demonstrate that the symmetry of magnetoelastic coupling governs the topological organization of composite defects and can qualitatively alter phase-ordering kinetics. In a two-dimensional theory of an in-plane magnetic vector coupled to elastic strain, we find that integer magnetic vortices bind pairs of elementary elastic half-defects. Isotropic magnetostriction produces a core-induced elastic winding independent of the magnetic charge, giving defect binding without charge locking and leaving the magnetic sector with essentially XY-like coarsening. In contrast, quadrupolar magnetoelastic coupling locks magnetic and elastic defect charges: continuous rotational symmetry favors $q_φ=q_θ$, whereas crystalline anisotropy can reverse the locking to $q_φ=-q_θ$. We reveal that both locking sectors display anomalously slow vortex annihilation: defect density shows a power-law decay $t^{-2/5}$, while the average defect-velocity scales as $t^{-4/5}$. These results are consistent with a dynamic exponent $z\simeq 5$. We show that this coarsening slowdown is associated with large anisotropic elastic friction. An adiabatic elimination of the elastic field reveals that isotropic magnetostriction merely renormalizes local magnetic interactions, whereas orientational coupling induces effective nonlocal interactions between magnetic spins. Our experimentally testable results thus establish magnetoelastic coupling symmetry as a means of controlling defect topology and ordering kinetics.

### Dynamical chiral symmetry breaking in rotating QED$_3$: critical flavor number and the occupation-reweighting channel
- **arXiv**: [2610.15568](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15568)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15568.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Dynamical chiral symmetry breaking in rotating QED$_3$: critical flavor number and the occupation-reweighting channel
- **Authors**: Wei Li
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, hep-th
- **TL;DR**: 在刚体旋转圆盘上的(2+1)维QED中，用rainbow Schwinger–Dyson框架研究动力学手征对称破缺：零温下旋转对凝聚的响应恒为零，临界味数保持不变；有限温下旋转通过占据重加权通道强烈抑制凝聚。

We study dynamical chiral symmetry breaking in $(2{+}1)$-dimensional QED on a rigidly rotating disc, $ΩR&lt;1$, within the rainbow Schwinger--Dyson framework. At zero temperature the rotation response of the condensate vanishes identically, so $N_c^{\rm SD}(Ω,T{=}0)=N_c^{\rm SD}(0)=2.60\pm0.01$ (using three operational definitions). At finite temperature, rotation suppresses the condensate through a dominant occupation-reweighting channel, $\hatκ_{\rm occ}=-1.55$, accounting for $\ge 99.995\%$ of the linearized total response at the reference parameters, with self-consistent feedback amplifying it by $26.5$. The screening channel is reported at fixed regularization, $\hatκ_{\rm scr}(\varepsilon = 0.01)=-0.97$; its $\varepsilon \to 0$ behavior is logarithmically divergent. The crossover flavor $N^{*}(Ω)$ falls monotonically at fixed temperature (a corollary). The critical flavor number is unchanged by rotation at $T=0$ (theorem). Together these results set the finite-temperature reference for the model.

### Photon echo from Maki-Thompson interference in a fluctuating s-wave superconductor
- **arXiv**: [2610.16462](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16462)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16462.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Photon echo from Maki-Thompson interference in a fluctuating s-wave superconductor
- **Authors**: Hui Zhou, Yuan Wan
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con, cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 本文理论研究了由两个连续太赫兹脉冲驱动的涨落s波超导体的非线性光学响应，发现Maki-Thompson过程中的时间反演电子-空穴轨迹量子干涉会产生光子回波信号，而Aslamazov-Larkin过程不产生回波，可用于区分不同的涨落电导机制。

We study theoretically the nonlinear optical response of a fluctuating s-wave superconductor driven by two successive terahertz pulses. We show that the response exhibits a photon echo signal that arises from the quantum interference between the time-reversed electron and hole trajectories in the Maki-Thompson process. By contrast, the transport of the fluctuating Cooper pairs responsible for the Aslamazov-Larkin conductivity does not generate an echo. The photon echo signal can be readily measured using terahertz two-dimensional coherent spectroscopy, highlighting its potential to distinguish different fluctuation conductivity mechanisms.

### Tersoff-NN: Hybrid Machine-Learned Bond-Order Extension of the Tersoff Potential
- **arXiv**: [2610.15591](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15591)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15591.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Tersoff-NN: Hybrid Machine-Learned Bond-Order Extension of the Tersoff Potential
- **Authors**: Yusuke Nishimura, Sho Kubota, Kotaro Takematsu, Kentaro Hirai, Takanobu Watanabe
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph, cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 作者提出 Tersoff-NN：保留 Tersoff 势的解析径向项与截断结构，仅用紧凑神经网络表示环境依赖的键序因子，从而在保持高效与稳定性的同时超越经验势精度。

Accurate, robust, and efficient interatomic potentials are highly desirable for large-scale molecular dynamics simulations. Although machine-learning interatomic potentials can reproduce first-principles potential-energy surfaces with high accuracy, they typically incur higher computational costs than conventional empirical potentials and may be less reliable outside their training domains. We developed Tersoff-NN, a hybrid machine-learning potential for Si, which retains the analytical radial interactions and cutoff structure of the Tersoff potential while representing its environment-dependent bond-order factor using compact neural networks (NNs). Benchmarks for 32,768-atom systems show that Tersoff-NN is 4-12 times faster than the Behler-Parrinello-type NN potentials tested in this study. Tersoff-NN improves the short-range structure of liquid Si relative to the conventional Tersoff potential, closely reproduces the ab initio radial and angular distributions, and reproduces the equilibrium structure of diamond Si and qualitative energetic trends among several crystalline phases. Furthermore, melt-quench simulations reveal a disordered solid with predominantly four-coordinated, tetrahedral-like local environments. Additionally, characteristic reconstruction motifs are retained on Si surfaces despite their exclusion from the training dataset. A direct comparison with an alternative atom-centered bond-order NN highlights the critical role of physical constraints in ensuring dynamic stability. Despite exhibiting comparable validation errors, the selected triplet-based architecture remains stable, while an alternative model exhibits unphysical bond-order growth and atomic collapse. These results confirm that learning only the bond-order term improves the description of diverse Si environments while retaining substantial computational efficiency and enhancing dynamical stability.

### SIREN: A nonlinear Rankine-source boundary element solver for the stationary motion of a marine vehicle in calm water
- **arXiv**: [2610.14027](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14027)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14027.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: SIREN: A nonlinear Rankine-source boundary element solver for the stationary motion of a marine vehicle in calm water
- **Authors**: Daniele Peri
- **Categories**: physics.comp-ph, math-ph, physics.flu-dyn
- **TL;DR**: SIREN是一个基于Rankine源面元法的船舶稳态兴波阻力求解器，沿用Dawson方法传统。它在真实边界（干湿船体+自由面）上求解Neumann问题作为基流，取代经典双体流，并通过迭代线性化实现非线性自由面。阻力由压力积分与横切波分析两种独立方法获得，结果一致。

SIREN (Ship Inviscid Resistance and Elevation eNgine) is a Rankine-source panel solver for the steady wave resistance of a ship, in the tradition of Dawson's method. The free-surface condition is linearised about a base flow, and the linearisation is iterated. In the present formulation the base flow is the solution of a Neumann problem on the actual boundary: sources on the entire hull, wet and dry, and on the free surface panels, with impermeability imposed on both, so that the free surface acts as a rigid lid at the position it currently occupies. This replaces the classical double-body flow, whose image about z=0 is meaningful only for a flat surface and a hull cut at z=0, and it removes the need for desingularised free-surface panels. In the non-linear iteration the free-surface panels are moved rigidly in the vertical to the relaxed wave elevation at their centroid, the hull panels are marked wet or dry against the moved surface, and the base flow is recomputed on the moved geometry; impermeability applies to the whole hull regardless of this marking, which is used only to select which panels enter the force integral, and under-relaxation is confined to the passes where the non-linear correction accumulates without being reset. The radiation condition is imposed by a one-row upstream shift of the collocation points, and the wave resistance is obtained by pressure integration and, independently, by transverse wave-cut analysis.

### Graphene-Confined Growth of Few-Layer PtSe2 via One-Step Selenization
- **arXiv**: [2610.15022](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15022)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15022.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Graphene-Confined Growth of Few-Layer PtSe2 via One-Step Selenization
- **Authors**: Timea Ema Krajčovičová, Peter Kotrusz, Viera Skákalová, Agáta Laurenčíková, Igor Píš, Martin Hulman, Kimmo Mustonen, Michaela Sojková
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 利用氧化石墨烯覆盖层作为物理限域层，在硒化过程中抑制PtSe₂的垂直团簇生长、促进二维横向生长，实现厘米级超薄PtSe₂/石墨烯异质结构的可控制备。

Platinum diselenide (PtSe$_2$), a member of the transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) family, is a promising material for next-generation electronic and optoelectronic applications due to its thickness-dependent properties, transitioning from semimetallic in bulk to semiconducting behavior at the few-layer limit. However, the scalable fabrication of large-area ultrathin PtSe$_2$ films remains challenging, as conventional selenization of pre-deposited Pt layers often leads to undesired three-dimensional clustered growth. This work explores a novel strategy for promoting two-dimensional growth of PtSe$_2$ by employing a graphene-mediated confinement approach. The method involves coating a Pt layer with graphene oxide (GO), followed by annealing in a selenium atmosphere, which results in the simultaneous formation of PtSe$_2$ and the reduction of GO to graphene. The graphene overlayer acts as a physical confinement layer, suppressing vertical growth and enabling the synthesis of graphene-confined PtSe$_2$ heterostructures on centimetre-scale substrates and promoting lateral connectivity of PtSe$_2$ regions observed by STEM. Scanning transmission electron microscopy (STEM), including temperature-resolved in situ atomic-resolution measurements, was used to investigate the influence of selenization temperature on the growth mechanism and structural evolution of PtSe$_2$ films. The results show that graphene confinement stabilizes lateral growth and promotes the formation of few-layer films with improved structural ordering. Beyond PtSe$_2$, this graphene-assisted approach provides a general strategy for fabricating and stabilizing ultrathin TMD layers, including those prone to environmental degradation, providing a new outlook for enabling detailed studies of their intrinsic properties.

### Emergence of Nonlocal Hydrodynamics in Many-Body Radiative Heat Transport
- **arXiv**: [2610.16488](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16488)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16488.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Emergence of Nonlocal Hydrodynamics in Many-Body Radiative Heat Transport
- **Authors**: Mauricio Gomez Viloria, Florian Herz, Riccardo Messina, Philippe Ben-Abdallah
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 本文通过对多体系统微观电动力学相互作用做粗粒化，建立了一个辐射热输运的非局域流体动力学理论，并揭示Fourier定律只在核函数二阶空间矩有限时作为长波极限出现，否则表现为非Fourier输运。

We develop a nonlocal hydrodynamic theory of radiative heat transport in many-body systems by coarse graining their microscopic electrodynamic interactions. Long-range radiative exchange naturally generates a spatially nonlocal constitutive relation in which the heat current at a given position depends on temperature gradients over an extended region, while energy conservation retains its local conservative form. Fourier transport emerges as a controlled long-wavelength limit when the interaction kernel has a finite second spatial moment. When this moment fails to converge, spatial nonlocality survives coarse graining and gives rise to non-Fourier transport. The resulting framework establishes a systematic bridge between discrete many-body fluctuational electrodynamics and a continuous description of radiative heat transport.

### Role of defect trapping in the enhanced corrosion resistance of Ni-Mo alloys with yttrium and yttrium oxide additions
- **arXiv**: [2610.15089](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15089)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15089.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Role of defect trapping in the enhanced corrosion resistance of Ni-Mo alloys with yttrium and yttrium oxide additions
- **Authors**: Jia-Hong Ke, Xinchang Zhang, Jingfan Yang, Qiufeng Yang, Ruchi Gakhar, Rongjie Song
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该文提出通过稀Y溶质与Y₂O₃弥散相捕获空位、抑制扩散来减缓Ni合金在熔盐中的腐蚀，并用DFT+SCMF计算与750°C熔盐实验证实NM→NMY→NMYO腐蚀逐级减轻。

Corrosion of structural alloys remains a critical challenge for advanced molten salt reactors, where aggressive fluoride salts promote continuous dissolution at the salt-metal interface. While most mitigation strategies emphasize thermodynamic stability, kinetic control of atomic transport has received comparatively less attention. Here, we propose a corrosion mitigation strategy based on diffusion control and vacancy trapping by dilute yttrium (Y) solutes and yttrium oxide (Y$_2$O$_3$) dispersions in Ni alloys. A combined computational and experimental approach integrates density functional theory (DFT), self-consistent mean-field (SCMF) diffusion modeling, and an impurity trapping model to quantify the impact of defect trapping on effective vacancy mobility. Strong first-nearest-neighbor vacancy-Y binding, off-lattice vacancy configurations, and elevated rotational and dissociation barriers result in pronounced suppression of vacancy diffusivity, while Y$_2$O$_3$-associated sites treated as fixed traps in the model further reduce the mobile vacancy population. Ni-Mo (NM), Y-modified (NMY), and Y$_2$O$_3$-containing (NMYO) alloys were tested in molten fluoride salt at 750°C. Corrosion penetration decreases systematically from NM to NMY to NMYO, consistent with the predicted reduction in vacancy-mediated transport. The same trend is observed in mass change, with net weight loss decreasing from 0.52% for NM to 0.23% for NMY and 0.09% for NMYO. These results are consistent with an alloy-side transport contribution from defect trapping and reduced atomic transport. This study therefore suggests point defect engineering as a promising alloy design strategy for extreme nuclear environments.

### Macroscopic fluctuation theory of stochastic systems undergoing monitoring
- **arXiv**: [2610.18500](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18500)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18500.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Macroscopic fluctuation theory of stochastic systems undergoing monitoring
- **Authors**: João Costa, Pedro Ribeiro, Andrea De Luca
- **Categories**: cond-mat.stat-mech
- **TL;DR**: 本文为受连续粗粒化监测的经典扩散晶格气体建立了宏观涨落理论（MFT），揭示监测在MFT作用量中引入二次约束项，并系统分析了其对电流大偏差性质、动力学相变以及强监测极限的影响。

We develop a macroscopic fluctuation theory for diffusive classical lattice gases subject to continuous coarse-grained monitoring. Treating measurements in the typical-outcome (quenched) regime, we show that observation adds a quadratic confining term to the MFT action that pins the density to its hydrodynamic profile. We derive sufficient conditions under which, in the presence of monitoring, the current large-deviation function remains convex, the additivity principle holds, and dynamical phase transitions are excluded. For particle-hole-symmetric one-dimensional models we find that monitoring suppresses dynamical phase transitions and can change their order from second to first, as we demonstrate in the Katz--Lebowitz--Spohn model. In the strong-measurement limit, we find that the current distribution becomes Gaussian and determine the scaling of its cumulants: they follow simple power laws when the mobility remains nonzero throughout the system, but exhibit anomalous logarithmic scaling when the mobility vanishes at the boundaries.

### Realistic Simulation of Valence-band Photoemission Spectra by using First-principles Calculations on the Basis of FLAPW Method
- **arXiv**: [2610.15602](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15602)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15602.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Realistic Simulation of Valence-band Photoemission Spectra by using First-principles Calculations on the Basis of FLAPW Method
- **Authors**: Noriaki Hamada, Satoshi Nakamura, Kenta Takahashi, Mario Okawa, Tomohiko Saitoh
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 提出一种基于能带计算（LAPW）的原子亚壳层光电子微分截面（PE-DCS）新方法，用PDOS与PI-DCS乘积之和再现PES谱，并用Na、NaCl及CuAlO2的HX-PES数据验证。

In order to reproduce experimental photoemission spectroscopy (PES) data by using band structure calculations with linearized augmented-plane-wave method, we developed a new method to calculate the atomic-subshell photoelectron differential cross section (PE-DCS) of the valence band. PE-DCS was calculated to be the sum of the products of the partial density of states (PDOS) and the photoionization differential cross section (PI-DCS) of each atomic subshell. To construct an appropriate PDOS beyond the conventional muffin-tin sphere (MTS) PDOS, we proposed a ``nearly-neutral atomic sphere", which can be defined as a natural extension of MTS. Although the calculational method for atomic-subshell PI-DCS was the standard Hartree-Fock-Slater method, we developed a new computer code in order to deal with the unoccupied electron shells in the ground state of isolated atoms up to $\sim$10 keV. Details of the PE-DCS calculations were examined by using two simple examples of Na metal and NaCl, and then, the method was applied to hard x-ray PES (HX-PES) data of CuAlO$_2$. The experimental spectra were satisfactorily reproduced, demonstrating the potential applications of our method to HX-PES spectra of many compounds.

### Molecule-intercalated van der Waals Josephson junctions uncover complex multiband superconductivity
- **arXiv**: [2610.16483](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16483)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16483.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Molecule-intercalated van der Waals Josephson junctions uncover complex multiband superconductivity
- **Authors**: Daniel Margineda, David Caldevilla-Asenjo, Covadonga Álvarez-García, Daniel Tezze, Raul Arenal, Maxim Ilyn, Celia Rogero, Luis E. Hueso, Marco Gobbi
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con, cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 基于TaS2/NbSe2异质结的分子插层Josephson结，观测到不对称Fraunhofer干涉、场致与自发Josephson二极管效应，指向时间反演对称破缺的多带超导态。

Intercalation of molecular species provides a route to tailor the electronic structure in van der Waals superconductors, yet intercalated compounds have rarely been integrated into phase-sensitive quantum devices. Here, we realize hybrid molecular Josephson junctions based on TaS2/NbSe2 heterostructures. Selective intercalation of the TaS2 electrode induces monolayer-like Ising superconductivity, while the junctions exhibit asymmetric Fraunhofer interference, field-induced and spontaneous Josephson diode effects. The zero-field diode disappears upon reversing the electrode stacking and re-emerges after intercalating the lower NbSe2 electrode. These findings indicate that the hybrid superconducting electrode hosts unconventional superconductivity underlying the nonreciprocal transport, while molecular intercalation acts as a tuning knob for its internal phase structure. This behavior is consistent with a time-reversal-symmetry-breaking multiband superconducting state characterized by a nontrivial relative phase between superconducting condensates. Our results establish hybrid molecular Josephson junctions as a quantum-device platform for phase-sensitive investigation and integration of molecularly engineered superconductors, opening opportunities to uncover and engineer unconventional superconducting phases.

### Kernel-Level Constant-Potential Molecular Dynamics in GROMACS
- **arXiv**: [2610.15733](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15733)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15733.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Kernel-Level Constant-Potential Molecular Dynamics in GROMACS
- **Authors**: Sheng Bi, Mathieu Salanne, Jun Cheng, Guang Feng
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph, physics.comp-ph
- **TL;DR**: 本文在GROMACS中开源实现了恒电势方法，将电极原子电荷的动态计算集成到CPU与GPU优化例程中，从而低成本地模拟电极电荷重排及其电化学响应。

How charge rearranges within an electrode shapes the behavior of batteries, supercapacitors, and other electrochemical devices. Molecular simulations can capture this response by allowing the charge on each electrode atom to adjust to its surroundings, but repeatedly calculating these charges adds substantial computational cost. We present an open-source implementation of the constant-potential method in GROMACS that integrates these calculations into the program's optimized CPU and GPU routines. Comparisons with established codes validate the electrode charges, while charging, voltammetry, and impedance simulations demonstrate the response to changing electrical conditions. The implementation also supports electrodes that move or deform and models of finite metallic screening and electron spillover. In a pair of charged carbon nanotubes, allowing charge to move away from the facing surfaces reduces the repulsive force at contact by about half at an intermediate charge and keeps the pair bound where a uniform-charge model opens within the simulated time. These capabilities make the electrical response of electrodes accessible within GROMACS for studies of interfacial structure, charging dynamics, and electrode motion.

### A Gauge-Inspired Coupled Fokker--Planck System for Multistate Stochastic Dynamics: Formalism and 1-D Numerical Implementation
- **arXiv**: [2610.14052](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14052)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14052.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: A Gauge-Inspired Coupled Fokker--Planck System for Multistate Stochastic Dynamics: Formalism and 1-D Numerical Implementation
- **Authors**: Francesco Pio De Cosmo, Gianluca Di Natale, Leandro Cieri
- **Categories**: physics.comp-ph, cond-mat.stat-mech, hep-th
- **TL;DR**: 本文首次为SU(2)规范耦合的Fokker–Planck系统 (1+1维) 构建数值求解器，采用算子分裂与非振荡对流格式保证概率守恒、非负性与稳定性，揭示非阿贝尔规范耦合会形成交叉反馈回路，导致状态间持续概率交换、抑制局部峰值并迫使长期空间离域。

Extending the Fokker--Planck equation to a coupled system of equations through non-Abelian symmetry groups offers a powerful theoretical framework for coupled multi-state stochastic systems. However, translating this abstract gauge field formulation into viable numerical predictions remains an unaddressed challenge. Motivated by our previous work on ice crystal habit evolution, in this work we bridge this gap by presenting the first numerical solver for an SU(2) gauge-coupled Fokker--Planck system in (1+1) dimensions. Using an operator-splitting scheme combined with a non-oscillatory advection solver, our algorithm strictly ensures probability conservation, non-negativity, and numerical stability. We demonstrate that non-Abelian gauge coupling acts as a cross-reactive feedback loop that fundamentally alters system dynamics, driving continuous probability exchange between states, suppressing localised density peaks, and forcing long-time spatial delocalisation. Validated across linear and quadratic coupling regimes up to strong interactions (g &lt;= 1.0), this work establishes the foundational computational infrastructure needed to integrate complex multi-state physics. Overall, while our approach provides a key step toward improving ice cloud representation in atmospheric and climate models, the underlying physical framework and computational engine remain fundamentally general and multidisciplinary, offering a versatile paradigm for any domain requiring coupled multi-state stochastic equations.

### Interfacial Coupling Reshapes Nonlinear Polarization in a Spin-Defect van der Waals Heterostructure
- **arXiv**: [2610.15189](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15189)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15189.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Interfacial Coupling Reshapes Nonlinear Polarization in a Spin-Defect van der Waals Heterostructure
- **Authors**: Lena Dalifoski, Eveline Mayner, Poonam Singh
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, physics.optics
- **TL;DR**: 利用偏振分辨二次谐波成像，在 hBN/PbI₂ 异质结中观测到缺陷非线性响应的非加和增强。

Van der Waals heterostructures offer a direct route to couple an optically active layer to a two-dimensional spin sensor, yet their coherent nonlinear response at interfaces remains poorly resolved. Here, we use polarization-resolved second harmonic-generation imaging to resolve four distinct regions of a hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) -based heterostructure, native hBN, neutron-irradiated hBN containing negatively charged boron vacancies (VB-), PbI2 and their complete overlap. The detected intensity scales quadratically with incident power, consistent with a dominant second-order optical process. Polarization scans show that adding PbI2 transforms the distorted VB- SHG anisotropy into a predominantly fourfold response with a sixfold stronger SHG than native hBN alone. This non-additive nonlinear response occurs in the same layer architecture also shown to enhance defect photoluminescence, linking spin-defect sensitization to an interface-modified effective second-order susceptibility.

### Probing quantum nonlinear phononics in multilayer graphene with time-resolved Raman scattering
- **arXiv**: [2610.16566](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16566)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16566.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Probing quantum nonlinear phononics in multilayer graphene with time-resolved Raman scattering
- **Authors**: Jakob Dolgner, Michael Rübhausen, Dirk Manske
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, cond-mat.other
- **TL;DR**: 本文比较经典、半经典与完整量子理论，说明半经典方法能更好刻画驱动声子的量子涨落，并提出非平衡自发拉曼散射作为探测量子非线性声子学的灵敏手段。

Coherently driven phonons are conventionally described classically. Nonlinear phonon-phonon interactions, however, allow an infrared drive to reshape the fluctuation spectrum and generate substantial nonthermal enhancement of phonon fluctuations, motivating a description beyond the coherent displacement alone. We benchmark classical and semiclassical approximations against the full quantum theory and find that the semiclassical treatment substantially improves agreement with the quantum dynamics. We propose nonequilibrium spontaneous Raman scattering as a sensitive probe of these effects and apply our framework to recent experiments suggesting quantum nonlinear phononics involving the $\mathrm{E}_{1u}$ and $\mathrm{E}_{2g}$ modes in multilayer graphene. At very strong pump fields, driving the $\mathrm{E}_{1u}$ mode significantly modifies the $\mathrm{E}_{2g}$ Raman spectrum; however, reproducing the reported response at experimental field strengths requires accounting for pump-induced Raman matrix element dynamics or alternative energy pathways into the $\mathrm{E}_{2g}$ mode. Our findings extend nonlinear phononics beyond average atomic displacements to the dynamics of lattice fluctuations, highlighting the interplay between coherent motion and quantum correlations in driven materials.

### Nucleation at the GST--oxide interface and beam-driven ordering in amorphous Ge--Sb--Te: HAADF, EDS and electron pair distribution functions across a 4D-STEM dose series
- **arXiv**: [2610.15196](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15196)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15196.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Nucleation at the GST--oxide interface and beam-driven ordering in amorphous Ge--Sb--Te: HAADF, EDS and electron pair distribution functions across a 4D-STEM dose series
- **Authors**: Mridul Kumar, Kirill Sobolev, Alexander Upcher, Yevgeny Rakita
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该研究通过多次4D-STEM扫描（同时采集HAADF与EDS）研究磁控溅射GST薄膜中晶核的形成位置，发现晶核在成分与数密度上与基体无异，真正决定成核的是薄膜与下方氧化物的界面。

Where crystal nuclei form in amorphous phase-change materials decides how they switch. We study six successive 4D-STEM scans, with simultaneous HAADF and EDS data, of a magnetron-sputtered Ge-Sb-Te (GST) film, restricted to the GST layer and its interface with the oxide beneath it. Nucleation sites do not differ in composition or number density: the nuclei lie on the same robust HAADF-EDS relation as the matrix, and at fixed HAADF the spread of the summed EDS signal equals its Poisson counting noise (ratio 1.03 +- 0.05). The nuclei are nevertheless crystalline and stand out in the 4D-STEM max-intensity virtual image (median z = 11.0) but not in HAADF (z = 1.3) or EDS (z = 0.3). We classify the film in four ways (HAADF intensity, I(q) phase mapping, PCA of the whole diffraction patterns and a self-supervised SimCLR encoder) and compare the electron pair distribution functions (PDFs) of all classes after calibrating reciprocal space on the Au cap. All bulk classes share one short-range structure (first shell 2.70-2.74 Å); towards the interface the first sharp diffraction peak (FSDP) moves to lower q, and every method isolates a thin interface-edge environment with a shorter or missing first shell. The I(q) classification isolates, in every scan, an interface environment that holds 62-100% of the nucleus pixels (1.2-2.5-fold above depth) and persists when the nucleus pixels are removed before classification. The interface, rather than a distinct composition or density of the nucleating volume, therefore marks where nuclei form. Over 4.7e3 e/Å^2 the HAADF signal falls by 17%, the first shell shortens by 0.03 Å and the FSDP narrows by 9%, apparently ordering the film as it damages it. All analyses used PySTEMLab, an interactive 4D-STEM analysis environment developed for this work.

### Exact Microscopic One-Body Reduction in the Symmetric Exclusion Process
- **arXiv**: [2610.18626](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18626)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18626.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Exact Microscopic One-Body Reduction in the Symmetric Exclusion Process
- **Authors**: Olivier Bénichou, Aurélien Grabsch
- **Categories**: cond-mat.stat-mech
- **TL;DR**: 对称排他过程(SEP)的电流-密度关联层级在格点微观层面精确坍缩为单个线性方程，等价于单个辅助随机游走者的持久性问题。

The symmetric exclusion process (SEP) is a paradigmatic model of interacting particle transport. Its current--density correlations are governed by an infinite many-body hierarchy of equations. Only recently, this hierarchy was found to collapse at large scales onto a single closed equation. Is this remarkable simplification created by coarse graining, or does it reflect a deeper property already present in the microscopic dynamics? Here we show that the second scenario is realized. At arbitrary time on the lattice, the entire hierarchy collapses onto a single linear equation which is exactly equivalent to a persistence problem for a single auxiliary random walker. Thus an interacting many-body problem reduces exactly to a genuine one-body problem before any scaling limit is taken. This reduction determines the complete finite-time correlation profile, including during nonequilibrium relaxation. More broadly, the microscopic operator uncovered here opens a route to more general correlation functions in the SEP, and may point to a general organizing principle for nonequilibrium interacting particle systems.

### Single-Particle Spectroscopy and Cross-Barrier Dynamics in Inhomogeneous Luttinger Liquids via Unified Field Bosonization Technique
- **arXiv**: [2610.15608](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15608)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15608.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Single-Particle Spectroscopy and Cross-Barrier Dynamics in Inhomogeneous Luttinger Liquids via Unified Field Bosonization Technique
- **Authors**: Soundarya P, Venkata Suryanarayana M, Joy Prakash Das
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 本文用统一场玻色化技术(UFBT)研究含局域杂质团簇的非均匀Luttinger液体的单粒子性质，利用费米子两点关联函数导出动力学态密度(DDOS)与跨势垒隧穿电导的领头奇异项，发现DDOS在杂质附近与远场呈不同幂律，隧穿电导随温度呈幂律且指数仅由相互作用决定，还原子杂质强度无需微扰或显式RG处理。

The Unified Field Bosonization Technique (UFBT) is employed to investigate the single-particle properties of inhomogeneous Luttinger liquids containing localized clusters of impurities in the presence of forward-scattering interactions. Within this framework, fermionic two-point correlation functions are used to derive the leading-order singular contributions to the dynamical density of states (DDOS) and the cross-barrier tunneling conductance. The results reveal distinct power-law behaviours of the DDOS near the impurity cluster and in the far-field regime. The tunneling conductance exhibits a power-law temperature dependence, decreasing with decreasing temperature for repulsive interactions and increasing for attractive interactions, while remaining temperature-independent in the noninteracting limit. The impurity cluster modifies the amplitudes and spatial structure of these observables, whereas their characteristic power-law exponents are determined solely by the interaction parameters. The formulation accommodates arbitrary impurity strengths without requiring perturbative expansions in the impurity or interaction strengths. The characteristic scaling behaviour emerges directly from the fermionic correlation functions, without an explicit renormalization-group (RG) treatment of the impurity strength, while the weak-interaction limit recovers the leading perturbative correction to the tunneling response. These findings establish UFBT as a unified framework for describing local spectral properties and cross-barrier transport in inhomogeneous Luttinger liquids.

### Superconductivity from altermagnetic fluctuations: the crucial role of sublattice polarization
- **arXiv**: [2610.16507](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16507)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16507.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Superconductivity from altermagnetic fluctuations: the crucial role of sublattice polarization
- **Authors**: Mats Barkman, Andreas Kreisel, Brian M. Andersen
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con, cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 研究无序交替磁涨落中的超导，发现亚晶格极化(SLP)阻止不同亚晶格间形成库珀对，使配对由近邻AFM交换主导转为同亚晶格长程键主导，在NNN铁磁交换下产生自旋三重态超导。

Unconventional superconductivity in correlated systems often appears in the vicinity of a magnetically ordered state. This situation is also relevant for altermagnets, characterized by a nonrelativistic spin-split electronic structure in the presence of Néel order. Many proposed altermagnetic candidates, however, never cross the magnetic instability. We study superconductivity arising from altermagnetic fluctuations in such systems and show that the sublattice polarization (SLP) of the normal-state bands plays a pivotal role in determining the superconducting state. SLP, an inherent property of altermagnetic band structures that persists even without magnetic order, prevents Cooper pairs from forming between different sublattices. This suppresses pairing mediated by the nearest-neighbor antiferromagnetic exchange, which favors spin-singlet superconductivity, and leaves longer-range same-sublattice bonds dominant. When the next-nearest-neighbor exchange is ferromagnetic, as expected for frustration-free Néel order, this results in spin-triplet superconductivity. This mechanism is fundamentally different from that in altermagnets with long-range Néel order, where the spin-split Fermi surfaces favor equal-spin pairing. Finally, when longer-range interactions in the disordered phase instead favor singlet pairing, SLP leads to singlet states with characteristic properties distinct from nearest-neighbor pairing states.

### The RAS-CCSD model, with applications for open-shell molecules
- **arXiv**: [2610.15848](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15848)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15848.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: The RAS-CCSD model, with applications for open-shell molecules
- **Authors**: Sarai Dery Folkestad
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph
- **TL;DR**: 提出并实现RAS-CCSD模型，一种基于限制性活性空间的耦合簇方法，用于开壳层和多参考体系，基态精度可比UCCSD，激发态误差较大但保持自旋匹配。

The restricted active space coupled cluster singles and doubles (RAS-CCSD) model, and its pilot implementation is detailed. The approach offers a simple, pragmatic way to formulate a coupled cluster theory for open-shell and multireference systems. For ground state energies, errors (compared to FCI) are shown to be similar to those of unrestricted (spin-orbital) CCSD. For excited states, the errors of RAS-CCSD are generally larger, and adaptations to the methodology are needed to achieve an approach which is balanced for both ground and excited states. However, unlike unrestricted CCSD, the approach is spin-adapted and can be used to add dynamical correlation to a multideterminantal reference state.

### acousticHelmholtzFoam: an MPI-parallel unstructured finite-volume solver for frequency-domain acoustics in heterogeneous two-phase media with perfectly matched layers
- **arXiv**: [2610.14057](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14057)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14057.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: acousticHelmholtzFoam: an MPI-parallel unstructured finite-volume solver for frequency-domain acoustics in heterogeneous two-phase media with perfectly matched layers
- **Authors**: Chuanchao Xu, Jun Liu, Dieter Bothe, Tomislav Maric
- **Categories**: physics.comp-ph, cs.MS, physics.flu-dyn
- **TL;DR**: acousticHelmholtzFoam 是一个基于 OpenFOAM 的开源求解器，用于在非结构多面体网格上计算气液两相介质中的时谐声压场，支持 PML 开放边界、PETSc/MUMPS 并行直接求解，并输出声辐射压力与声力。

acousticHelmholtzFoam is an OpenFOAM application that computes time-harmonic acoustic pressure fields in heterogeneous gas-liquid media on unstructured polyhedral meshes. The complex pressure is stored as real and imaginary volume fields, the material coefficients are constructed from a volume-of-fluid phase fraction with geometric face-fraction averaging, and open boundaries are truncated by Cartesian perfectly matched layers (PMLs) whose damping tensor is generated by the companion utility setPMLFields. The two coupled real-valued equations are discretized with the cell-centred finite-volume operators of OpenFOAM and assembled explicitly, including processor couplings and boundary contributions, into a distributed PETSc matrix that is solved by default with the MUMPS sparse direct solver. The solver writes pressure amplitude, complex velocity, acoustic radiation pressure and time-averaged momentum flux for acoustic-force evaluation. This paper documents the implemented equations, the case interface, the assembly and solution algorithm, the build procedure and the test infrastructure. A layered gas-liquid-PML case with an analytical solution serves as an end-to-end regression: on 8000 cells the whole-domain relative complex-pressure error is $1.197\times10^{-3}$, and serial and MPI solutions on up to eight ranks agree to within $10^{-12}$ in relative $L_2$ norm.
