A single-axis acoustic-levitator height sweep with constant-volume spheroidal drops demonstrates the two-phase material input and the force output, and strong- and problem-size-scaling measurements characterize the direct-solver workflow. The numerical formulation and its verification are presented in a companion paper (arXiv:2610.01593).

### Transition-Metal Chalcogenide Nanowires: A First-Principles Study of Stability, Electronic Structure, and Mechanical Response
- **arXiv**: [2610.15214](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15214)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15214.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Transition-Metal Chalcogenide Nanowires: A First-Principles Study of Stability, Electronic Structure, and Mechanical Response
- **Authors**: Danilo de Araújo Moura, Flavio Bento de Oliveira, Andreia Luisa da Rosa
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 该文用DFT+机器学习力场分子动力学系统研究了MoS、MoSe、MoTe、WS、WSe、WTe六种亚纳米过渡金属硫族化物纳米线的结构稳定性、电子性质与力学响应。

Subnanometer transition-metal chalcogenide nanowires provide a one-dimensional platform in which electronic functionality and mechanical response can be tuned through chemical composition. Here, density functional theory calculations are combined with machine-learning force-field (MLFF) molecular dynamics to investigate MoS, MoSe, MoTe, WS, WSe, and WTe nanowires. The optimized structures are dynamically stable within the harmonic approximation, and their electronic character evolves systematically across the chalcogen series: MoS, MoSe, WS, and WSe are metallic, whereas MoTe and WTe are semiconducting, with HSE06 band gaps of 0.52 and 0.20 eV, respectively. The effective axial stiffness decreases from sulfides to selenides and tellurides, consistent with the increase in metal--chalcogen bond length; using a circular effective cross section, the corresponding Young moduli range from 392 to 718 GPa. All six nanowires have negative formation energies relative to the selected elemental reference phases. MLFF trajectories extending to 2.5 ns at 300 K preserve the one-dimensional morphology and local atomic order, providing evidence of finite-temperature dynamical stability on the simulated time scale. The corresponding MLFF molecular-dynamics energy traces are shown in. We further examine the response of the nanowires to axial strain and curvature, including strain-induced band-gap modulation in the tellurides. These results establish composition and mechanical deformation as complementary routes for tuning the electronic and mechanical properties of ultrathin transition-metal chalcogenide nanowires and support their further investigation as components of flexible nanoelectronic systems.

### Admittance extraction of quantum dot arrays for charge sensing
- **arXiv**: [2610.16590](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16590)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16590.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Admittance extraction of quantum dot arrays for charge sensing
- **Authors**: Conor Power, Mathieu Moras, Andrii Sokolov, Robert Bogdan Staszewski, Elena Blokhina
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 用速率方程框架对双量子点阵列进行时变扩展，模拟反射计读出并提取导纳，与实验测量的反射计数据高度吻合。

This work demonstrates admittance extraction of quantum dot arrays using a rate equation frame- work matching experimental reflectometry measurements. Using a semiclassical rate equation, we consider a perturbation of the source/drain chemical potential to model reflectometry readout in a quantum dot array. This is a time-dependent extension of the rate equation which has been employed in literature for pumped arrays of quantum dots and capacitance extractions. Here we extend this to a double quantum dot for admittance extraction. The simulations are calibrated us- ing Fermi-Hubbard parameters extracted from experimental data, which it is then shown to closely approximate to.

### Framework for an AI-Ready ARPES Community Database
- **arXiv**: [2610.15236](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15236)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15236.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Framework for an AI-Ready ARPES Community Database
- **Authors**: Zhi-Xun Shen, Jin Gene Wong, Karen Lei, Leon Garcia, Felix Baumberger, Sergey Borisenko, Aaron Bostwick, Ting Cao, Tanny Chavez, Yulin Chen, Monika Choudhary, Andrea Damascelli, Dan Dessau, Thomas P. Devereaux, Sandy A. Ekahana, Alexei Fedorov, Atsushi Fujimori, Yucheng Guo, M. Zahid Hasan, Makoto Hashimoto, Yu He, Philip Hofmann, Zahid Hussain, Harold Y. Hwang, Zhurun Ji, Chunjing Jia, Timur K. Kim, Phil D. C. King, Takeshi Kondo, Alessandra Lanzara, Chun Lin, Donghui Lu, Matteo Michiardi, Sung-Kwan Mo, Brian Moritz, Laurenz Rettig, Eli Rotenberg, Takafumi Sato, Kyle M. Shen, Jonathan A. Sobota, Inna Vishik, Shuolong Yang, Ming Yi, Timothy Zaklama
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.supr-con, cs.AI
- **TL;DR**: 该观点文章基于社区研讨会成果，提出为ARPES（角分辨光电子能谱）建立共享数据生态系统，以标准化、可检索、可引用的数据集替代以论文为中心的数据组织方式，并在近期、中期、长期三个时间尺度上分别支撑可复现分析、跨实验室自动化实验及量子物 foundation 模型。

Condensed matter stands to gain profoundly from widespread adoption of artificial intelligence (AI). However, its current means of data organization impedes this endeavor. As a premier measure of electronic structure underlying material properties, Angle-Resolved Photoemission Spectroscopy (ARPES) has accumulated over four decades of spectra, yet the community's publication-centered workflow preserves only a highly selected fraction of its experimental record. A shared data ecosystem could transform these fragmented measurements and their contexts into a persistent, collective scientific memory, creating immediate value for reproducibility and reuse while laying the foundation for AI-enabled discovery. In this Perspective, we report outcomes of a recent community workshop and propose a practical path toward such an ecosystem. Its benefits unfold along three timescales. In the near term, standardized, searchable, and citable datasets strengthen analysis pipelines independently of AI. In the intermediate future, community-validated tools operating across laboratories could enable broader reuse and increasingly closed-loop automated experiments. In the long term, a diverse corpus of measured electronic spectra could support foundation models for quantum matter. Looking forward, a diverse corpus of measured electronic spectra could support foundation models for quantum matter and new discoveries. We discuss lessons from existing scientific databases, the current state of AI in ARPES, as well as areas of community convergence on data organization, access, credit, governance, and a staged pilot program.

### Extending the mean first-passage-time formalism to the reconstruction of high free-energy barriers: A top-start approach
- **arXiv**: [2610.18653](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18653)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18653.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Extending the mean first-passage-time formalism to the reconstruction of high free-energy barriers: A top-start approach
- **Authors**: Ying Jiang, Ivan Saika-Voivod
- **Categories**: cond-mat.stat-mech, cond-mat.dis-nn, cond-mat.soft, physics.chem-ph
- **TL;DR**: 提出"top-start"（顶端起始）扩展方法，从势垒顶附近出发采样轨迹以重建自由能剖面，在高势垒（稀有事件）区域比传统MFPT方法高效且准确。

Rare events, such as crystal nucleation in mildly supercooled liquids, are characterized by waiting times that can be much longer than the timescale of the underlying microscopic dynamics. The mean first-passage time (MFPT) formalism [J. Wedekind and D. Reguera, J. Phys. Chem. B 112, 11060 (2008)] provides a route to reconstruct one-dimensional free-energy profiles from the MFPT and steady-state distribution measured along a reaction coordinate, but conventional implementations become inefficient for sufficiently high barriers because spontaneous crossings are rarely observed. We introduce a "top-start" extension that initiates trajectories near the barrier top and reconstructs the free-energy profile from their subsequent evolution away from the barrier toward lower-free-energy regions. We establish the equivalence between the top-start and two-region constructions using a Markov-chain transition-matrix formulation. We validate the method using one-dimensional random-walk simulations across low, medium, and high free-energy barriers, with transition-matrix calculations providing an exact benchmark for the corresponding discretized models. The top-start reconstruction remains accurate in the high-barrier regime where conventional MFPT sampling becomes prohibitively inefficient.

### Field Response of the Orbital Moment Probed by Integrated Magnetic Circular Dichroism
- **arXiv**: [2610.15684](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15684)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15684.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Field Response of the Orbital Moment Probed by Integrated Magnetic Circular Dichroism
- **Authors**: Qian-Sheng Shi, Ziqiang Wang, Jian Kang, Yi Zhang
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 该文定义了磁圆二色性（MCD）磁化率，用电磁U(1)规范不变方法推导多带体系的普遍表达式，并证明其与热力学轨道磁化率不同，是探测轨道磁响应的一种独立探针。

Magnetic circular dichroism (MCD) is an optical probe of orbital time reversal symmetry breaking. Although frequency integrated MCD is related to the self-rotation contribution to orbital magnetization at zero field, its magnetic response generally differs from the thermodynamic orbital susceptibility. Here, we define the MCD susceptibility and derive general expressions for multiband systems using an electromagnetic $U(1)$ gauge invariant approach. For insulators, the response contains contributions from the non-Abelian quantum metric, Berry curvature, and field induced interband mixing. For metals, a finite temperature Green function formulation separates the smooth response into Fermi sea and Fermi surface contributions. An independent Landau level calculation confirms the expressions for the zero-field MCD moment and its linear magnetic response. In a continuum model for twisted bilayer graphene, the MCD susceptibility vanishes within the charge neutrality gap, while the thermodynamic orbital susceptibility remains markedly diamagnetic. Near the gap edges, the orbital moment term dominates the response. Features near the van Hove energies are associated with density of states enhancement. These results clarify how the two susceptibilities reflect different orbital processes even for the same electronic structure and thus establish the MCD susceptibility as a distinct probe of orbital magnetic response.

### Tailored VxOy Metal--Insulator-Transition Oxides for Temperature-Triggered Smart Insulation in Superconducting Coils
- **arXiv**: [2610.16581](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16581)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16581.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Tailored VxOy Metal--Insulator-Transition Oxides for Temperature-Triggered Smart Insulation in Superconducting Coils
- **Authors**: Mattia Ortino, Davide Uglietti, Andrea Testino, Nicola Casati, Alexander Steppke, Wiktoria Wnetkowska, Andrea Masi, Kamil Sedlak
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con
- **TL;DR**: 本文探索钒氧体系以降低金属-绝缘体转变(MIT)温度，用于REBCO磁体的"智能绝缘"层；TGA氧化还原制备的多相粉末有效转变温度降至约127 K，比V2O3低约25 K，并建立了电-热模型。

Smart insulation is a turn-to-turn layer that insulates in normal operation and conducts only near a hot spot. It could combine the fast ramping of insulated coils with the passive energy dump of non-insulated coils. Metal-insulator transition (MIT) oxides are natural candidates, but the only one tested in a coil so far, V2O3, switches at 150-170 K, far above the 30-70 K window useful for REBCO magnets. We explore the vanadium-oxygen system to lower the transition. TGA redox processing produced multi-phase powders with an effective transition near 127 K, about 25 K below V2O3. Hydrothermal V3O7, hydrated or annealed, was far too resistive and showed no usable transition. V2O3-epoxy specimens provided a robust reference and an acceptance criterion. Cryogenic measurements revealed a reproducible thermal hysteresis, and the measured specific heat of V2O3 corrects earlier model inputs by close to two orders of magnitude. A first electro-thermal model reproduces current sharing through the smart-insulation layer. Doped V2O3 and V8O15 are identified as the next targets.

### Single-electron Fluorine Bonding. I. The SEFB-15 and CSFB-14 Benchmark Datasets, Reference Binding Energies and Intermolecular Bond Distances: Assessment of M06-2X density functional
- **arXiv**: [2610.16629](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16629)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16629.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Single-electron Fluorine Bonding. I. The SEFB-15 and CSFB-14 Benchmark Datasets, Reference Binding Energies and Intermolecular Bond Distances: Assessment of M06-2X density functional
- **Authors**: Govinda Prasad Khanal, Elangannan Arunan
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph
- **TL;DR**: 本文构建了单电子氟键(SEFB-15)与闭壳氟键(CSFB-14)两个数据集，以DF-RMP2-F12/CBS为参考并对照CCSD(T)等验证，系统评估M06-2X表现。

Halogen bonding in which a covalently bound fluorine acts as halogen-bond donor (F-bonding) is rare, and its open-shell variant with radical electron donors remains unexplored. We introduce SEFB-15 dataset (15 open-shell DF$\cdots$CH$_3$ complexes) exemplifying single-electron F-bonding (SEFB) and CSFB-14 dataset (14 closed-shell DF$\cdots$NH$_3$ complexes) representing closed-shell F-bonding (CSFB). Reference binding energies (BEs) at DF-RMP2-F12/CBS//DF-RMP2-F12/VDZ-F12 are validated against CCSD(T), CCSD(T)-F12b, W1-F12 and W2-F12 for selected systems (DF = F$_2$, NCF, CNF, NH$_3$F$^+$ and NH$_2$F$_2^+$), with MA%D of 4.5% (DF$\cdots$CH$_3$) and 3.4% (DF$\cdots$NH$_3$) against CCSD(T)-F12b/CBS//CCSD(T)-F12b/VDZ-F12. BEs from W1-F12 and W2-F12 are identical. Reference F-bond distances at DF-RMP2-F12/VDZ-F12 are validated against CCSD(T)-F12b, CCSD(T), CCSD, and DCSD for the same systems, with MA%D of 0.9% (SEFB) and 0.4% (CSFB) against CCSD(T)-F12b/VDZ-F12. M06-2X is recommended for closed-shell halogen bonding with heavier halogens but has never been assessed for open-shell F-bonding. M06-2X/AVTZ systematically overbinds all SEFB-15 complexes and underestimates their F-bond distances, with MA%Ds of 75.8% on BEs and 8.9% on F-bond distances. M06-2X predicts comparable BEs for F$_2\cdots$CH$_3$ and F$_2\cdots$NH$_3$, contrary to all ab initio results, an anomaly persisting across all 16 basis sets. Neither basis set nor BSSE correction resolves the overbinding. BSSE correction worsens BEs for DF$\cdots$NH$_3$ complexes. For CSFB-14, M06-2X/AVTZ yields MA%Ds of 16.0% for BEs and 3.3% for F-bond distances. M06-2X is reliable for CSFB but unreliable for SEFB regardless of basis set. Companion paper II reports performance of other Minnesota functionals and ab initio methods for these datasets.

### FDTD Model of Noise Pollution from an AI Data Center
- **arXiv**: [2610.14899](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14899)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14899.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: FDTD Model of Noise Pollution from an AI Data Center
- **Authors**: Gavin Buxton
- **Categories**: physics.comp-ph, cs.LG
- **TL;DR**: 利用FDTD模型模拟AI数据中心周边的低频噪声传播，揭示地形对噪声污染分布的关键作用。

The sudden growth in AI data centers is raising numerous environmental concerns. Low-frequency noise pollution is a concern for local residents, that may not just be an annoyance but also carry significant health impacts. An FDTD model of sound propagation over the topography near an AI data center is presented. The topography plays a large role in the level of noise pollution, with areas of sudden elevation on either side of the river near the AI data center more susceptible to high noise levels, while areas further afield largely protected from noise pollution by the topography. Understanding the role that topography plays in noise pollution, and capturing the noise pollution in specific topographies, can help identify areas where noise mitigation strategies might be best employed.

### Three-dimensional reconstruction of weakly scattering objects from single-defocus 4D-STEM data
- **arXiv**: [2610.15303](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15303)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15303.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Three-dimensional reconstruction of weakly scattering objects from single-defocus 4D-STEM data
- **Authors**: Kousuke Ooe, Yufan Shen, Yuichi Shimakawa, Daisuke Kan, Scott D. Findlay
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, physics.optics
- **TL;DR**: 提出3D线性反演(3D LINV)STEM算法，可从单离焦4D-STEM数据快速直接重建三维原子结构。

Three-dimensional (3D) atomic-structure analysis is of central importance in (scanning) transmission electron microscopy (STEM). STEM techniques to reconstruct 3D structure include tilt-series or defocus-series annular dark-field, best suited to heavier atomic species, and dynamical-scattering-model inversion applied to single-defocus four-dimensional (4D) STEM data, requiring computationally expensive post-experiment analysis. In this study, we propose a 3D-structure linear inversion (3D LINV) STEM algorithm, which enables a fast and direct reconstruction of 3D structures from a single-defocus 4D-STEM dataset. This method is based on solving linear equations relating the measured 4D-STEM data and the unknown 3D specimen potential under the thick weak phase object approximation. We show the capabilities of 3D LINV STEM through systematic 4D-STEM simulations across various observation parameters such as specimen thickness, partial coherence, and shot noise, and through an experimental demonstration of 3D structure reconstruction of a stacked freestanding membrane with atomic-scale lateral resolution and nanometre-scale depth resolution.

### Nontrivial topological properties from the cooperation of static perturbations and Floquet fields in type-II semi-Dirac semimetals
- **arXiv**: [2610.16659](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16659)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16659.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Nontrivial topological properties from the cooperation of static perturbations and Floquet fields in type-II semi-Dirac semimetals
- **Authors**: Wen-Hao Bian, Jing Wang
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 本文理论研究了第二类自旋简并半导体(SDSM)在静质量扰动、双频正交偏振光(TFOPL)驱动及其混合情形下的拓扑与反常输运性质，揭示了光可调的能隙闭合点、双峰型Berry曲率偶极以及量子化AHC/ATHC等特征。

In this work, we present a comprehensive theoretical study for the topological and anomalous transport properties of type-II SDSMs under three scenarios, i.e., static symmetry-breaking perturbations, periodic Floquet driving by two-frequency orthogonally polarized light (TFOPL), and their cooperative effects in the hybrid scenario. We first show that a static mass $m_z$ opens a bulk gap and drives the system into a Chern insulator with Chern number $C^{\rm per}=\pm 2\,{\rm sgn}(m_z)$. In comparison, TFOPL alone only shifts the positions of Dirac points and cannot open a full gap. Nevertheless, it produces a Berry curvature odd in the $x$-direction, yielding a finite Berry curvature dipole (BCD) along $x$ proportional to the sine of the initial phase, while the $y$-component vanishes. The hybrid scenario, by contrast, exhibits a remarkably rich phenomenology. Specifically, its gap-closure criterion becomes cubic and thus admits up to three real roots when the effective parameter along the $x$-direction is negative, thereby producing multiple light-tunable gap-closing points and a series of topological phase transitions. In particular, the BCD exhibits a double-peak structure with strong enhancement near gap-closing manifolds and synchronized sign reversal of the full vector under external tuning. We further analyze the anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC), anomalous Nernst conductivity (ANC), and anomalous thermal Hall conductivity (ATHC). The AHC and ATHC exhibit quantized plateaus that serve as signatures of Chern-insulator phases, while the ANC, being a Fermi-surface probe, provides complementary local information and can identify light-induced gaps even when global topological invariants remain unchanged. Taken together, these results offer useful insights into the topological properties of 2D type-II SDSMs and related systems.

### Programmable memristive circuits for reconfigurable collective pseudomagnetic ordering, transport, and learning
- **arXiv**: [2610.15346](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15346)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15346.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Programmable memristive circuits for reconfigurable collective pseudomagnetic ordering, transport, and learning
- **Authors**: Sichang Qiu, Yuxin Jiang, Yongchao Wu, Peng Han, Xinxin Gao, Zekun Huang, Ce Shang, Ronny Thomale, Tie Jun Cui, Shuo Liu
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cond-mat.other
- **TL;DR**: 该论文用相同忆阻单元通过可编程连接构建模块化电路，把器件局部状态组织成可重配置的集体序（铁磁/反铁磁/交错磁型对称），并实现对称依赖的输运和储备池计算。

Programmable hardware typically uses fixed architectures, whereas complex physical systems obtain diverse behavior by connecting reusable local elements through different interaction rules. Memristive devices combine writable states with nonlinear dynamics, but translating these local properties into reconfigurable collective order and symmetry-governed transport remains challenging. Here we develop modular circuits configured through programmable connections between identical memristive units. Experiments establish binary conductance states as Ising-type pseudospins and show that signed feedback between units selects uniform or staggered pseudospin order. Circuit-model simulations then examine controllable domain-wall motion and pattern recovery. A symmetry encoder combines measured conductance patterns with sublattice labels and a selected symmetry rule to configure a fixed transport layer, implementing circuit analogs of ferromagnetic, antiferromagnetic, and altermagnetic transport symmetries. Although the antiferromagnetic and altermagnetic encodings share a compensated pattern, their distinct voltage spreading illustrates how programmed circuit symmetry affects transport. Device-calibrated simulations show that memristive dynamics generates history-dependent features for reservoir computing. By separating local memory from programmable interactions, symmetry encoding and task-specific readout, this architecture provides a modular route from device dynamics to collective order, symmetry-dependent transport, and learning.

### Emergent Loop Geometry in Gauge-Equivariant Graph Neural Networks
- **arXiv**: [2610.18742](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18742)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18742.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Emergent Loop Geometry in Gauge-Equivariant Graph Neural Networks
- **Authors**: Ali Rayat, Gia-Wei Chern
- **Categories**: cond-mat.stat-mech, cond-mat.str-el, cs.LG, hep-lat
- **TL;DR**: 规范等变图神经网络通过局部消息传递生成规范链的有序乘积，但仅凭感受野无法刻画这些内部结构的几何。本文研究递归节点/边特征操作产生的闭合格点游走统计系综，发现轮廓长度快速增长而空间扩展缓慢，深度主要丰富局部表示而非扩展范围。

Gauge-equivariant graph neural networks generate ordered products of gauge links through local message passing, but the geometry of these internal structures is not captured by the receptive field alone. We investigate the statistical ensemble of closed lattice walks generated by recursive node- and edge-feature operations, independently of network training. Analytical and numerical results reveal a separation between rapid growth of contour length and much slower spatial expansion: deeper compositions generate increasingly long paths through repeated traversal of relatively compact regions. Loop expansions of fermionic observables provide a physical interpretation of this hierarchy, showing how spatial locality remains compatible with high-order dependence on nearby gauge fields. These findings identify complementary roles of network depth in extending spatial reach and enriching the representations constructed within that reach, providing a geometrical perspective on learning lattice gauge observables.

### Variational Minimization of Almost Strong Zero Modes in Parafermionic Wires
- **arXiv**: [2610.15748](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15748)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15748.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Variational Minimization of Almost Strong Zero Modes in Parafermionic Wires
- **Authors**: Niklas Tausendpfund, Matteo Rizzi
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, quant-ph
- **TL;DR**: 提出一种量子启发的变分算法，用于在一般parafermionic链模型中搜索几乎强零模（almost strong zero modes），并在非可积模型中找到强束缚的parafermionic算符。

Strongly interacting fermionic chains obeying a $Z_n$ symmetry can host topological phases with highly degenerate ground-state manifolds. The different ground states are connected by strongly bound fractionalized edge modes called parafermionic zero modes. While exactly preserved under time evolution at zero temperature, interactions lead to a destabilization of these modes at finite energy densities, and, given a generic Hamiltonian, obtaining the lifetime of these modes is often impossible as it requires knowledge of the full spectrum of the Hamiltonian or long time evolutions. Here, we propose a novel quantum-inspired algorithm that can be used to variationally search for these almost strong zero modes in generic parafermionic chain models. We demonstrate analytically and numerically that our ansatz faithfully reproduces the exact results and finds strongly bound parafermionic operators in the non-integrable Kitaev-Hubbard chain and chiral Potts model. Our results further allow us to verify the existence of a true strong zero mode in the symmetrical limit of the chiral Potts model for $Z_3$-parafermions.

### Microscopic Modeling of Interlayer Tunneling in Twisted Bilayer Cuprates
- **arXiv**: [2610.16930](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16930)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16930.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Microscopic Modeling of Interlayer Tunneling in Twisted Bilayer Cuprates
- **Authors**: Siddhant Panda, Andreas Kreisel, Laura Fanfarillo, P. J. Hirschfeld
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con, cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 通过真实轨道重叠计算层间隧穿，构建更精确的扭转铜氧化物微观模型，发现TRSB d+id'区域缩小、45°附近约瑟夫森临界电流被强烈抑制。

The field of twistronics, recently extended to twisted bilayer cuprate systems, has generated considerable interest due to the prediction of $d+id'$ states close to a $45^\circ$ twist, as suggested by multiple experimental probes. Theoretical studies of these systems using a variety of models and many-body theoretical techniques have revealed multiple competing order parameters. The large variability in experimental results and theoretical predictions demands an accurate microscopic model for these systems, in particular a more realistic description of the interlayer tunneling. In this study, we fit the in-plane hopping and compute the interlayer tunneling amplitudes by calculating the orbital overlap between $d_{x^2-y^2}$ wave functions at each site of the twisted square-lattice model. We observe sign-changing tunneling amplitudes, a strong twist-angle dependence of the direct tunneling term, and long-range tunneling amplitudes that often dominate over direct tunneling. We investigate the band structure, Fermi surfaces, and spectral functions in comparison with those obtained from the phenomenological model and find a substantial redistribution of spectral weight that modifies the competition between different superconducting ground states. We determine the phase diagram as a function of twist angle, filling, and direct tunneling strength, which is controlled by the $c$-axis distance between the monolayers, and observe a reduction in the region of time-reversal-symmetry-breaking (TRSB) $d+id'$ states compared with the phenomenological model. Finally, we investigate the Josephson critical current across the phase diagram and find strongly suppressed critical currents close to a $45^\circ$ twist, consistent with experimental observations.

### Exact Gaussian--Hermite closure of the mapping quantum--classical Liouville equation
- **arXiv**: [2610.16864](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16864)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16864.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Exact Gaussian--Hermite closure of the mapping quantum--classical Liouville equation
- **Authors**: Sahand Nikzat Ali Nassimi, Rony Modak Parai
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph, physics.comp-ph, quant-ph
- **TL;DR**: 在Hermite函数基（以共同高斯因子展开）下，Meyer–Miller–Stock–Thoss映射表象中的过量耦合项纯为升算符，恰好抵消反作用力产生的升贡献，从而精确动力学在有限稀疏方程上闭合，物理子空间内退化为子系统量子–经典Liouville方程；PBME因保留升贡献而持续产生高阶项并有偏差。

In the Meyer--Miller--Stock--Thoss mapping representation, the Liouville operator of the quantum--classical Liouville equation contains an excess coupling term in addition to the Poisson bracket. Neglecting this term yields the Poisson bracket mapping equation (PBME). We expand the mapping dependence of the density in Hermite functions about the Gaussian factor common to all physical mapping densities. In this basis, the excess coupling term acts purely as a raising operator and exactly cancels the raising contribution generated by the back--reaction force. Consequently, the exact dynamics does not generate higher Hermite orders and closes on a finite, sparse system of equations for the coefficient fields. Within the physical sector, these equations reduce to the subsystem quantum--classical Liouville equation. In contrast, the PBME retains the raising contribution of the force, modifying the back reaction within the physical sector while continuously generating higher Hermite orders. For Tully's dual avoided crossing model, direct propagation of the coefficient equations confirms this finite closure and agrees with independent solutions of the subsystem equation. Comparison with converged full quantum dynamics further shows that, across all regimes examined, the PBME is less accurate than the quantum--classical description for populations, coherences, and momentum distributions.

### A quantum algorithm for meshless lattice Boltzmann method with unitary interpolation
- **arXiv**: [2610.14943](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14943)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.14943.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: A quantum algorithm for meshless lattice Boltzmann method with unitary interpolation
- **Authors**: Chi Zhang, Hefeng Wang, Hua Xiang
- **Categories**: physics.comp-ph, math.NA
- **TL;DR**: 提出质量加权的无网格移位不可压LBM公式，使插值流步精确酉化，并结合Carleman嵌入实现量子友好的块编码。

We present a mass-weighted meshless formulation for the shifted incompressible lattice Boltzmann method (LBM) whose streaming step is exactly unitary, combining with the Carleman embedding. In traditional meshless LBM, on a scattered node set the semi-Lagrangian streaming step is an interpolation, which is not unitary; a generic block encoding of this matrix therefore requires a normalization proportional to its sparsity and logarithmically many ancillas. To overcome the non-unitarity, we instead express the interpolation as the exponential exp(a Dx + b Dy) of mimetic derivative operators. Endowing each node with its Voronoi mass and applying a mass-weighted similarity transform renders these derivative operators skew-Hermitian by enforcing moment constraints together with low-wave number trigonometric differentiation conditions, so that the interpolation becomes exactly unitary. Such unitarization of interpolation leave the local collision step unchanged up to a rescaling of its quadratic term, where the same weights define the amplitude encoding of the distribution functions. For quasi-uniform node sets, the mass-weighting diagonal remains within a unit-order normalization that degrades only mildly as the Carleman truncation order grows, thereby mitigating the cost of block encoding. Numerical experiments on the Taylor Green vortex confirm that the method reproduces the full meshless LBM, with the velocity error decreasing as the Carleman order increases.

### Enhancement of local magnetic ordering temperature due to non-magnetic disorder in anti-Heusler alloy Al$_2$MnCu
- **arXiv**: [2610.15622](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15622)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15622.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Enhancement of local magnetic ordering temperature due to non-magnetic disorder in anti-Heusler alloy Al$_2$MnCu
- **Authors**: Soumya Bhowmik, Suvayan Saha, Saikat Debnath, Arka Patra, Biswarup Satpati, Maxim Avdeev, Shishir Kumar Pandey, Chandan Mazumdar
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 本文以反Heusler合金Al2MnCu为平台，区分磁性与非磁性原子无序对磁性的不同作用，发现大比例磁性Mn位无序对Tc影响甚微，而少量非磁性无序却通过增强交换作用提升局域磁有序温度。

Structural disorder is generally regarded as detrimental to magnetism. Since magnetic properties are governed by both the arrangement of magnetic atoms and the crystal environment, including non-magnetic atoms that mediate exchange interactions, the distinct role of magnetic and non-magnetic disorder remains less understood. The recently reported anti-Heusler alloy, Al$_2$MnCu, forming with $\sim$50\% intra-symmetry site disorder (B2) involving the magnetic Mn atom and $\sim$5\% inter-symmetry site disorder associated with non-magnetic Al and Cu atoms, offers a good platform to disentangle the individual roles of magnetic and non-magnetic atomic disorder. Our magnetic critical analysis, in the vicinity of the Curie temperature ($T_{\rm C}$), does not lead to any conventional single universality class expected for a standard ferromagnet; instead, the non-monotonic temperature dependence of effective critical exponents points towards more complex magnetic interactions, akin to inhomogeneous ferromagnets. Dc magnetometry complemented by higher-harmonic ac magnetic susceptibility data, further confirms the persistence of locally ordered magnetic clusters above $T_{\rm C}$. Theoretical analysis establishes that the enhancement of the local magnetic ordering temperature arises directly from the strengthening of exchange interactions due to the small degree of non-magnetic disorder, whereas $T_{\rm C}$ is hardly influenced by the much larger level of magnetic-atom disorder. The combined experimental and theoretical investigations on the anti-Heusler alloy Al$_2$MnCu reveal and explain the unusual observation of nonmagnetic disorder-induced elevation of $T_{\rm C}$, providing a paradigm for the counterintuitive role of disorder in magnetic materials.

### 1/f frequency-noise reduction in nanomechanical resonators by dissipation dilution
- **arXiv**: [2610.16711](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16711)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16711.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: 1/f frequency-noise reduction in nanomechanical resonators by dissipation dilution
- **Authors**: Kostas Kanellopulos, Silvan Schmid
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, physics.app-ph, physics.ins-det
- **TL;DR**: 该文指出纳米机械谐振器1/f频率噪声底的来源是张力的热噪声（涨落-耗散定理），并给出无量纲闪烁系数公式，实验验证并解释了温度依赖极限。

The frequency noise of a resonant nanomechanical sensor is well understood down to its thermomechanical limit, but the longest useful averaging time is set by a flicker, or $1/f$, frequency-noise floor whose origin has remained unknown. We show that this floor is the thermal noise of the tension. By the fluctuation-dissipation theorem, the tension of a resonator with fixed ends fluctuates by an amount set by the loss angle of its material; for structural damping, these fluctuations produce $1/f$ frequency noise with a flicker coefficient $h_{-1}=E k_{\mathrm{B}} T/(2πVσ_0^2 Q_{\mathrm{el}})$, with $Q_{\mathrm{el}}$ the intrinsic loss under elongation, that contains no free parameter and is diluted by the tensile stress $σ_0$ in the same way as damping is in dissipation dilution. Silicon nitride strings and membranes from 30 to 340~nm thickness follow the predicted volume scaling, the predicted $σ_0^{-2}$ dilution across resonators and within stress-engineered strings, and the predicted thickness dependence, which places noise-active two-level systems in the volume and at the surfaces of the resonators, all at the absolute level set by the independently measured intrinsic loss. The same relation accounts for published data between 78 and 296~K; below 78~K, the loss measured at the resonance no longer represents the loss probed by the noise, marking the limit of the structural-damping assumption. Dissipation dilution therefore lowers the $1/f$ floor, and the longest useful integration time of a resonant sensor is set by its prestress, volume, temperature, and intrinsic loss angle.

### Hidden Spin-Valley Hall Effect and Gate-Switchable Electric Hall Effect in 2D Altermagnets
- **arXiv**: [2610.15630](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15630)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15630.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Hidden Spin-Valley Hall Effect and Gate-Switchable Electric Hall Effect in 2D Altermagnets
- **Authors**: Jiajun Lu, Mu Tian, Chaoxi Cui, Zhi-Ming Yu, Yugui Yao, Run-Wu Zhang
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 在具有自旋-谷-层耦合(SVLC)的二维交变磁体中，栅极电场可解除谷间层极化补偿，将隐藏的自旋-谷霍尔响应转换为可开关的净电荷霍尔信号，并伴随符号可翻转的磁光克尔效应。

Hidden beneath the vanishing charge Hall response of altermagnets are spin-up and spin-down Hall currents of opposite sign---concealed, not absent---whose cancellation is enforced by symmetry. When these spin states are locked to distinct valleys, the hidden currents constitute a spin-valley Hall effect. Here we show that in 2D altermagnets with spin-valley-layer coupling (SVLC), a gate field unveils this hidden spin-valley Hall response.~By lifting the symmetry-enforced compensation between valleys with opposite layer polarizations, the field converts the hidden response into a net charge Hall signal, yielding a gate-switchable electric Hall effect (EHE) enabled by SVLC. Using collinear spin layer group (cSLG) theory, we classify all 158 altermagnetic cSLGs, identify 32 that support SVLC, and predict monolayer Mg$_2$Os$_2$(SO$_5$)$_2$ and the Ca(CoN)$_2$-family as candidate materials. First-principles calculations for Mg$_2$Os$_2$(SO$_5$)$_2$ reveal reversible valley splitting, a gate-switchable Hall response, and a Kerr rotation exceeding 1 mrad whose sign reverses together with the EHE signal. These results establish a symmetry-based route to all-electrical valley-spin control and identify altermagnets as promising platforms for high-performance valley transport and electrically switchable magneto-optics.

### Towards synergy between theory and experiments: First-passage processes under resetting with stochastic return protocols
- **arXiv**: [2610.18814](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18814)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18814.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Towards synergy between theory and experiments: First-passage processes under resetting with stochastic return protocols
- **Authors**: Ashutosh Dubey, Arnab Pal
- **Categories**: cond-mat.stat-mech
- **TL;DR**: 研究一维谐振势中带有限时间返回的随机重置搜索问题，解析求出平均首达时间并绘制相图，发现搜索阶段势强度大、返回阶段势强度小时 SR 最优于 IR。

Stochastic resetting with stochastic return (SR) has recently emerged as an effective and practically relevant strategy for controlling first-passage phenomena and optimizing search processes by significantly reducing completion times in a wide class of stochastic systems. While most theoretical studies assume instantaneous return (IR), in realistic systems the return to the resetting position typically occurs over a finite time. We consider a 1-d diffusing particle in a harmonic trap with stiffnesses $k_1$ and $k_2$ during search and return, respectively. The particle searches for the target and, upon resetting at rate $r$, enters the return phase, continuing until it reaches either the origin or the absorbing target at $L&gt;0$. Notably, the model permits target detection throughout the return phase, distinguishing it from IR scenarios. We analytically derive the MFPT for our model and determine parameter regions where SR enhances or suppresses search efficiency over the underlying dynamics, constructing the corresponding phase diagram using the coefficient of variation criterion. We further identify regimes in which SR is more efficient than IR using the SR criterion. This behavior is tested across different parameter sets taken from experimental studies by Goerlich et al., Biroli et al., and Besga et al. We map the parameter values reported in existing experimental studies onto our theoretical phase diagram, thereby identifying the regimes in which SR outperforms IR. In particular, we find that the most relevant region of the phase space, where SR is most beneficial corresponds to larger values of the potential strength in the search phase and lower values of potential in the return phase. Our results provide experimentally testable predictions and bridge the gap between theoretical resetting models and optical trap mediated finite-time return dynamics.

### Magpie: A Graphical Magnon Software
- **arXiv**: [2610.15790](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15790)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15790.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Magpie: A Graphical Magnon Software
- **Authors**: Tobias Weber
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, physics.comp-ph
- **TL;DR**: Magpie 是一款完全图形化的线性自旋波软件，聚焦磁性模型与磁振子色散关系的三维可视化，开源、C++ 编写、模块化，可与外部自旋波库互操作。

Magpie is a fully graphical linear spin-wave software which has its main focus on 3-d visualisation of magnetic models and magnon dispersion relations. The software is distributed under an open-source license and is written in high-performance, portable C++. Its modular architecture allows for a rapid development of new calculation modules and permits the inclusion into external applications. Included code generators ensure seamless interoperability with other linear spin-wave libraries.

### High-Efficiency Field-Free Superconducting Diodes in Mo$_2$N/FePt Bilayers
- **arXiv**: [2610.16972](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16972)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16972.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: High-Efficiency Field-Free Superconducting Diodes in Mo$_2$N/FePt Bilayers
- **Authors**: Juan S. Cruz, Emilio De Biasi, Juan C. Zapata, Yeonkyu Lee, Jeehoon Kim, Nestor Haberkorn
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con, cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 在室温生长的Mo2N/FePt双层中实现了近理想的、无需外场的超导二极管效应，效率达约85%，机制为FePt铁磁条纹畴诱导的边缘控制磁通进入与涡旋运动不对称。

Near-ideal, field-free superconducting diode behavior is demonstrated in room-temperature-grown Mo$_2$N/FePt bilayers. The response is consistent with remanent FePt stripe domains producing polarity-dependent thresholds for flux entry and vortex motion. Micromagnetic simulations reveal a periodic stray-field modulation across the stripes and asymmetric field enhancement near their ends at the bridge edges. One current polarity remains nearly dissipationless up to a sizable fraction of the estimated depairing current, whereas the opposite polarity develops dissipation at much lower current, yielding diode efficiencies up to approximately 85% in the thinner bilayer. The nonreciprocity can be continuously tuned by the in-plane magnetic preparation angle and reversibly switched by reverse-field excursions through the hysteretic reversal of the FePt layer. These results support edge-controlled flux entry and vortex motion as a route to high-efficiency superconducting diodes.

### Solvent Fluctuations Enable Photoionization of Aqueous Nitrite Microdroplets
- **arXiv**: [2610.17021](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17021)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17021.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Solvent Fluctuations Enable Photoionization of Aqueous Nitrite Microdroplets
- **Authors**: Brandon J. Wallace, Eric R. Heller, Alex Prophet, Pranav Ballapuram, Ashok Ajoy, David T. Limmer, Kevin R. Wilson
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph, cond-mat.stat-mech
- **TL;DR**: 研究揭示水相亚硝酸盐在可见光下可通过直接光离子化产生溶剂化电子，这一被忽视的通道是大气气溶胶和天然水体中氮氧化物与氧化剂的重要来源。

Nitrite photochemistry in atmospheric aerosols and natural waters is a major source of hydroxyl radicals and reactive nitrogen oxides. Contrary to the prevailing view that in the environment aqueous nitrite photochemistry proceeds exclusively through photoexcitation followed by homolytic bond cleavage, we show that visible light directly ionizes aqueous nitrite in individual micron-sized droplets. Although the photoionization cross section is exceptionally small, this pathway is observable at the high photon flux densities accessible in single-droplet experiments. Molecular simulations reveal that this is because photoionization absorption has an exceptionally long wavelength tail due to the large solvent reorganization energy in aqueous solutions. Kinetic modeling of an aqueous-phase reaction mechanism quantitatively accounts for the observed chemistry and predicts the formation of nitrate, hydroxyl radicals, and solvated electrons. The overlap of the predicted broad photoionization continuum with the visible solar spectrum further suggests that visible-light photoionization is an overlooked source of nitrogen oxides, solvated electrons, and oxidants in atmospheric aerosols, snowpack, and natural waters. Under dilute conditions, where dissolved oxygen is the main sink for solvated electrons, photoionization is expected to be the dominant photochemical loss pathway for nitrite.

### Self-Adaptive Model-Order Reduction for Shock-Dominated Problems
- **arXiv**: [2610.15944](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15944)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15944.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Self-Adaptive Model-Order Reduction for Shock-Dominated Problems
- **Authors**: Ali Mohaghegh, Cheng Huang
- **Categories**: physics.comp-ph
- **TL;DR**: 提出一种自适应模型降阶(MOR)框架，依据解的演化特征在线动态选择自适应或静态ROM公式，以在保持精度同时优化计算效率，并在旋转爆震发动机(RDE)上验证。

Projection-based model-order reduction (MOR) offers a promising approach for accelerating expensive full-order model (FOM) by constructing reduced-order models (ROMs) that retain the majority of FOM accuracy while significantly reducing the computational cost. However, conventional static ROMs, often following offline/online training strategy, can exhibit limited predictive capability for convection-dominated problems featuring strongly evolving physics such as shocks. Adaptive ROMs emerge as a promising alternative and address such limitation by dynamically updating the low-dimensional subspace during online simulation to track locally evolving system dynamics, thereby enabling accurate predictions even for shock-dominated flows. However, such online adaptation introduces additional computational overhead, and frequent basis and sampling-point updates can substantially reduce their computational advantage relative to their static counterpart. To reconcile this tradeoff, the current work introduces a self-adaptive MOR framework that dynamically constructs and deploys the most appropriate ROM formulation (either adaptive or static) on the fly according to the evolving characteristics of the solution. to achieve optimal computational efficiency while maintaining high modeling accuracy of the local physics during the online model execution. In this regard, the proposed framework seeks to exploit the advantages of both adaptive- and static-ROM approaches to achieve an optimal computational efficiency and predictive accuracy while reducing reliance on extensive offline training. The framework is developed and evaluated for modeling rotating detonation engines (RDEs), which provide a challenging test environment involving strongly convection-dominated, transient, and multiscale reacting-flow dynamics.

### Indirect prediction and screening of frustrated magnetic materials using machine learning
- **arXiv**: [2610.15683](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15683)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15683.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Indirect prediction and screening of frustrated magnetic materials using machine learning
- **Authors**: Regina Burganova, Irina Piyanzina, Ruslan Akhmerov, Alexander Sigov, Maxim Fedorov
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该文提出一种机器学习工作流，通过分别预测居里-魏斯温度与奈尔温度来间接预测磁阻挫指数，并从约6.7万种Materials Project化合物中筛选出20个高置信度阻挫磁性候选材料。

Magnetic frustration is commonly quantified by the frustration index, calculated from the Curie-Weiss and Neel temperatures. Because both quantities are not simultaneously available for most materials, direct prediction of the magnetic frustration index remains challenging. Here, we propose a machine learning workflow for indirect prediction of the frustration index and consequent screening of potential frustrated magnetic compounds through independent prediction of Curie-Weiss and Neel temperatures. In our work, composition-based models implemented via the CatBoost algorithm, trained on experimental data were optimized using chemically informed dataset subdivision. The highest predictive accuracy was achieved for the rare earth-oxide model in predicting Neel and for the rare earth model in predicting Curie-Weiss. Ensemble uncertainty was incorporated through a conservative frustration index to improve the reliability of candidate selection. Screening of approximately 67000 Materials Project compounds identified 20 high-confidence frustrated magnetic candidates, including several experimentally known frustrated magnets and previously unexplored oxide systems potential for further investigation.

### Emergent Spinorial Structure in Mesoscopic Irreversible Thermodynamics
- **arXiv**: [2610.16738](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16738)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16738.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Emergent Spinorial Structure in Mesoscopic Irreversible Thermodynamics
- **Authors**: Giorgio SONNINO
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 本文在一个介观正则模型中，由输运系数重构热力学坐标的几何结构，建立输运张量、演化生成元与谱距离（度量）之间的联系，并用Bi₂Te₃薄膜数据给出数值示例。

We reconstruct a geometry of thermodynamic coordinates from transport coefficients within a mesoscopic canonical model. Self-adjoint flux observables determine a compatible evolution generator containing symmetric and antisymmetric transport coefficients. When the Hermitian transport tensor is positive, an exact graded first-order factorization reproduces this generator on its scalar sector. Its commutators define a spectral distance whose metric depends only on the symmetric tensor. Gradients of the antisymmetric tensor can change the evolution while leaving the metric unchanged. The construction allows coordinate-dependent coefficients. In the constant Onsager regime, a Clifford representation gives the same distance and an algebra fixed by the transport matrix. Published transport data for a $\mathrm{Bi_2Te_3}$ film give a principal-distance ratio of approximately $2.12$ in normalized coordinates at $ZT\simeq0.68$. We also examine a separate quadratic entropy-production observable and distinguish its discrete spectrum from the spectrum of the evolution generator. The spinorial representation adds no physical spin degrees of freedom.

### Reversible intrinsic-extrinsic crossover of the anomalous Hall effect in PrAlSi via Al/Si ratio tuning
- **arXiv**: [2610.15726](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15726)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15726.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Reversible intrinsic-extrinsic crossover of the anomalous Hall effect in PrAlSi via Al/Si ratio tuning
- **Authors**: Huiyang Yang, Xiaohua Luo, Hongwei You, Wei Ren, Yuntao Gao, Changcai Chen, Zhishuo Zhang, Chunsheng Fang, Shengcan Ma
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 通过偏离化学计量比调控PrAlSi中Al/Si比例，实现了反常霍尔效应从内禀到外禀再到内禀机制的可逆转变，中间组分获得1413 Ω⁻¹cm⁻¹的巨反常霍尔电导。

The anomalous Hall effect (AHE) in magnetic Weyl semimetals arises from distinct microscopic origins-intrinsic Berry curvature and extrinsic scattering mechanisms-yet reversible switching between these regimes within a single material platform remains elusive. Here, we demonstrate that off-stoichiometric tuning of the Al/Si ratio (from ~1.05 to ~0.75) reversibly drives an intrinsic-extrinsic-intrinsic crossover of the AHE in PrAlSi single crystals. Across four compositions spanning a narrow Si variation (PrAl0.93Si0.89 to PrAl0.79Si1.06), the anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) exhibits a non monotonic arch (170-1413- 507-164 Ω-1cm-1). The intermediate composition PrAl0.90Si0.94 (Al/Si ~0.96) exhibits high carrier mobility, multi carrier transport, yielding a giant AHC of 1413 Ω-1cm-1 dominated by extrinsic skew-scattering. The Si-poor and Si-rich compositions exhibit lower AHC values and intrinsic Berry-curvature-dominated AHE, establishing a composition-driven intrinsic-extrinsic-intrinsic crossover. Deviating from the intermediate composition suppresses mobility and reverses carrier type from hole-like to electron-like. The Al/Si ratio thus serves as a chemical knob that primarily modulates the Fermi level via compositional band filling, while off-stoichiometry-induced chemical disorder plays a subordinate role in determining the AHE mechanism. These results position off-stoichiometric PrAlSi as a tunable platform where subtle compositional variation reversibly switches the AHE mechanism.

### Scalable Quantum Monte Carlo for Shannon-Rényi Entropy: An Experiment-visitable Probe of Quantum Phases and Criticality
- **arXiv**: [2610.16494](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16494)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16494.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Scalable Quantum Monte Carlo for Shannon-Rényi Entropy: An Experiment-visitable Probe of Quantum Phases and Criticality
- **Authors**: Dong-Xu Liu, Zhe Wang, Zenan Liu Yi-Ming Ding, Zheng Yan
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.stat-mech
- **TL;DR**: 该文构造了Shannon-Rényi熵的时空流形与复本粘贴表述，将其转化为广义配分函数之比，用重加权退火量子蒙特卡洛实现多项式代价计算，并首次建立其量子临界点附近的有限尺寸标度理论，可从该熵提取关联长度指数、Goldstone模式数与基态简并度。

The Shannon-Rényi (SR) entropy characterizes the probability distribution of many-body wave functions in a chosen basis and is directly accessible from single-shot measurement snapshots in cold-atom experiments, yet its theoretical evaluation in interacting systems carries exponential cost. Here we construct a field-theoretic spacetime manifold for the SR entropy in which $q$ replicas are glued at the imaginary-time boundaries, recasting it as a ratio of generalized partition functions amenable to large-scale quantum Monte Carlo simulation. On this basis we develop a reweight-annealing framework with two complementary strategies: a virtual-parameter scheme interpolating between unglued and fully glued limits via binomial-weighted partial gluing, and a real-parameter scheme using a physical coupling constant as the annealing variable within stochastic series expansion. Both reduce the computational cost from exponential to polynomial. Most importantly, we establish for the first time a finite-size scaling theory for the SR entropy near a quantum critical point, $S_q=a(g)L^2+S_q^0\left((g-g_c)L^{1/ν}\right)$; data collapse of its first derivative yields the correlation-length exponent $ν$ for both the O(3) and Ising universality classes, realized in the spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ columnar dimerized Heisenberg model and the bilayer Heisenberg-Ising model. The same simulations extract the Goldstone-mode count $N_G=2$ and the $Z_2$ ground-state degeneracy $\ln 2$ from subleading terms. These results elevate the SR entropy to a standalone, order-parameter-free diagnostic of quantum criticality with the same scaling status as the entanglement entropy, and close the loop between theory and quantum simulation by enabling quantitative benchmarking of experimental snapshots from state-of-the-art quantum simulators.

### Electronic Structure of a Delafossite Oxide CuAlO$_2$ in Comparison with CuCrO$_2$
- **arXiv**: [2610.16061](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16061)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16061.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Electronic Structure of a Delafossite Oxide CuAlO$_2$ in Comparison with CuCrO$_2$
- **Authors**: Kenta Takahashi, Ryo Kato, Mario Okawa, Tetsuji Okuda, Akira Yasui, Eiji Ikenaga, Kanta Ono, Noriaki Hamada, Tomohiko Saitoh
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 通过VUV与硬X射线光电子能谱及LDA+U能带计算，对比CuAlO2与CuCrO2的电子结构，证实CuAlO2中Cu为一价，并揭示铜铁矿氧化物中空3d轨道的关键作用。

We investigated the electronic structure of a delafossite oxide CuAlO$_2$ in comparison with CuCrO$_2$, using vacuum ultraviolet (VUV) and hard x-ray (HX) photoemission spectroscopy (PES) and band structure calculations within the local-density approximation +$U$ scheme. Cu 3$p\! -\! 3d$ resonant PES and Cu $2p$ core-level HX-PES give concrete evidence that the Cu valence in CuAlO$_2$ is monovalent, which makes a sharp contrast to CuCrO$_2$ that showed contradictory results depending upon probing methods [Yokobori \textit{et al.}, Phys. Rev. B \textbf{87}, 195124 (2013)]. We further noticed weak but detectable satellite structures due to Cu$^{2+}$ state in the Cu $2p$ HX-PES spectrum of CuCrO$_2$, surprisingly, without an observable main peak due to the Cu$^{2+}$ state. Comparisons between the valence-band HX-PES spectra and the band structure calculations revealed a significant amount of the Cu $4s$ and Al $3s$/Cr $4s$ states in the valence band. The above results can reasonably be explained by the Cu $3d, 4s-$Cr $3d$ charge transfer model that we previously proposed in the above paper, thereby demonstrating the critical role of empty $3d$ orbitals in the electronic structure of the copper delafossite oxides.

### H$_2$-Like Units and Pressure-Tunable High-$T_\mathrm{c}$ Superconductivity in NaH$_6$
- **arXiv**: [2610.17148](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17148)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17148.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: H$_2$-Like Units and Pressure-Tunable High-$T_\mathrm{c}$ Superconductivity in NaH$_6$
- **Authors**: Tianran Chen, Taner Yildirim
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con
- **TL;DR**: 本文通过第一性原理研究NaH6的相图与超导电性：R-3m相在10 GPa动力学稳定、Tc=34 K，50 GPa升至99 K；立方Pm-3m相在50 GPa达230 K且继续加压后衰减很小，属于"分子型"高Tc氢化物。

Hydrogen-rich materials offer a route to high-temperature conventional superconductivity, but often require extreme pressures. Here we report a first-principles study of NaH$_6$, identified in our large-scale hydride search and independently predicted in a subsequent high-throughput study. Several phases compete, with $R\bar{3}m$ and $Pm\bar{3}m$ dominant over broad pressure ranges. The $R\bar{3}m$ phase remains dynamically stable down to 10 GPa, where its calculated $T_\mathrm{c}$ is 34 K, rising to 99 K at 50 GPa. Cubic $Pm\bar{3}m$ becomes thermodynamically competitive at higher pressures and reaches a calculated $T_\mathrm{c}$ of 230 K at 50 GPa, decreasing only weakly upon further compression. Molecular dynamics and phonon-renormalization calculations support structural persistence over the sampled timescale and indicate relatively small anharmonic effects. Despite their high $T_\mathrm{c}$, both phases contain elongated H$_2$-like units, often considered unfavorable for high-$T_\mathrm{c}$ hydrides. Mode-resolved electron-phonon coupling links Raman-active hydrogen vibrations to superconductivity. In $R\bar{3}m$, the linewidths of these modes increase with the pressure-induced tilt of H$_2$ units relative to the threefold axis, alongside the rise in $T_\mathrm{c}$. A minimal symmetry-based model of the vibration-induced second-moment tensor of the hydrogen sublattice shows that these trends are consistent with a significant contribution from response components perpendicular to the threefold axis. NaH$_6$ thus represents an unusual molecular-like hydride in which hydrogen-sublattice reorganization may enable high-$T_\mathrm{c}$ superconductivity at comparatively moderate pressures.

### Solvating machine-learning interatomic potentials for solution-phase chemistry
- **arXiv**: [2610.17226](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17226)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17226.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Solvating machine-learning interatomic potentials for solution-phase chemistry
- **Authors**: Anton Z. Ni, Joonho Lee
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph, cs.LG
- **TL;DR**: AnySolv: 可迁移的隐式溶剂化势，为现有气相MLIP提供溶剂依赖的能量与力修正，支持179种溶剂，在DFT-SMD精度下实现低成本溶剂化学模拟。

Most machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are trained on gas-phase data, which limits their use for chemistry in solution. Here, we introduce AnySolv, a transferable implicit solvation potential that provides solvent-dependent energy and force corrections to existing gas-phase potentials without retraining them. It is trained on 1.4 million solvation energies and forces computed with density functional theory (DFT) and SMD in 36 solvents, spanning charged, open-shell, and transition-state structures. Because solvents are represented by continuous physical descriptors, AnySolv supports 179 solvents, including those absent from training. On external benchmarks, AnySolv reproduces DFT-SMD solvation energies to 2-4 meV mean absolute error at more than three orders of magnitude lower cost, and it matches the accuracy of DFT-SMD against experimental solvation free energies. This enables screening of the mechanisms of 120 nucleophilic aromatic substitution reactions in six phases as well as molecular dynamics simulations that reproduce the experimentally observed solvent-dependent conformational preferences of alanine dipeptide. By providing transferable solvation corrections to existing MLIPs, AnySolv enables scalable simulations of solvent-dependent chemical reactivity and molecular dynamics.

### Principles and Techniques for Agentic Research in Experimental Physics
- **arXiv**: [2610.17193](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17193)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17193.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Principles and Techniques for Agentic Research in Experimental Physics
- **Authors**: Niko R. Reed, Declan M. Daly, Andrew J. Beling, Jordan D. Giandrea, Ronald L. Walsworth, John W. Blanchard, Daniel Ang
- **Categories**: physics.comp-ph, quant-ph
- **TL;DR**: 本文提出面向实验物理的协作式AI智能体十条要求与实现框架，并通过六个案例证明闭环工作流能产出超越已发表结果的有用成果。

The use of artificial intelligence (AI) in physics research is increasingly common, yet few guidelines exist for its effective use. Starting with a principles-based approach, we outline a set of ten requirements for collaborative AI agents in experimental physics, spanning scientific norms, effective agent use, and domain expertise. We take a human-centered approach in which agents act as collaborators that amplify rather than replace the expertise of a human researcher (user) who directs the work and is responsible for its results. We then present a framework designed to enforce these requirements, detailing methods for improving off-the-shelf behavior of large language models (LLMs) with custom scaffolding and describing general-purpose scientific workflows and skills. In particular, we highlight the power of closed-loop campaigns where agents work iteratively toward a well-defined goal. We demonstrate the potential of this mode of research through six case studies in experimental physics, covering device design, hardware control, neural network optimization, physical model identification, and data analysis. All six case studies result in useful work products that add utility to our research, including a neural network architecture and solid-state quantum defect photodynamic model that substantially outperform our published work; a microwave waveguide design that extends a published design concept to a fabrication-ready device meeting our experimental constraints; a quantum control pulse sequence candidate that improves simulated performance by more than an order of magnitude; commissioning of an NMR spectrometer to $\sim$1~Hz frequency precision on live hardware; and finally a data-fitting pipeline that achieves, to our knowledge, the first reconstruction of the Ising microstate of an artificial spin-ice array from diffraction-limited magnetic imaging.

### Ionomer-free Iridium Anodes with Low Loading for Zero-gap Electrolyzers via Direct Atomic Layer Deposition
- **arXiv**: [2610.15798](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15798)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15798.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Ionomer-free Iridium Anodes with Low Loading for Zero-gap Electrolyzers via Direct Atomic Layer Deposition
- **Authors**: Peter M. Piechulla, Iris Blommestein, Anne van Gerven, Henri Pelzer, Ezgi Demiröz, Jonas Englhard, Mingliang Chen, Julien Bachmann, J. Ruud van Ommen
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 本研究用定制微孔基底与两种基于Ir(EtCp)(CHD)前驱体的ALD工艺制备无离聚物Ir阳极催化剂层，热ALD实现0.21 mg/cm²低载量下与商用CCM相当的PEMWE性能并经受600 h加速耐久测试；等离子体ALD进一步降至0.04 mg/cm²但活性较低，且该无墨策略兼容多种膜材料。

Proton exchange membrane water electrolysis (PEMWE) is among the most promising techniques to provide hydrogen as both energy carrier and feedstock for the chemical industry, due to its compatibility with intermittent renewable electricity sources. However, a major bottleneck in scaling up PEMWE is the limited supply of the anode catalyst Ir. This work shows a novel approach to tailoring ionomer-free catalyst layers based on custom-made microporous substrates and two different atomic layer deposition (ALD) processes, both using Ir(EtCp)(CHD) precursor. Thermal ALD using O3 as counter-reactant yields mostly metallic Ir/IrOx and an electrode Ir loading of 0.21 mg/cm2. The PEMWE cell-level performance is comparable to a commercial catalyst-coated membrane anode despite 7x lower loading, and is maintained over an accelerated durability test equivalent to 600 h of intermittent operation. Moreover, using a low-loading ALD-made cathode, the total platinum group metal loading is only 0.24 mg/cm2. Plasma-enhanced ALD reduces the Ir loading to 0.04 mg/cm2 by tailoring the catalyst distribution, however, yields lower-activity IrO2. Finally, direct ALD catalyst deposition needs no ionomer-bearing ink. The anodes are therefore compatible with various membrane materials, as demonstrated in a CO2 electrolysis experiment with an anion exchange membrane.

### Transport Determines Spectral Distance in Mesoscopic Thermodynamics
- **arXiv**: [2610.16752](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16752)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16752.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Transport Determines Spectral Distance in Mesoscopic Thermodynamics
- **Authors**: Giorgio SONNINO
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 论文从介观正则模型中的正Hermitian输运张量出发，构造热力学态之间的距离度量 G=2g（g为对称输运张量），并给出双通道热电系统中主距离比与 ZT 的解析关系。

We derive a distance between thermodynamic states from a positive Hermitian transport tensor within a mesoscopic canonical model. An exact first-order factorization of the evolution generator gives a commutator norm and the metric $G=2g$, where $g$ denotes the symmetric transport tensor. Gradients of the antisymmetric tensor can change the evolution, but they leave this distance unchanged. The construction allows coordinate-dependent coefficients. For constant Onsager coefficients, a Clifford representation gives the same metric. In a two-channel thermoelectric system, the ratio of normalized principal distances is $\sqrt{1+ZT}+\sqrt{ZT}$. Transport coupling thus fixes the anisotropy of this metric. The construction requires neither an independent metric nor a physical spin variable.

### Latent Heat and Raman Spectrum Change on Phase Transition of Water Including Calcium Chloride
- **arXiv**: [2610.15821](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15821)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15821.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Latent Heat and Raman Spectrum Change on Phase Transition of Water Including Calcium Chloride
- **Authors**: Dongha Kim, Changyoun Lee
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 研究利用拉曼O-H伸缩谱追踪水相变潜热相关能量学，纯水与CaCl2掺杂样品显示可重复的温度依赖与系统偏移。

This study investigates how Raman O-H stretching signatures may be able to track latent-heat-related energetics during water's phase transitions. Prior work on latent heat and Raman analysis of phase change is reviewed to establish theoretical approach. Building on a framework that links O-H frequency shifts to hydrogen-bond strength, we relate entropy/enthalpy variations to temperature-dependent changes in peak position and integrated intensity. Experiments on pure water and impurity-doped samples, specifically CaCl$_2$, were performed under controlled temperatures, and spectra were deconvoluted and integrated over defined bands. The Raman metrics exhibit reproducible temperature dependence that captures the transition window, while CaCl$_2$ induces systematic shifts and measurable modifications in the apparent latent-heat signature. These results highlight Raman spectroscopy as a quantitative probe of thermodynamic evolution and impurity effects in water and related systems.

### Turning a superconducting diode into a Feynman ratchet to realize a nanoscale heat engine
- **arXiv**: [2610.17257](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17257)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17257.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Turning a superconducting diode into a Feynman ratchet to realize a nanoscale heat engine
- **Authors**: Konstantinos Kontogeorgiou, İnanç Adagideli, Géraldine Haack, Fabian Hassler
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, cond-mat.stat-mech, quant-ph
- **TL;DR**: 本文提出基于具有超导二极管效应的约瑟夫森结实现费曼棘轮的物理方案，该装置可仅靠温度梯度驱动的热涨落输出功；分析了输出功率与效率，并通过塞贝克系数判定其作为珀尔帖制冷器反向运行的条件。

Feynman's ratchet illustrates how work can be extracted from thermal fluctuations in the presence of two heat baths at different temperatures. While some experimental realizations of the ratchet effect have been studied, most lack miniaturization, which is important for some applications. Here, we propose a physical implementation of Feynman's ratchet based on Josephson junctions exhibiting the superconducting diode effect. This setup functions as a heat engine that generates work solely from a temperature gradient via thermal fluctuations. We analyze its output power and efficiency, and, through the Seebeck coefficient, identify the conditions under which the device operates in reverse, functioning instead as a Peltier cooler.

### Field-driven phase transition crossover in kagome antiferromagnet Mn$_{3-x}$Co$_x$Sn
- **arXiv**: [2610.16189](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16189)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16189.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Field-driven phase transition crossover in kagome antiferromagnet Mn$_{3-x}$Co$_x$Sn
- **Authors**: Shubhankar Paul, Chanchal Sow
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 通过在Mn位微量Co掺杂破坏Mn3Sn的kagome晶格对称性，调控Mn3-xCoxSn单晶的磁相变性质，观测到逆磁热效应与场驱动的一级到二级相变交叉，并构建了新的磁场-磁序相图。

Mn$_3$Sn exhibits a complex magnetic phase diagram with multiple symmetry-breaking phenomena. Minute electron doping at the Mn site breaks the kagome lattice symmetry in Mn$_3$Sn. In this work, we exploited this to tune the order of the magnetic phase transition using magnetocaloric effect in Mn$_{3-x}$Co$_x$Sn single crystals. Pristine Mn$_3$Sn shows metallic behaviour with a spin-reorientation transition at 278 K, which shifts to 175 K upon Co doping. Additionally, Co doping also induces a weak-ferromagnetic transition near $\approx 210$ K. The effect of Co doping results inverse to conventional magnetocaloric effect, with enhanced magnetic entropy change. Further, the scaled entropy change reveals a field-driven magnetic phase transition crossover from first order to second order in Mn$_{2.75}$Co$_{0.25}$Sn crystal. Based on experimental data, a new phase diagram is constructed, depicting the field evolution of various magnetic orders.

### Observation of stacked vortex structure with number controllable Majorana zero mode in Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe heterojunction
- **arXiv**: [2610.17701](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17701)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17701.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Observation of stacked vortex structure with number controllable Majorana zero mode in Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe heterojunction
- **Authors**: Xinru Han, Zhaokun Luo, Quanxin Hu, Xingjian Zhu, Xiaohong Pan, Dan Mu, Xinyu Liang, Zulin Xu, Qiheng Wang, Ryan L. Kong, Mingyuan Li, Zhengliang Zhang, Fazhi Yang, Mojun Pan, Xin Liu, Qiangqiang Gu, Baiqing Lv, Gan Wang, Hong Ding
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con, cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 在化学计量比单五重层Bi2Te3/FeTe异质结中，STM/STS观测到数目可调的Majorana零模(MZM)，其数量可由磁场和Bi2Te3岛尺寸调控。

Creation and control of Majorana zero modes (MZMs) in solid devices is the key challenge in realizing fault tolerant topological quantum computation. Here we report spectroscopic evidence for number controllable MZMs in stoichiometric one quintuple layer Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe heterostructures, together with the observation of stacked vortices in superconducting islands. Low temperature scanning tunnelling microscopy and spectroscopy reveal a long period moire superlattice arising from lattice mismatch, which induces nanoscale modulation of superconducting gaps. Magnetic vortices exhibit a stacked core structure with two layer derived bound state branches: a dispersive branch evolving into an X-shaped pattern away from the vortex center, and a quantum-limit branch characterized by a spatially unsplit MZMs together with discrete integer quantized Caroli-de Gennes-Matricon states. Notably, MZMs are observed in all measured vortices, and their number can be tuned by magnetic field and the size of Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$ islands. These results establish stoichiometric 1QL-Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe as a patterned superconducting interface that enables isolated and few-vortex Majorana configurations, providing a controlled platform for fuse MZMs.

### Chromatographic Peak Shape beyond the High-Péclet Approximation: Analytic Time-Domain Expression and Derivatives
- **arXiv**: [2610.17297](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17297)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17297.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Chromatographic Peak Shape beyond the High-Péclet Approximation: Analytic Time-Domain Expression and Derivatives
- **Authors**: Hernán R. Sánchez
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph
- **TL;DR**: 从线性等度色谱的随机模型中推导洗脱时间密度及其参数导数，通过正态-逆高斯密度的有限高斯混合近似，将其表示为高斯-伽马卷积级数，并给出高效的系数递推、舍入误差估计与反向重构算法。

Analytic time-domain expressions for the elution-time density and its parameter derivatives were recently derived from a stochastic model of linear isocratic chromatography incorporating advective-diffusive transport, injection dispersion, and fast and slow retention mechanisms. A Gaussian approximation, justified asymptotically at high column-scale Péclet numbers, was adopted for the distribution of the combined mobile-phase and fast-retention time, while slow retention was incorporated through a series of Gaussian-Gamma convolutions. Here, the underlying normal-inverse-Gaussian density is approximated by a finite Gaussian mixture using an existing quadrature rule. The elution-time PDF is a weighted sum of series of the previously derived Gaussian-Gamma form. Its parameter derivatives are also obtained. Newly derived recurrences reduce the cost of evaluating the coefficients of that series and their derivatives from quadratic to linear in truncation order for a fixed number of mechanisms. Through a probabilistic analysis of the series coefficients, the required number of terms is found to be small enough for rapid evaluation under usual chromatographic conditions. A relative roundoff-error estimator is derived to quantify finite-precision loss in the inherited forward recurrence for Gaussian-Gamma convolutions. For the cancellation-prone regime, a backward reconstruction is developed, initialized from an exact ratio of consecutive Gaussian-Gamma terms expressed through parabolic-cylinder functions. A highly accurate approximation is derived for rapid evaluation of this ratio. Independent high-precision calculations confirm the high accuracy of the resulting double-precision evaluation in the tested numerical case.

### Wisevolve Potential Engine: Machine-Learning Molecular Dynamics at 100 Million Atom-Steps per Second on a Single GPU
- **arXiv**: [2610.17366](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17366)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17366.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Wisevolve Potential Engine: Machine-Learning Molecular Dynamics at 100 Million Atom-Steps per Second on a Single GPU
- **Authors**: Hekai Bu, Zheyong Fan
- **Categories**: physics.comp-ph
- **TL;DR**: WPE 是为 GPUMD 设计的 GPU 势能执行引擎，按势模型与模拟阶段选择并复用执行策略，在多种负载上实现 1.24x–10.5x 加速且结果与 GPUMD 一致。

General-purpose molecular dynamics software supports diverse interatomic potentials and simulation requirements. Within a simulation stage, the loaded potential model and the required calculations typically remain fixed as the atomic configuration evolves. We present the Wisevolve Potential Engine (WPE), which uses this information from GPUMD to select and reuse a GPU execution policy tailored to the model and simulation stage. GPUMD retains simulation control and ownership of the simulation data. On a single NVIDIA RTX 5090, WPE-enabled GPUMD (GPUMD-WPE) achieves a median throughput of 109 million atom-step/s across 106 neuroevolution potential (NEP) workloads. For a 15.1-million-atom diamond Si system, it sustains 142 million atom-step/s. Relative to GPUMD, geometric-mean speedups are 3.49x across these NEP workloads, 3.61x across 16 workloads using NEP with dynamic charges (qNEP), 1.24x across 28 classical-potential workloads, and 10.5x across 56 path-integral molecular dynamics (PIMD) configurations. End-to-end calculations of water structure, lattice thermal conductivity, and PIMD thermal expansion show close agreement with corresponding GPUMD results and published simulation results. A separate 12.5-million-atom Cu shock-compression simulation demonstrates its applicability to large-scale nonequilibrium dynamics. These results support model- and stage-specific specialization as a practical way to accelerate GPU potential evaluation in general-purpose molecular dynamics workflows.

### Epitaxial Oxide Thin Films with High Entropy and Anomalous Crystallinity
- **arXiv**: [2610.15861](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15861)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15861.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Epitaxial Oxide Thin Films with High Entropy and Anomalous Crystallinity
- **Authors**: Joseph Petruska, Christina M. Rost, Jon-Paul Maria, Saeed S. I. Almishal
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该综述聚焦脉冲激光沉积(PLD)制备高熵氧化物(HEO)薄膜，以岩盐型MgCoNiCuZnO及其衍生物为核心，讨论生长动力学、外延约束与各阳离子角色如何共同决定薄膜的晶格调控、缺陷结构与外延质量，并展望功能构型的发现与设计。

Pulsed laser deposition (PLD) expands the compositional and structural possibilities of high-entropy oxide (HEO) thin films through far-from-equilibrium growth and kinetic arrest of metastable configurations. This review connects growth kinetics and epitaxial constraints to the roles of individual cations in shaping the resulting films. We focus primarily on rock salt MgCoNiCuZnO and its derivatives, with selected fluorite, spinel, and perovskite examples illustrating broader applicability. In MgCoNiCuZnO, Co valence and Cu coordination illustrate how chemistry and growth conditions jointly enable lattice tuning and coherent nanostructure formation, while aliovalent and size-misfit additions expand the accessible defect and compositional landscapes. These coupled degrees of freedom underpin anomalous crystallinity, the coexistence of extensive chemical disorder with exceptional crystalline quality and epitaxial order, including full coherence in selected systems, particularly rock salts grown on MgO. This combination opens opportunities to discover and engineer functional configurations in high-quality single-layer films and heterostructures.

### Bifurcation Structure of Double Hysteresis and Isolated Branches in a Parametric Duffing Nanoresonator
- **arXiv**: [2610.17052](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17052)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17052.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Bifurcation Structure of Double Hysteresis and Isolated Branches in a Parametric Duffing Nanoresonator
- **Authors**: Dingda Zhang, Maria Kallergi, Chanyoung So, Eva M. Weig, Ahmed A. Barakat
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, nlin.CD
- **TL;DR**: 该论文在直驱与参量驱动相位差45°下，实验与解析地追踪了随参量激励增大时稳态响应的分岔路径：单峰先分裂为双峰（仍单值）→两次尖点分岔生成两对鞍结、形成双迟滞→参量失稳阈值处一支鞍结移向无界幅值/失谐、阈值以上出现孤立分支结构。

Direct and parametric driving together generate nonlinear response structures beyond conventional Duffing hysteresis, including multiple switching regions and isolated branches. Understanding how these structures are connected requires identifying the bifurcations that reorganize the response as the parametric excitation is varied. At a phase difference of 45 degrees between the direct and parametric excitations, we experimentally and analytically determine how the steady-state response changes as the parametric excitation is increased. A single resonance peak first splits into two while the response remains single valued. Two successive cusp bifurcations then create two saddle-node pairs and establish a double-hysteretic response.
