At the parametric-instability threshold, one saddle node of each pair is displaced towards unbounded amplitude and detuning, and an isolated-branch structure emerges above threshold. A parametric representation of the steady state distinguishes amplitude extrema from genuine saddle-node bifurcations and provides analytical conditions for these changes. Measurements on a dielectrically actuated pre-stressed SiN nanostring reproduce the predicted response and associated switching behavior. These results establish the bifurcation pathway connecting the different response structures at this phase configuration, providing a systematic basis for controlling multistability and switching in parametrically driven nonlinear resonators.

### Extreme anharmonicity determines the superionic transition in the prototypical antifluorite Li$_2$O
- **arXiv**: [2610.15986](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15986)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.15986.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Extreme anharmonicity determines the superionic transition in the prototypical antifluorite Li$_2$O
- **Authors**: Lorenzo Bastonero, Chiara Cignarella, Nicola Marzari, Lorenzo Monacelli
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该研究结合非微扰晶格动力学与机器学习原子间势，揭示Li₂O超离子相变由强非谐性而非单纯体积驱动的谐波声子软化决定，并给出可实验探测的谱学特征。

Superionic conductors are promising electrolyte candidates for all-solid-state batteries, thanks to their liquid-like ionic mobility. In the prototypical lithium-ion oxide superionic conductor with antifluorite structure, the high-temperature transition to the fast-ion regime has long been attributed to volume-driven harmonic phonon softening. However, this quasi-harmonic picture neglects the strong lattice anharmonicity inherent to the fast-ion regime, leaving the microscopic origin of the transition unresolved. Here, we apply non-perturbative lattice dynamics combined with a first-principles-accurate machine-learning interatomic potential to study the phase transition in Li$_2$O. We demonstrate that anharmonicity, rather than volume expansion alone as commonly assumed, plays the decisive role in determining the transition temperature and the underlying mechanism. Finally, we analyze the spectral properties using the time-dependent self-consistent harmonic approximation, revealing the breakdown of the quasi-particle picture in this material. The spectral function and the infrared response provide signtures of the phase transition that can be investigated experimentally using inelastic neutron or X-ray scattering and infrared spectroscopy. All these results establish the extreme anharmonicity of Li$_2$O near the superionic regime and provide a lattice-dynamic framework for understanding and engineering the fast-ion transition in this type of materials.

### Thermally Enhanced Pseudo-Goldstone Spin Gap in the Isotropic Helimagnet ZnCr$_2$Se$_4$
- **arXiv**: [2610.16439](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16439)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16439.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Thermally Enhanced Pseudo-Goldstone Spin Gap in the Isotropic Helimagnet ZnCr$_2$Se$_4$
- **Authors**: N. D. Andriushin, A. Sanders, J. Reuther, D. C. Peets, P. Steffens, A. Hiess, J. Lass, L. Prodan, V. Tsurkan, V. Felea, D. S. Inosov
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 非弹性中子散射发现螺旋磁体ZnCr₂Se₄中赝戈德斯通自旋能隙随升温先增大后于T_N附近闭合，呈反常非单调温度依赖，与基于有效四次势的各向同性体系自旋动力学模拟一致，源于热涨落。

In magnetically ordered systems, the spin gap in the magnon spectrum is normally expected to decrease on warming and close at the transition out of the long-range order. The presence of a pseudo-Goldstone gap is commonly associated with nonlinear terms in spin-wave theory. However, its precise origin and detailed properties can vary and remain under debate. Here we show by means of inelastic neutron scattering that the pseudo-Goldstone gap in the Heisenberg helimagnet ZnCr$_2$Se$_4$ exhibits an atypical, nonmonotonic temperature dependence: the gap increases over much of the ordered phase before closing near $T_\mathrm{N}$. These results align with recent spin-dynamic simulations based on an effective quartic potential in an isotropic system~[Phys. Rev. B \textbf{113}, L220411 (2026)]. As the pseudo-Goldstone spin gap in ZnCr$_2$Se$_4$ is dominated by quantum effects at subkelvin temperatures, its characteristic growth on warming is caused by thermal fluctuations.

### Imaging atomic-scale surface structure and superconducting gap of the misfit layered compound (SnS)1.17NbS2
- **arXiv**: [2610.17879](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17879)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17879.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Imaging atomic-scale surface structure and superconducting gap of the misfit layered compound (SnS)1.17NbS2
- **Authors**: Dongming Zhao, Shamarita Deb, Lichen Ji, Souvik Das, Paul Simon, Raúl Cardoso-Gil, Steffen Wirth, Walter Schnelle, Sushmita Chandra, Jian-Feng Ge, Claudia Felser
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con
- **TL;DR**: 该研究用XRD、截面STEM、电输运与低温STM/STS研究了天然失配层状化合物(SnS)1.17NbS2的表面原子结构与局域超导性。

Superlattices of van der Waals-coupled two-dimensional materials offer a platform for engineering electronic band structure and fostering emergent quantum states. Misfit layered compounds provide naturally assembled superlattices, in which crystallographically distinct two-dimensional subsystems coexist within a single bulk crystal. Therein, the presence of both commensurate and incommensurate lattice directions introduces structural modulation, which might alter real-space electronic orders such as superconductivity. Here, we investigate the atomic-scale surface structure and local superconductivity of the misfit layered compound (SnS)1.17NbS2 using structural characterization, electrical transport, and low-temperature scanning tunnelling microscopy (STM). While X-ray diffraction and cross-sectional scanning transmission electron microscopy confirm the bulk misfit structure, STM resolves large SnS- and NbS2-terminated terraces, where the surface atomic registry differs from the bulk misfit structure. Furthermore, tunnelling spectroscopy at 2 K reveals nearly identical superconducting gaps on both terminations, and the spatial variation of the gap shows no visible modulation that corresponds to the misfit lattice. These results establish an atomic-scale picture of the surface structure and superconducting states of this naturally assembled misfit heterostructure.

### All-electron machine-learned orbital-free density functional for periodic CHNO systems
- **arXiv**: [2610.17975](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17975)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17975.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: All-electron machine-learned orbital-free density functional for periodic CHNO systems
- **Authors**: Marc K. Ickler, Andreas Hermann, Fred A. Hamprecht
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph, cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cs.LG, physics.comp-ph
- **TL;DR**: 提出STRUCTURES-P：面向周期性CHNO体系的全电子、无轨道密度泛函，静电项用原子中心高斯基组解析求解，动能与交换关联能由等变网络从非基态密度的能量与梯度学习；从原子密度叠加出发变分极小化即可恢复Kohn-Sham基态。

We introduce STRUCTURES-P, a machine-learned all-electron orbital-free density functional for periodic CHNO systems. Electrostatics are evaluated analytically in an atom-centered Gaussian basis, while an equivariant network learns the kinetic and exchange-correlation energy from energies and gradients of non-ground-state densities. Minimizing the functional from a superposition of atomic densities recovers the Kohn-Sham ground state: on held-out crystals, the density error falls from 6.7% to 0.4% of the electron count and the energy error reaches 0.25 mHa per atom, half that of a same-architecture interatomic potential. A single functional can be trained for both isolated molecules and periodic systems, and the model generalizes to larger unit cells. It largely preserves relative-energy rankings and reproduces convex-hull vertices on the tested H-N composition slice. Together, these results demonstrate the viability of machine-learned all-electron orbital-free DFT for variational density optimization and energy evaluation in fixed-geometry solids.

### KINTORA: a semi-Lagrangian discontinuous Galerkin drift-kinetic, unstructured mesh tokamak solver
- **arXiv**: [2610.17428](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17428)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17428.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: KINTORA: a semi-Lagrangian discontinuous Galerkin drift-kinetic, unstructured mesh tokamak solver
- **Authors**: Mark F. Adams
- **Categories**: physics.comp-ph
- **TL;DR**: 提出非结构三角网格上的连续谱漂移动理求解器（SLDG + FCI 场线映射），全算子交叉验证，ITG 演示与未收敛的带状流残余。

Continuum gyrokinetic codes use structured, field-aligned grids on nested flux surfaces; particle-in-cell codes support unstructured meshes and open field lines but carry a statistical noise floor. We present a continuum drift-kinetic solver that occupies neither position: a semi-Lagrangian discontinuous Galerkin discretization on unstructured triangular poloidal planes extruded toroidally, whose parallel advance is a map along the exact field line in the flux-coordinate independent manner, so the mesh is never aligned with the field, with adiabatic electrons and a long-wavelength polarization field solve. Every operator is verified against an independently computed reference, from Landau damping to the geodesic-acoustic root and a linear ion-temperature-gradient eigenvalue problem, every measured rate within 4% except the geodesic-acoustic damping, within 12%. A linear toroidal ion-temperature-gradient demonstration on a canonical-Maxwellian equilibrium recovers a radially coherent mode whose growth rate, 0.065 c_s/a, is stationary in time. The collisionless zonal-flow residual is the one measurement still moving: it rises with poloidal refinement from 0.72 to 0.87 of the model's own orbit-quadrature prediction for the measured observable and has not converged. The solver is fully device resident, and every result is reproduced between CPU and GPU builds and across MPI process counts. The code, the data behind every table and an artifact description are in a public repository.

### An empirical model for deriving information on ion dynamics from $μ$SR data via the conventional Kubo-Toyabe function analysis
- **arXiv**: [2610.16006](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16006)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16006.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: An empirical model for deriving information on ion dynamics from $μ$SR data via the conventional Kubo-Toyabe function analysis
- **Authors**: Ryosuke Kadono
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 提出将扩展Kubo-Toyabe(KT)函数与常规KT函数参数解析关联的经验模型，用其从μSR数据中正确提取离子扩散的活化能和前因子。

We present an empirical model that analytically relates the parameters of the extended Kubo-Toyabe (KT) function $G_z^{\rm EA}(t;Δ,ν_{\rm i},Q)$, which describes muon spin relaxation ($μ$SR) induced by jump diffusion of ions using the Edwards-Anderson parameter $Q$, to those of the conventional KT function $G_z^{\rm KT}(t;Δ_{\rm KT},ν_{\rm KT})$ that serves as an approximation of $G_z^{\rm EA}(t)$. The proposed formulae successfully reproduce the anomalous temperature dependences of the linewidth $Δ_{\rm KT}(T)$ and the fluctuation rate $ν_{\rm KT}(T)$ which are artifacts observed upon analyzing $μ$SR data on ion diffusion using $G_z^{\rm KT}(t)$. Applying this model to $Δ_{\rm KT}$ and $ν_{\rm KT}$ published in the literature of battery materials yields activation energy $E_{\rm i}$ and pre-exponential factor $ν_{\rm i0}$ in the Arrhenius equation for ion diffusion, $ν_{\rm i}(T)=ν_{\rm i0} e^{-E_{\rm i}/kT}$, that are in excellent agreement with those deduced using other techniques, transforming peculiar artifacts into a powerful diagnostic tool.

### Non-volatile Orbital magnetoelectric effect in a two-dimensional electron gas
- **arXiv**: [2610.17073](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17073)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17073.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Non-volatile Orbital magnetoelectric effect in a two-dimensional electron gas
- **Authors**: Yu Chen, Martando Rath, Julien Brehin, Cinthia Piamonteze, Daniele Preziosi, Payal Wadhwa, Alessio Filippetti, Yuri Fukaya, Mario Cuoco, Filomena Forte, Daniela Stornaiuolo, Manuel Bibes, Marco Salluzzo
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该文利用原位电场调控的X射线吸收谱，在LaAlO3/EuTiO3/Ca-STO界面二维电子气中实现了平衡轨道磁矩的非易失电控翻转，提供了铁电控制轨道磁性的光谱学证据。

Electrical control of orbital magnetism is a central goal of orbitronics. While current-induced orbital polarization has recently been demonstrated in inversion-asymmetric systems, nonvolatile electrical control of an equilibrium orbital moment has remained elusive.
  Here, using x-ray absorption spectroscopy with \textit{in situ} electric-field control of the FE polarization, we demonstrate non-volatile switching of the equilibrium orbital magnetization of the oxide q2DEGs formed at the LaAlO$_3$/EuTiO$_3$/Ca-STO (001) interface. X-ray linear dichroism reveals a reversible modulation, driven by the FE-polarization direction, of the Ti-3$d$ crystal-field splitting and orbital occupancy, establishing a ferroelectric-like metallic state. X-ray magnetic circular dichroism shows a polarization-controlled switching of the Eu$^{2+}$ orbital magnetic moment induced by the orbital magnetization of the q2DEG projected onto Eu-5$d$ states and detected at the Eu-$M_{4,5}$ edge through Eu-5$d$/Eu-4$f$ hybridization in the inversion-symmetry-broken interface. These results provide spectroscopic evidence of ferroelectric control of orbital magnetism in an oxide q2DEG.

### Nonrelativistic Optically-Induced Spin Torque in Noncollinear Magnets
- **arXiv**: [2610.16052](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16052)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16052.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Nonrelativistic Optically-Induced Spin Torque in Noncollinear Magnets
- **Authors**: Zhiyuan Li, Jian Zhou
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该文提出利用非共线磁构型的非相对论自旋劈裂，实现远超自旋轨道耦合极限的光致自旋力矩。

Manipulating spin using contactless and non-invasive optical approaches is one of the forefront topic in the modern spintronic science and technology. Prior studies on optically-induced spin reorientation usually scales with spin-orbit coupling strength, setting a limitation on composing heavy elements. In this work, we extend such a limit into nonrelativistic regime by exploring noncollinear magnetic configuration. Using a general spin group-theoretical investigation, we show how the symmetric operators in both spin and lattice spaces control the spin torque and quantum geometry of Bloch wavefunction--including both quantum metric and Berry curvature. We describe their site-resolved spin symmetries and unravel their selection rules. Furthermore, we adopt a Kagomé lattice model and a realistic material family to showcase the local spin torque under light irradiation. Since the nonrelativistic spin splitting usually spans on the order of electron-volt, significantly larger than typical spin-orbit induced splitting, the nonrelativistic light-induced spin torque is concomitantly much stronger. This work extends the previous opto-spintronics from relativistic to nonrelativistic limit, significantly broadening its realization platform.

### Topological Chiral Superconductivity from Doping Correlated Charge Transfer Insulator with Orbital Magnetism
- **arXiv**: [2610.16674](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16674)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16674.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Topological Chiral Superconductivity from Doping Correlated Charge Transfer Insulator with Orbital Magnetism
- **Authors**: Wen Sun, Yuan Yang, Shou-Shu Gong, Wen Huang
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.supr-con
- **TL;DR**: 本文研究强关联电荷转移绝缘体掺杂后涌现拓扑手性超导的实空间机制。作者在含三个子晶格、每元胞2π磁通的强排斥无自旋费米子模型中，结合微扰分析、精确对角化和DMRG，确立了具有p+ip对称性的拓扑超导基态。

Topological chiral superconductivity has been extensively studied from the perspective of electronic structures in momentum space, while its emergence from real-space processes in strongly interacting systems remains largely unexplored. Here, we study a doped charge-transfer insulator in a strongly repulsive spinless-fermion model with three sublattices and $2π$ flux per unit cell. Combining perturbative analysis, exact diagonalization, and density matrix renormalization group simulations, we establish a topological superconducting ground state with $p+ip$ symmetry. In the large charge-transfer gap limit, chiral charge-transfer excitations generate a correlated-hopping process that provides a microscopic mechanism for electron pairing and its chiral structure. The chiral superconductivity persists into the smaller-gap regime, where chiral excitonic two-particle bound state can emerge. Our results establish a concrete real-space route to topological chiral superconductivity upon doping strongly interacting charge-transfer insulators with orbital magnetism.

### Effects of the choice of the exchange-correlation functional on high-temperature superconductivity predictions in covalent-cage M(BN)5 family
- **arXiv**: [2610.18171](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18171)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18171.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Effects of the choice of the exchange-correlation functional on high-temperature superconductivity predictions in covalent-cage M(BN)5 family
- **Authors**: Zhongyu Wan, Guo-Hua Zhong, Cheng-wei Ju, Ruiqin Zhang, Hai-Qing Lin
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con
- **TL;DR**: 该文通过高通量第一性原理计算，量化了M(BN)5笼状超导体中电子-声子耦合对交换关联泛函选择的异常敏感性，指出PBE系统性高估体积导致声子软化和Peierls失稳，而PBEsol给出Rb(BN)5在常压113 K、2 GPa下138 K的Tc。

The recent experimental synthesis of covalent cage superconductors has ignited hopes for ambient-pressure high-temperature superconductivity, while the computational strategy for discovering new materials poses challenges for accurately estimating superconducting properties. Here, we quantified the response of electron-phonon coupling (EPC) properties in M(BN)5 superconductors to different exchange-correlation (XC) effects using high-throughput first-principles calculations. We found that the metallic covalent bonds responsible for superconductivity exhibit anomalously high sensitivity to functional selection uncertainty, distinguishing them from conventional superconductors mediated by metallic bonding. The widely used PBE functional systematically overestimates lattice volumes, inducing phonon softening, enhanced EPC, and consequent Peierls instability. In contrast, PBEsol, which is more accurate through enhanced treatment of short-range interactions, eliminates imaginary frequencies in Rb(BN)5 that appear at the PBE level, yielding an outstanding superconducting critical temperature (Tc) of 113 K at ambient pressure and 138 K at 2 GPa. Enhanced electron localization and short-range correlation in covalent bonds manifest through dual mechanisms: phonon hardening at the lattice scale and band widening at the electronic scale, which collectively suppress Peierls distortion and delocalize antibonding states. Pressure-dependent analysis reveals that compression attenuates phonon discrepancies between XC functionals, whereas electronic properties remain notably sensitive to XC-induced perturbations of fine electronic bands near the Fermi level.

### Development of a Minimal Auxiliary Basis for the Particle--Particle Random Phase Approximation (ppRPA): Accelerating Excited-State Calculations in Diradical Nanoparticles and Periodic Systems
- **arXiv**: [2610.18020](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18020)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18020.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Development of a Minimal Auxiliary Basis for the Particle--Particle Random Phase Approximation (ppRPA): Accelerating Excited-State Calculations in Diradical Nanoparticles and Periodic Systems
- **Authors**: Yuchen Wang, Zhenyu Li
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph
- **TL;DR**: 提出ppRPA-as方法：将ppRPA的辅助基组压缩为基于自由原子半径的极小原子中心高斯壳层集合（含单一全局缩放因子），只作用于激发态求解步骤，从而以低代价高精度给出单-三重态能隙。

Singlet--triplet gaps set the energy scale of the two-spin manifold in molecular quantum information materials, and such systems frequently carry diradical character: metal nanoclusters, metal--organic frameworks with radical linkers, and the nitrogen--vacancy center in diamond. Time-dependent density functional theory is the standard tool at this size but describes multireference systems poorly. A closed-shell reference is inadequate for a multiconfigurational ground state, and the adiabatic kernel excludes doubly excited configurations. The particle--particle random phase approximation (\pprpa{}) changes the particle number rather than the spin. Singlet and triplet states descend from one closed-shell reference, and double excitations enter on the same footing as single ones. Computational cost has confined \pprpa{} to small systems, and the two-electron integrals that drive it are factorized through an auxiliary basis. We develop \pprpa{}--as, which reduces that basis to a minimal set of atom-centered Gaussian shells whose exponents follow from free-atom radii and one global scaling factor. The approximation enters only the excited-state step, so both calculations proceed from the same self-consistent reference. We test molecules and clusters of up to 286 atoms, including heavy elements, and seven point defects in four periodic hosts, obtaining both multiplicities and their splitting without symmetry breaking or spin projection. We optimize the scaling factor and give guidance on choosing it. An order-of-magnitude smaller auxiliary basis delivers a speed-up of up to eightfold while staying within $0.03$~eV of conventional \pprpa{}, bringing diradical nanoclusters and periodic systems within reach.

### First-Principles Calculations of the Electron-Hole-Pair Creation Energy in Semiconductors and Dielectrics
- **arXiv**: [2610.16949](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16949)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16949.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: First-Principles Calculations of the Electron-Hole-Pair Creation Energy in Semiconductors and Dielectrics
- **Authors**: Dallin O. Nielsen, Massimo V. Fischetti
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, physics.comp-ph
- **TL;DR**: 采用DFT+TDDFT+全带蒙特卡洛计算多种半导体/介电材料电子-空穴对产生能，Si在GW/BSE修正后达3.50 eV/pair，与实验吻合。

We present first-principles calculations of the electron-hole-pair creation energy in semiconductors and dielectrics, focusing in particular on AlN, 3C-SiC, 4H-SiC, Si, and SiO2. We have employed density functional theory (DFT) to calculate the band structure, density-functional perturbation theory to obtain the phonon dispersion, and time-dependent DFT (TDDFT) to calculate the electron energy loss function (via the dielectric function). We performed full-band Monte Carlo simulations of electron thermalization for energies up to ~100 eV and calculated the average energy required to excite an electron-hole pair. We calculated pair-creation energies of approximately 12.2, 6.5, 7.3, 3.10, and 16.7 eV/pair for AlN, 3C-SiC, 4H-SiC, Si, and SiO2, respectively. These values are in good agreement with measured data, with the largest discrepancy being that of Si (about 14.9% lower than the accepted experimental value of ~3.6 eV/pair). To improve the agreement for Si, we used the GW approach and the Bethe-Salpeter equation (BSE) to calculate the quasiparticle shifts of the electronic band structure and the dielectric function, respectively. Finally, using the GW/BSE data, we calculated a creation energy of ~3.50 eV/pair. We find that the marked improvement in the accuracy of the calculated creation energy, when using the GW/BSE approach, is primarily attributable to the slight decrease in the magnitude of the electron energy-loss function when using the GW/BSE approach for energies below ~10-15 eV. Overall, it is clear that the competition between phonon emission and ionization near the intersection of the phonon scattering and ionization rates is largely responsible for determining the value of the creation energy, with the other major factor being the location and shape of the plasmon peak in the energy-loss function.

### Vibrational Fingerprints of Competing Structural and Polar Phases in CuInP2S6
- **arXiv**: [2610.16091](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16091)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16091.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Vibrational Fingerprints of Competing Structural and Polar Phases in CuInP2S6
- **Authors**: Pegah Mohammadi, Sobhit Singh
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 本文用第一性原理DFT计算系统研究了CuInP2S6各候选相（顺电C2/c、P-31c，铁电Cc、P31c，反铁电P-1、P-3）的振动性质，建立了可区分各相的拉曼/红外指纹及拉曼选择定则，为实验相识别提供理论框架。

Layered van der Waals CuInP2S6 crystals exhibit a rich sequence of temperature- and pressure-driven phase transitions, giving rise to multiple structural phases with distinct physical properties. Despite extensive experimental and theoretical efforts, unambiguous identification of these phases remains challenging because of their structural similarities and complex vibrational responses. In this work, we employ first-principles density functional theory calculations to systematically investigate the vibrational properties of the reference paraelectric C2/c and P-31c phases and their corresponding dynamically stable ferroelectric Cc and P31c phases, as well as the antiferroelectric P-1 and P-3 phases. From calculated phonon spectra and symmetry analysis, we determine the Raman- and infrared-active modes of each candidate structure and identify characteristic vibrational fingerprints that enable their phase determination through vibrational spectroscopy. Furthermore, using Porto notation, we establish polarization- and crystal-orientation-dependent Raman selection rules for different backscattering geometries and identify the modes that are expected to emerge or vanish under specific experimental configurations. Thus, our results provide a comprehensive theoretical framework for interpreting Raman and infrared measurements of CuInP2S6 and for reliably identifying its competing structural, ferroelectric, and antiferroelectric phases across temperature- and pressure-driven phase transitions.

### Spatial Engineering of Topological Quantum Matter with Structured Light
- **arXiv**: [2610.17181](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17181)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17181.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Spatial Engineering of Topological Quantum Matter with Structured Light
- **Authors**: Matias Mustonen, Ali G. Moghaddam, Teemu Ojanen, Marco Ornigotti
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, physics.optics
- **TL;DR**: 结构化光可用于局域调控物质的拓扑性质，实现无需材料边界的空间拓扑图案化。

Structured light provides a powerful means of controlling matter while itself exhibiting nontrivial spatial topology. Here, we show that these spatial structures can be harnessed to locally engineer the topological properties of matter. In a paradigmatic example of a topological insulator with Dirac surface states driven by an optical skyrmion beam, we show that structured light spatially tailors the effective Hamiltonian, yielding position-dependent topological gaps and Dirac velocities. The resulting gap modulation can create topological and trivial regions separated by optically defined interfaces, without introducing material boundaries or chemical modifications. We further show that the geometry of these interfaces can be controlled through the spatial structure of the light, in particular its orbital angular momentum. Our results establish structured light as a route to dynamically pattern topological phases and open a broader perspective on the interplay between topology in light and matter, including the possibility of transferring topological characteristics between the two.

### Angle-Resolved Photoemission as a Sensitive Probe of Surface Quality: Sputtered versus Annealed Cu(001)
- **arXiv**: [2610.16421](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16421)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16421.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Angle-Resolved Photoemission as a Sensitive Probe of Surface Quality: Sputtered versus Annealed Cu(001)
- **Authors**: Hyeonhee Na, Jeonghwan Oh, Jinju Lee, Hanjun Sim, Myeongun Go, Jimin Sohn, Jeonggu Kang, Byungmin Sohn
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该文对比Cu(001)在四种表面处理阶段（初始、6 K老化24小时、Ar⁺溅射、退火）的LEED、XPS与ARPES结果，发现溅射面与退火面LEED和Cu 2p谱几乎一致，但溅射面ARPES呈动量无关谱、色散消失，退火后恢复。提示干净的LEED图样与核能级谱并不能保证表面适合ARPES。

Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) requires a well-ordered surface, whose quality is routinely judged from low-energy electron diffraction (LEED) and x-ray photoelectron spectroscopy (XPS). We compare LEED, XPS, and ARPES of a Cu(001) single crystal measured in situ at four stages of surface preparation: as-prepared, after aging for 24 h at 6 K, after Ar$^+$ sputtering, and after annealing. The sputtered and annealed surfaces show nearly identical $(1\times1)$ LEED patterns and Cu 2$p$ core levels, but the sputtered surface yields a momentum-independent ARPES spectrum, and the band dispersion recovers after annealing. We attribute this contrast to how each probe responds to sputtering-induced defects: LEED reflects the average lattice, which remains periodic, whereas ARPES reflects the electronic states, whose crystal momentum is no longer well defined. Our results indicate that a sharp LEED pattern and clean core-level spectra do not guarantee a surface suitable for ARPES.

### Confinement-deconfinement transitions in the dimer-loop models on two-dimensional non-bipartite lattices
- **arXiv**: [2610.16676](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16676)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16676.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Confinement-deconfinement transitions in the dimer-loop models on two-dimensional non-bipartite lattices
- **Authors**: Jay Pandey, Souvik Kundu, Kedar Damle
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 该文提出在非二分晶格（kagome、三角晶格）上的dimer-loop模型中也可存在一种拓扑禁闭相变：两相均为短程关联液体，其拓扑区别由Z2绕数而非无散极化场通量刻画，但该相变仍属Ising普适类。

Recent work [Kundu, Damle, Phys. Rev. X 15, 011018 (2025)] has demonstrated that a class of $S=1$ kagome lattice antiferromagnets with competing anisotropies can exhibit unusual low-temperature behavior that is best described in terms of the statistical mechanics of an interesting interpolation between the fully-packed dimer model and the fully-packed loop model on the bipartite honeycomb lattice. On this and other bipartite lattices in two and three dimension, such fully-packed dimer-loop models generically have two distinct power-law correlated Coulomb phases, both of which admit a low-energy description in terms of a fluctuating divergence-free polarization vector field [Phys. Rev. X 15, 011018 (2025); arXiv:2510.11607]. The transition between these phases corresponds to a confinement transition for the $Z_2$ part of the corresponding $U(1)$ flux [Phys. Rev. X 15, 011018 (2025); arXiv:2510.11607]. Here we demonstrate that a variant of such a topological confinement transition can also exist in dimer-loop models on non-bipartite lattices such as the kagome and triangular lattices although the low-energy description does not feature any conserved $U(1)$ flux. In these non-bipartite dimer-loop models, both phases are short-range correlated liquids, and the topological distinction between them is in terms of a $Z_2$-valued winding number instead of the flux of a divergence-free polarization field. In spite of this key difference, this transition appears to be in the same Ising universality class as the analogous transition of the bipartite dimer-loop models. These kagome and triangular lattice dimer-loop models correspond respectively to the low-energy limit of a class of $S=1$ antiferromagnets with competing anisotropies on the ruby lattice comprising a corner-sharing arrangement of fully-frustrated squares and a lattice of corner-sharing fully-frustrated hexagons.

### Quantitative Impact of Occupation Smearing Methods on Conventional Superconductivity: from Electronic Structure through Lattice Dynamics to Electron-Phonon Coupling
- **arXiv**: [2610.18185](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18185)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18185.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Quantitative Impact of Occupation Smearing Methods on Conventional Superconductivity: from Electronic Structure through Lattice Dynamics to Electron-Phonon Coupling
- **Authors**: Zhongyu Wan
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con
- **TL;DR**: 以CsH6为基准，系统量化了金属体系第一性原理计算中展宽函数及其宽度对声子媒介超导预测的影响，并给出客观选取展宽宽度的标度关系。

In first-principles calculations of metallic systems, the discrete electronic mesh in the Brillouin zone necessitates a smearing function and its width (e.g., the widely used 0.02 Ry Methfessel-Paxton scheme) to enforce continuity of energy and occupation at the Fermi surface. However, the quantitative impact of such empirical parameters on phonon-mediated superconductivity has long been overlooked, leading to their habitual selection by mere convention. Here, using hexahydride CsH6 as a benchmark, we systematically investigate the magnitude and propagation mechanism of smearing-induced errors against the parameter-free optimized tetrahedron method. Long-tailed smearing delocalizes the occupation window, causing substantial Fermi-level shifts, flattening of the density of states, and hardening phonon modes, thereby weakening electron-phonon coupling. Conversely, short-tailed smearing suffers from insufficient sampling, producing density-of-states oscillations and enhanced Fermi-surface nesting; via amplified electronic screening and Peierls instabilities, this softens acoustic modes and generates imaginary frequencies, causing CsH6 to be dynamically unstable and discarded. However, the optimized tetrahedron method reveals that CsH6 is dynamically stable under ambient pressure with a remarkable superconducting transition temperature of 121 K. To prevent subjective empirical parameters from obscuring theoretically promising high-temperature superconductors, we derive a simple scaling relation for the smearing width, applicable to different smearing functions, from the free-electron-gas model, offering an objective, precise, and efficient protocol for parameter selection in high-throughput screening.

### Can Machine Learning Potentials Be More Accurate Than the Methods They Are Trained On?
- **arXiv**: [2610.18027](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18027)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18027.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Can Machine Learning Potentials Be More Accurate Than the Methods They Are Trained On?
- **Authors**: Pavlo O. Dral, Yi-Fan Hou, Yuxinxin Chen, Hassan Nawaz, Mateusz Witkowski, Szymon Śmiga, Ireneusz Grabowski
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph, cs.AI, cs.LG, physics.comp-ph
- **TL;DR**: 机器学习势(MLP)的精度通常被认为受限于训练方法，本文提出通过跨"第三空间"——量子化学方法空间——泛化，实现非经验性地超越训练方法精度的"方法可迁移MLP"。

Although machine learning potentials (MLPs) generalize across configurational and chemical compound spaces, their accuracy is commonly believed to be limited by their training methods, with improvements beyond this limit achieved so far only by refitting to experimental observables. In this Perspective, we argue that MLPs can also be made more accurate than their training methods non-empirically by generalizing across a third space, that of quantum-chemical methods. We propose routes to method-transferable MLPs that predict at unseen methods, with working implementations in both density-functional and wavefunction-based theories showing that they can be more accurate than any of their training methods. Aiming beyond the accuracy of the training methods could also make universal MLPs more accurate than single-method models, even when the method-transferable MLPs remain less accurate than those QM methods. Method-transferable MLPs will shape the construction of training datasets and the evaluation of models, pushing the accuracy limits of chemical simulations.

### Honeycomb Crystal in a Mass-Asymmetric Electron-Hole Bilayer
- **arXiv**: [2610.17030](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17030)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17030.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Honeycomb Crystal in a Mass-Asymmetric Electron-Hole Bilayer
- **Authors**: Shengdu Chai, Wangqian Miao, Yuqiang Li, Lei Wang, Xi Dai, X. C. Xie
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cs.LG, physics.comp-ph
- **TL;DR**: 在强质量不对称电子-空穴双层中，关联的可移动电子可克服三角晶格偏好，稳定出蜂窝状电子-空穴晶格，并呈现丰富的自旋与金属-绝缘体物态。

A two-dimensional electron gas in a uniform neutralizing background forms a triangular Wigner crystal at low density. In a strongly mass-asymmetric electron-hole bilayer, localized heavy holes instead provide a nonuniform positive background that supports an electron crystal at densities two orders of magnitude higher than for a homogeneous electron gas in the same material. Here we show that correlated mobile electrons can overturn the preference for triangular order, stabilizing a honeycomb electron-hole crystal. Using the powerful real-space neural-network variational Monte Carlo in the Born-Oppenheimer limit with fixed holes, we find that increasing the density drives a transition from a triangular electron-hole crystal to a honeycomb crystal, which eventually melts into itinerant electrons coexisting with the honeycomb or the triangular hole lattice at fixed interlayer separation. Relative to triangular order, honeycomb order emerges when gains in electronic kinetic energy and electron-hole attraction outweigh its higher intralayer Coulomb repulsion, a threshold never reached in the rigid point-dipole model. Moreover, the honeycomb hole lattice supports paramagnetic, antiferromagnetic, and stripe-ordered spin states. Complex polarization indicates a metal-insulator transition, while the states on the honeycomb hole lattice are consistent with a stripe-ordered Dirac semimetal and paramagnetic or antiferromagnetic Mott insulators via mapping to the half-filled Hubbard model. We further propose a BLG/hBN/WS$_2$-WSe$_2$ heterostructure as a platform for the predicted honeycomb crystal, at an experimentally feasible density $n\sim10^{11}~\text{cm}^{-2}$.

### Decoupling Trap States in Amorphous Oxide Semiconductor Transistor Using a Conductive-Semiconducting-Conductive MOS Structure
- **arXiv**: [2610.16438](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16438)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16438.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Decoupling Trap States in Amorphous Oxide Semiconductor Transistor Using a Conductive-Semiconducting-Conductive MOS Structure
- **Authors**: Kai Chen, Wei Zhang, Xuanyao Fong, Shengman Li, Yunlong Li
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 提出一种非晶氧化物半导体(AOS)基"导电-半导体-导电"(CSC)MOS测试结构，可解耦并分辨AOS晶体管中不同陷阱引起的电导损失，获得偏压分辨的陷阱分布。

We propose a novel amorphous oxide semiconductor (AOS)-based conductive-semiconducting-conductive (CSC) MOS test structure to effectively sense, discriminate, and analyze distinct trap-induced conductance losses in AOS transistors. In conventional test structures, non-ideal metal-AOS contacts convolute the conductance loss generated by various trap states. The CSC architecture mitigates these contact-related artifacts, enabling the decoupling of interface states and shallow states. Furthermore, the contributions from leakage, series resistance, and device-geometry effects are identified and calibrated. Our results yield bias-resolved trap profiles and reveal three distinct regimes: (1) In the depletion region, interface states dominate the conductance loss. These states exhibit sub-microsecond time constants and their density increases slightly with deeper depletion, which is associated with donor-like states. (2) From weak to moderate accumulation, shallow states become the primary contributor, displaying a Gaussian-like volumetric density-of-states distribution with response times spanning from microseconds to nanoseconds. (3) In the strong accumulation regime, interface states regain dominance with an increased state quantity, while their time constants remain invariant. These bias-resolved profiles clarify how different trap populations impact AOS transistors under distinct operating conditions, providing intuitive metrics for understanding reliability degradation. Consequently, the CSC structure offers a simple, scalable, and robust platform for trap characterization in AOS transistors.

### Revealing topological protection through unprotected operations in a Majorana parity qubit
- **arXiv**: [2610.17198](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17198)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17198.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Revealing topological protection through unprotected operations in a Majorana parity qubit
- **Authors**: Sebastian Miles, Juan Daniel Torres Luna, Francesco Zatelli, Nick van Loo, Antonio Lombardi, Vincent P. M. Sietses, Florian J. Bennebroek Evertsz', Bart Roovers, Leo P. Kouwenhoven, Michael Wimmer
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, quant-ph
- **TL;DR**: 该论文提出一种解析理论，说明如何通过"未受保护的"量子比特操作（Rabi与Ramsey实验）来显现双Kitaev链中Majorana宇称量子比特的指数保护，并定量预测随链长的标度行为。

A recent experiment reported coherent parity oscillations between minimal Kitaev chains, demonstrating a Majorana parity qubit (arXiv:2607.09511). Operations on this qubit are controlled by coupling two Kitaev chains and are intrinsically unprotected against noise in the coupling strength. Here we show how the exponential protection in the underlying chains can nevertheless be revealed through unprotected qubit operations. To this end, we develop an analytical theory of quasistatic dephasing for two coupled $N$-site Kitaev chains at the sweet spot. In Rabi experiments, protection can be resolved by adapting the operating point with chain length: depending on the coupling architecture, it appears as an exponentially increasing optimized number of coherent oscillations or as a longer coherence time. Ramsey experiments show an exponentially increasing coherence time, provided the chains can be sufficiently decoupled. Our analytical theory takes into account the non-Gaussian splitting statistics of the Kitaev chains, allowing us to quantitatively predict the scaling with chain length. Moreover, we determine how residual noise and imperfections limit the range of chain lengths over which this scaling can be observed. Our results show how qubit dynamics can be used to probe Majorana protection in experimentally realistic devices.

### Modelling the dynamics of the martensitic transformation in Ni$_2$MnGa with machine-learning force-fields
- **arXiv**: [2610.16467](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16467)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16467.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Modelling the dynamics of the martensitic transformation in Ni$_2$MnGa with machine-learning force-fields
- **Authors**: Markus E. Gruner, Yulia Klunnikova, Mike J. Bruckhoff, Konstatin P. Skokov, Olga N. Miroshkina
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 本文通过构建基于DFT训练的机器学习力场（ML-FF），在保持近DFT精度的同时实现了Ni₂MnGa磁控形状记忆合金马氏体相变的大尺度有限温度分子动力学模拟。

The martensitic transformations in stoichiometric Ni$_2$MnGa and related Heusler alloys are governed by a delicate interplay between electronic structure, magnetism, and lattice dynamics. While density functional theory (DFT) has successfully mapped the energy landscape of modulated martensites, it remains computationally prohibitive for capturing finite-temperature dynamics across relevant length and time scales. Here, we bridge this gap by constructing DFT-trained machine-learning force-fields (ML-FF) interpolated from ab initio molecular dynamics. We demonstrate that ML-FF trained on DFT data can reproduce key features of Ni$_2$MnGa, including the soft TA$_2$ phonon and a preference for nanotwinned martensites. Using classical molecular dynamics simulations, we explore the impact of adaptive nanotwinning on the austenite-martensite transition, which facilitates the dynamics of both, forward and backward transformation. We demonstrate, that the negative twin boundary energy along the entire transformations path predicted by DFT for $T=0$, supports the formation of ordered modulated martensites at finite temperatures. Our results pave the way for large-scale simulations of magnetic shape memory alloys with near-DFT accuracy.

### Destructive Interference of a Charge Density Wave in the Skyrmion Magnet GdRu2Si2
- **arXiv**: [2610.16682](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16682)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16682.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Destructive Interference of a Charge Density Wave in the Skyrmion Magnet GdRu2Si2
- **Authors**: G. D. A. Wood, D. A. Mayoh, F. Orlandi, A. Bombardi, D. D. Khalyavin, P. Manuel, S. H. Moody, P. D. Hatton, R. D. Johnson, T. W. Chamberlain, O. A. Petrenko, M. R. Lees, G. Balakrishnan
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: GdRu2Si2中，电荷密度波诱导的高阶交换相互作用可选择多Q基态；用共振X射线散射与中子衍射测得自旋-电荷耦合，一个含单一临界指数和两个有序温度的极小朗道模型即可重现全部六个序参量，并揭示自旋密度波分量驱动电荷有序、而磁螺旋的出现通过相消干涉抑制之。

In skyrmion magnets, charge density waves induce higher-order exchange interactions that can select multi-Q ground states. Using resonant X-ray scattering and neutron diffraction, we measure temperature-dependent spin-charge coupling in GdRu2Si2. A minimal Landau model with one critical exponent and two ordering temperatures reproduces all six observed order parameters. Spin-density wave components of the multi-Q ground state drive charge ordering, but the onset of a magnetic helix suppresses it via destructive interference.

### Phonon-Mediated Superconductivity near Phase Boundaries in Rhombohedral Multilayer Graphene
- **arXiv**: [2610.18286](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18286)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18286.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Phonon-Mediated Superconductivity near Phase Boundaries in Rhombohedral Multilayer Graphene
- **Authors**: Konstantinos Sourounis, Peter M. Oppeneer
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con
- **TL;DR**: 该文用第一性原理结合自洽Migdal-Eliashberg方程，研究菱方多层石墨烯中声子媒介超导，可复现实验观测的多个超导区域并预测新超导相。

The recent observation of superconductivity in untwisted multilayer graphene has sparked intense interest in its microscopic origin. As the displacement field and carrier density are varied, superconductivity emerges in several distinct regions with different temperature and magnetic-field responses. This behavior has been attributed to the presence of distinct electronic phases, raising the possibility that different superconducting regions originate from different microscopic mechanisms. While purely electronic pairing mechanisms have been extensively explored, an in-depth examination of phonon-mediated superconductivity is still missing. Here, we solve the coupled selfconsistent Migdal-Eliashberg equations using input from first-principles calculations and incorporating interaction-driven symmetry breaking of the electronic ground state. Varying the carrier density and displacement field, we reproduce the experimentally observed superconducting regions with comparable superconducting properties such as gap and $T_c$ and predict additional, as-yet undiscovered superconducting phases. Our findings exemplify that several superconductivity regions in rhombohedral graphene can be satisfactorily explained by phonon-mediated pairing.

### QUPEX: A Streamlit-Based Framework for Visualizing Quantum-Mechanical Property Spaces of Organic Molecules
- **arXiv**: [2610.18100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18100)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18100.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: QUPEX: A Streamlit-Based Framework for Visualizing Quantum-Mechanical Property Spaces of Organic Molecules
- **Authors**: Mirtha Pillaca Quispe, Alejandra Hinostroza Caldas, Leonardo Medrano Sandonas
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph
- **TL;DR**: QUPEX：基于 Streamlit 的开源交互式框架，用于量子力学性质空间探索，集成分子可视化、统计分析、聚类与 XGBoost+SHAP 建模，并在 QM7-X 子集（41,537 个分子、24 种 QM 性质）上演示。

The growing availability of quantum-mechanical (QM) datasets offers new opportunities to investigate structure-property and property-property relationships in drug-like molecules using data-driven methods. However, interactive tools that integrate molecular visualization, statistical analysis of QM properties, clustering, and machine learning (ML) remain limited, despite their value in facilitating dataset exploration and interpretation. To address this gap, we introduce QUPEX (QUantum Property EXplorer), an open-source Streamlit-based framework for the interactive exploration of QM property spaces in molecular systems. As a demonstration, we apply QUPEX to a subset of the QM7-X dataset comprising 41,537 equilibrium molecular structures containing up to seven non-hydrogen atoms (C, N, O, S, and Cl) and their 24 associated QM properties. In addition to property-space analysis, QUPEX incorporates an XGBoost regression pipeline for predicting PBE0 dipole moments from tight-binding-derived electronic descriptors, together with model evaluation and SHAP-based interpretability functionality. By combining interactive molecular visualization, data analysis, and predictive modeling, QUPEX provides an accessible and extensible environment for investigating molecular datasets and identifying patterns in their structural and electronic properties. These capabilities can support the exploration of chemical space and inform the rational design of drug-like molecules with targeted QM properties.

### Can Universal Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials Capture Small Polarons? A Geometry-Based Benchmark in Transition-Metal Oxides
- **arXiv**: [2610.17352](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17352)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17352.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Can Universal Machine-Learning Interatomic Potentials Capture Small Polarons? A Geometry-Based Benchmark in Transition-Metal Oxides
- **Authors**: Vladislav Korostelev, Pjotrs Žguns, Konstantin Klyukin
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, physics.chem-ph, physics.comp-ph
- **TL;DR**: 本文系统评估了通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIPs）描述过渡金属氧化物中小极化子的能力，发现其表现高度依赖训练数据与电子关联处理方式，普遍无法稳定空间分离的极化子-给体构型。

Small polarons, formed when excess electrons become localized through lattice distortions, play a critical role in charge transport, catalysis, and degradation processes in transition-metal (TM) oxides. Whether universal machine-learning interatomic potentials (uMLIPs), which lack explicit electronic degrees of freedom, can describe them remains unclear. Here, we investigate H-induced electron localization across a range of TM oxides using a geometric descriptor based on TM-O bond-length expansion (Z-score) to identify polaronic distortions. We find that uMLIPs exhibit varying degrees of electron localization, from delocalized states to metastable small polarons, with their behavior strongly dependent on the training data and treatment of electronic correlations. In particular, the absence of Hubbard U corrections for specific TM cations in the training data leads to electron delocalization, in contrast to DFT+U predictions. Although some uMLIPs reproduce polaron hopping barriers during coupled proton-polaron transfer, all of them fail to stabilize spatially separated polaron-donor configurations, instead favoring localization near the H dopant.

### Theoretical Investigation of Oxygen-Vacancy Diffusion in SrTiO$_3$ using Neural Network Potentials
- **arXiv**: [2610.16521](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16521)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16521.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Theoretical Investigation of Oxygen-Vacancy Diffusion in SrTiO$_3$ using Neural Network Potentials
- **Authors**: Kazutaka Nishiguchi, Ryota Yamamoto, Seiichiro Ten-no
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该论文开发了基于AIMD数据训练的神经网络势(NNP)，用于pristine与含氧空位STO，实现大尺度长时间MD模拟，准确再现DFT能量/力/virial及空位形成能，并给出与实验吻合的氧空位扩散系数与迁移焓(0.64 eV vs 0.67 eV)。

Oxygen-vacancy formation and diffusion play important roles in determining the electronic and atomic transport properties of SrTiO$_3$ (STO). Accurate analysis of such point defects requires sufficiently large-scale and long-time molecular dynamics (MD) simulations; however, direct investigations of oxygen-vacancy dynamics using {\it ab initio} MD (AIMD) based on density functional theory (DFT) are limited to relatively small systems and short time scales because of their high computational cost. In this study, we develop a neural network potential (NNP) for pristine and oxygen-deficient STO using a reference dataset generated from AIMD simulations. The trained NNP model accurately reproduces the DFT reference energies, atomic forces, and virials for both pristine STO and STO containing an oxygen vacancy. The NNP also reproduces the supercell-size dependence of the total energies and oxygen-vacancy formation energies, including the formation energy extrapolated to the dilute limit. Large-scale and long-time MD simulations using the NNP (NNP-MD) are then performed to investigate oxygen-vacancy diffusion. The resulting oxygen-vacancy diffusion coefficients show substantially better agreement with experimental data than those obtained from conventional classical MD simulations. An Arrhenius analysis yields an oxygen-vacancy migration enthalpy of 0.64 eV, in close agreement with the experimental value of 0.67 eV. These results demonstrate that NNPs trained on first-principles data provide an accurate and computationally efficient framework for investigating defect formation and long-time vacancy transport in perovskite oxides.

### Energy levels of screened charged impurity in gapped graphene under magnetic field
- **arXiv**: [2610.17492](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17492)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17492.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Energy levels of screened charged impurity in gapped graphene under magnetic field
- **Authors**: Andrii Iurov, V. P. Gusynin
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 用WKB方法研究带隙石墨烯中电荷杂质与磁场共同作用下的朗道能级修正，并考虑金属栅极屏蔽效应。

In this paper, we present a comprehensive theoretical study of the electronic states in gapped graphene in the presence of a charge impurity and a magnetic field. We focus on the impurity-induced modification of the Landau levels under the influence of an attractive or repulsive Coulomb potential. Since analytical solutions of the Dirac equation are not available in this case, the study is carried out using the Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin (WKB) approach. Special attention is paid to the screening of the Coulomb potential in the presence of metallic gates situated at some distances from graphene sheet, as is commonly the case in experiments. Using both analytical and numerical methods, we determine the energy spectrum as a function of the magnetic field strength and impurity charge for screened and unscreened Coulomb potentials. Our results reveal a significant dependence of the energy levels for sample-to-gate distances of less than 50 nm.

### Interlayer Registry Controls Polar Topology in Twisted Bilayer h-BN
- **arXiv**: [2610.16528](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16528)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16528.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Interlayer Registry Controls Polar Topology in Twisted Bilayer h-BN
- **Authors**: Di Fan, Changming Ke, Shi Liu
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该文用第一性原理训练的深度势分子动力学，揭示扭转双层h-BN中由层间配准(registry)控制莫尔极化拓扑的演化规律及其对扭转角、温度、应变的响应。

Twisting van der Waals bilayers creates a moir'e stacking field with periodically varying local interlayer registry, enabling spatially patterned polarization. However, a systematic understanding of how this registry-controlled polar topology evolves with twist angle, temperature, and mechanical strain is still lacking. Here, using first-principles-trained deep-potential molecular dynamics simulations, we show that spatially varying interlayer registry governs the polarization texture and can be directly tuned by twist and relative layer deformation. As the twist angle decreases, the moir'e polarization texture evolves from compact, nearly circular vortex-like patterns to extended triangular AB/BA domains. This crossover arises from enhanced lattice relaxation at small twist angles, which expands low-energy, oppositely polarized AB/BA regions and confines in-plane polarization mainly to domain boundaries. Despite this reconstruction, the local in-plane winding remains tied to interlayer registry and persists up to 1000 K, indicating robust topology against thermal fluctuations. We further show that the mechanical response is dictated by whether deformation changes the relative registry: homogeneous deformation of both layers produces only weak distortions, whereas layer-selective deformation strongly reconstructs the moiré polarization texture. A mismatch-matrix registry-mapping model is developed to capture this response, explaining deformation-induced changes in vortex radii at larger twist angles and triangular-domain geometry at smaller twist angles. These results identify interlayer registry as the central control parameter for forming and tuning moir'e polar topology in twisted bilayer h-BN.

### Quantum Ising transition on the Cayley tree: boundary criticality, universality, and an effective Bethe line
- **arXiv**: [2610.16774](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16774)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16774.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Quantum Ising transition on the Cayley tree: boundary criticality, universality, and an effective Bethe line
- **Authors**: Pietro Richelli, Natalia Chepiga
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 利用树张量网络模拟有限Cayley树上横场Ising模型，发现广泛边界导致边界驱动的连续相变（h_x^c≈1.76），并可通过调节局域边界场恢复无边界Bethe晶格的均场行为。

We investigate quantum criticality in the transverse-field Ising model on finite Cayley trees and demonstrate that their extensive boundary fundamentally changes the nature of the quantum phase transition compared with the infinite, boundary-free Bethe lattice. Using tree tensor-network simulations for systems with up to $N=12286$ sites, we identify a boundary-driven continuous transition at $h_x^c\approx1.76$, distinct from the mean-field Bethe-lattice transition at $h_x^c\approx2.23$. We characterize this transition through finite-size scaling of the magnetization and magnetic susceptibility and extract a consistent set of critical exponents. By introducing next-nearest-neighbor interactions, we demonstrate the robustness of the extracted critical exponents, providing numerical argument for the Ising boundary universality class on the Cayley tree. Furthermore, we show that the physics of the boundary-free Bethe lattice can be efficiently recovered on a finite Cayley tree by fine-tuning a local boundary field. The resulting \textit{effective Bethe line}, whose existence is tied to the mean-field nature of the bulk transition, effectively screens the extensive boundary and restores both the critical point and the mean-field critical scaling of the infinite Bethe lattice.

### The role of dependency range in thermally coupled superconducting networks
- **arXiv**: [2610.18418](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18418)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18418.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: The role of dependency range in thermally coupled superconducting networks
- **Authors**: Nahala Yadid, Bnaya Gross, Shlomo Havlin, Nadav M. Shnerb
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con
- **TL;DR**: 研究热耦合非晶超导网络，发现热驱动依赖随距离同时展宽与减弱，长程热耦合在升温时可"稳定"超导相，与几何模型的失稳结论相反，级联呈一维前沿各向异性传播。

Interdependent networks can be highly fragile because dependency links amplify local failures into cascades that trigger global collapse. Much of the theoretical understanding comes from geometric network models, where dependency range is imposed externally and coupling strength is distance independent, and it is unclear how these insights apply to real materials, where interdependence arises from physical transport. Here we study thermally coupled amorphous superconducting networks, in which dependencies emerge from current redistribution and heat-mediated interactions governed by thermal diffusivity. Using an experimentally calibrated model of resistively shunted Josephson junctions, we show the system reproduces the three canonical transition regimes: continuous, nucleation-dominated, and mixed-order. However, because heat-driven dependency simultaneously broadens and weakens with distance, long-range thermal coupling can stabilize the superconducting phase upon heating, in contrast with its destabilizing role in geometric models. Cascades are anisotropic, with one-dimensional front propagation. These results offer an experimentally testable perspective complementing the geometric framework.

### Photoinduced Modulation of Rectification and Charge Transport in Diarylethene-Viologen Molecular Junction
- **arXiv**: [2610.18251](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18251)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18251.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Photoinduced Modulation of Rectification and Charge Transport in Diarylethene-Viologen Molecular Junction
- **Authors**: Sofyan Tahri, Samuel Decorps, Stephane Lenfant, Denis Frath, Imen Hnid
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph
- **TL;DR**: 研究Au/DAEVIO/Ti/Au分子结（二芳基乙烯-紫精电接枝层，12-16 nm），初始"关"态具低电流密度与强整流（1V下整流比300）；385 nm原位光照后+1V电流密度提升200倍、整流比降至2，并产生短路电流+0.006 mA cm⁻²与开路电压-0.45 V。

Large-area molecular junctions combining photochromic and redox-active functionalities provide an attractive platform for controlling charge transport by light. In this work, we study Au/DAEVIO/Ti/Au molecular junctions incorporated with electro-grafted layers of diarylethene-viologen with thicknesses of 12-16 nm. In the initial OFF state, the junctions exhibit low current densities and strong rectification, with a rectification ratio of 300 at 1V. Temperature-dependent measurements reveal a thermally assisted contribution at higher temperatures and a much weaker temperature dependence below 160-180K, consistent with multistep charge transport through molecular and/or interfacial states rather than direct coherent tunneling across the entire molecular layer. Under continuous in situ 385 nm irradiation, the electrical response is strongly and asymmetrically modified. The current density at +1V increases by a factor of 200, while the negative-bias current is only weakly modified, reducing the rectification ratio from 300 to 2. Illumination also produces a nonzero short-circuit current density of + 0.006mA cm-2 and an open-circuit voltage of -0.45V. These results indicate that irradiation simultaneously modifies molecular charge transport and generates a measurable photoelectric response. This behavior is consistent with light-induced changes in electronic coupling associated with DAE photocyclization, combined with photoinduced charge generation and field-dependent charge separation involving the redox-active viologen units and asymmetric electrode interfaces.

### A grand potential for molten-salt electrochemical phase diagrams from first principles
- **arXiv**: [2610.17611](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17611)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17611.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: A grand potential for molten-salt electrochemical phase diagrams from first principles
- **Authors**: Lorenzo Bastonero, Lucio Colombi Ciacchi, Nicola Marzari
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, physics.chem-ph, physics.comp-ph
- **TL;DR**: 提出一种基于第一性原理构建多组分体系电化学相图（电化学势图/grand potential diagram）的框架，用于加速熔盐电解中电极与电解质的设计与材料筛选。

The clean-energy transition is driving unprecedented demand for metals, whose extraction is among the largest industrial sources of carbon emissions. Molten-salt electrolysis offers a path toward carbon-free production of aluminum, iron, titanium, and critical metals. However, corrosive operating conditions and complex, multicomponent feedstocks make experimental development costly and challenging. Here, we introduce a first-principles framework for constructing electrochemical phase diagrams of multicomponent systems to accelerate electrode and electrolyte design and guide the selection of compatible materials and operating conditions for electrochemical cells. We derive a grand potential that generates these diagrams at scale, and supply free energies using first-principles data obtained through a fine-tuned foundation machine-learned interatomic potential. For silicon in molten CaCl$_2$, the construction not only recovers the experimental diagram when using the first-principles framework on a limited set of structures, but also predicts further domains when extended to a larger set of crystal structures. Finally, we demonstrate the predictive power of the approach by screening ten alkali and alkaline-earth halide electrolytes for aluminum electrowinning. Three thermodynamic criteria identify NaF, where cryolite emerges as the stable aluminum-bearing phase, recovering the chemistry that Hall and Héroult established empirically in 1886. The framework provides a first-principles basis for selecting compatible electrodes, electrolytes, and operating conditions toward low-carbon metal production from complex source materials.

### Real-space obstruction control of the shift current in two-dimensional Quantum Spin Hall insulators
- **arXiv**: [2610.16544](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16544)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16544.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Real-space obstruction control of the shift current in two-dimensional Quantum Spin Hall insulators
- **Authors**: Michele Bagaglini, Mattia Iannetti, Jonas Erhardt, Jörg Schäfer, Ralph Claessen, Giorgio Sangiovanni, Gianni Profeta
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 在三角晶格量子自旋霍尔绝缘体的紧束缚模型中，阻碍型（obstructed）拓扑相相较平庸相具有显著增强的shift current非线性光电流，其起源于不同Wannier中心扇区之间的光学跃迁带来的大实空间位移，并指认indenene为候选材料。

The search for materials exhibiting a large bulk photovoltaic effect has recently expanded to low-band-gap systems capable of operating in the infrared regime, with potential applications in optoelectronics. Among the microscopic mechanisms contributing to this phenomenon, the shift current is one of the dominant sources of photocurrent generation. Owing to its geometric origin, the shift current is not only determined by the optical transition probability but also by the real-space displacement associated with the optical excitation, making non-centrosymmetric materials with obstructed electronic wave functions particularly promising candidates. In this work, using a tight-binding model of a quantum spin Hall insulator on a triangular lattice, we investigate the nonlinear photocurrent across trivial, quantum spin Hall, and higher-order topological insulating phases. We show that obstructed topological phases exhibit a substantially enhanced shift-current response, despite the similar electronic band structure. We identify the origin of this enhancement in optical transitions between states belonging to distinct Wannier-center sectors, which allow for a large real-space displacement upon photoexcitation and, depending on the topological phase, control the sign of the shift current. Our results extend the search for controllable bulk photovoltaic effects to materials characterized by real-space electronic obstruction and identify indenene, a monolayer of In on SiC(0001), as a promising candidate for realizing this phenomenon.

### A Full-Spectrum Theory of Moiré Superlattices
- **arXiv**: [2610.17749](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17749)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17749.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: A Full-Spectrum Theory of Moiré Superlattices
- **Authors**: Tong Liu, X. R. Wang, Jiansheng Wu
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 提出一种无近似的统一解析框架，用于任意转角/应变莫尔体系的全谱分析。

Conventional theoretical descriptions of moiré superlattices, epitomized by the Bistritzer--MacDonald continuum model, are fundamentally constrained by low-energy Dirac cone approximations and first-shell interlayer coupling truncations. While successful for small-angle twisted bilayer graphene near charge neutrality, these frameworks fail to provide universal analytical solutions for strained lattices, high-energy band behaviors, or arbitrary commensurate geometries. Here, we establish a unified, approximation-free analytical formalism for generalized moiré systems based on three pillars: (i) a rigorous two-dimensional-to-one-dimensional supercell cyclic mapping, (ii) exact block-circulant diagonalization of intralayer Hamiltonians, and (iii) quasi-degenerate Löwdin perturbation theory centered on arbitrary energy degeneracy points across the full spectrum. Unlike traditional schemes anchored to the Dirac point, our approach enables systematic perturbative expansions around any band crossing, unifying the characterization of low-energy flat bands with high-energy degeneracies. We demonstrate that this framework perfectly recovers the Bistritzer--MacDonald model in the low-energy limit while extending its reach to heterostrained systems and high-energy regimes. By incorporating high-order interlayer coupling shells, we reveal an intrinsic scale self-similarity in moiré lattices and predict the emergence of higher-order magic angles and tunable flat bands driven by secondary degeneracies. This work eliminates the long-standing lack of universal analytical tools for diverse moiré platforms, providing a full-spectrum theoretical foundation for band engineering and the exploration of exotic correlated and topological states in van der Waals heterostructures.

### Domain-wall-to-domain interconversion in a polar conducting oxide
- **arXiv**: [2610.16681](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16681)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16681.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Domain-wall-to-domain interconversion in a polar conducting oxide
- **Authors**: Martin F. Sarott, Majid Ahmadi, Johanna van Gent González, Ewout van der Veer, Mads C. Weber, Jacopo Pinna, Daniel A. Chaney, Arijit Sinha, Hamideh Hassani, Horatio R. J. Cox, Thomas Perrault, Amir Sabet Ghorabaei, Maria A. Loi, Philippe Ghosez, Bart J. Kooi, Beatriz Noheda
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该论文在极性外延WO₃中实现了畴壁到畴的可逆转换，将二维畴壁扩展为三维功能区域，从而在极性（畴）与导电（畴壁）两种互斥性质间可逆切换。

Domain walls in ferroic crystals are topological defects with a symmetry distinct from the domain interior, allowing them to display functionalities that are otherwise forbidden in the host material. Despite their unique properties, domain walls are strictly confined to two-dimensional interfaces, which hinders unveiling their true nature and limits their applicability. Here, we demonstrate reversible dimensionality control by expanding nanoscale domain walls into three-dimensional functional regions in polar epitaxial tungsten trioxide ($\text{WO}_3$). This domain-wall-to-domain interconversion is enabled by the conducting, non-polar domain walls adopting the parent-phase structure. An alternating electric field applied through a scanning probe tip induces localized heating, which expands this domain-wall phase into the adjacent polar domains. This approach enables reversible spatial control to switch between mutually exclusive properties of domains (polarity) and domain walls (conductivity), offering a new paradigm for reconfigurable oxide electronics.

### Magnetic properties and excitation spectrum of the S = 1/2 three-legged spin ladder, LiMoOP2O7
- **arXiv**: [2610.16856](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16856)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16856.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Magnetic properties and excitation spectrum of the S = 1/2 three-legged spin ladder, LiMoOP2O7
- **Authors**: Aly H Abdeldaim, Alexander A. Tsirlin, Jacques Ollivier, Clemens Ritter, Robin S. Perry, Lucy Clark, Gøran J. Nilsen
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 该文结合中子衍射、磁化、比热、非弹性中子散射与DFT，确定了S=1/2的LiMoOP2O7的三腿梯状磁结构、3.5 K反铁磁长程序及主导交换作用。

We report a comprehensive experimental study including density functional theory calculations detailing the magnetic ground state of the $S = 1/2$ Mo$^{5+}$ pyrophosphate, LiMoOP2O7, and its magnetic excitations. Neutron powder diffraction measurements confirm the $P2_1/n$ structural model which is comprised of a three-legged ladder of distorted Mo$^{5+}$ octahedra. Magnetometry and specific heat reveal a long-range magnetic ordering transition at $T_\textrm{N} = 3.5$~K and a field-induced transition at $μ_{0}$H$_\textrm{c}$ = 1 T. This zero-field long-range magnetic order was determined to be a commensurate $κ= 0$ order with an ordered moment that is reduced from the full $S = 1/2$ magnetic moment by 30%. Through inelastic neutron scattering measurements and density functional theory calculations, we define the minimal microscopic model and resolve the spin Hamiltonian up to the seventh leading exchange parameter, resulting in a model with dominant antiferromagnetic leg interactions, weaker ferromagnetic rung interactions, and a significant interlayer ferromagnetic coupling.

### Pairing from repulsive interactions in twisted WSe2 from functional renormalization
- **arXiv**: [2610.17222](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17222)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17222.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Pairing from repulsive interactions in twisted WSe2 from functional renormalization
- **Authors**: Hannes Braun, Dumitru Călugăru, Pietro M. Bonetti, Haoyu Hu, Yan Zhang, Yi Jiang, Quansheng Wu, Kin Fai Mak, Jie Shan, B. Andrei Bernevig, Michael M. Scherer, Laura Classen
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.supr-con
- **TL;DR**: 该文用Hartree–Fock、RPA和fRG研究扭转双层WSe₂在ν=−1、3.65°时的关联电子相，发现混合d波单态与p波三重态的超导不稳定性，其配对胶为谷间相干自旋涨落，且在门屏蔽库仑作用下可延伸至零位移场，与实验一致。

Motivated by the observation of superconductivity in twisted bilayer WSe$_2$ over an extended range of displacement fields, reaching from the correlated insulator down to zero field, we analyze the correlated electron phases of this system at hole filling $ν= -1$ and twist angle $3.65^\circ$ using Hartree--Fock, random phase approximation, and functional renormalization group calculations. We derive a continuum model with gate-screened Coulomb interaction from first principles and compare its instabilities to those of a realistic three-orbital Wannier model with a Hubbard interaction and an artificial single-orbital Hubbard model. In both cases, we find a superconducting instability of mixed $d$-wave singlet and $p$-wave triplet character, arising adjacent to inter-valley-coherent spin-density-wave order in the phase diagram spanned by displacement field and interaction strength. Our additional analysis of the pairing mechanism points to inter-valley coherent spin fluctuations as the pairing glue. The extent of the pairing regime and the associated critical temperatures depend on the model input, and we trace differences to the interaction range entering the projected interaction. Within the continuum model, the gate-screened Coulomb interaction yields pairing down to zero displacement field, bringing theory into agreement with experiment.

### Extrapolating Collisional Rate Coefficients for Molecules of Astrophysical Interest Using Neural Networks
- **arXiv**: [2610.18278](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18278)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18278.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Extrapolating Collisional Rate Coefficients for Molecules of Astrophysical Interest Using Neural Networks
- **Authors**: Yigit Ergen, Jérôme Loreau
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph, astro-ph.GA
- **TL;DR**: 该文用多层感知机从分子量子数、能级与能隙、温度等描述符预测非弹性碰撞的态-态速率系数，可在不进行量子散射计算的情况下扩展已有碰撞激发数据集，对未见转动态的预测平均误差因子约1.1–1.5。

State-to-state rate coefficients for inelastic molecular collisions are essential inputs for non-local thermodynamic equilibrium radiative transfer models, yet their computation using quantum scattering methods is prohibitive for large molecules and highly excited rotational states. We investigate the ability of artificial neural networks to extend existing datasets of collisional excitation rate coefficients without performing additional scattering calculations. A multilayer perceptron model was trained using molecular quantum numbers, level energies, energy gaps, and kinetic temperature as descriptors, and optimized on logarithmic rate coefficients. The model was tested on diverse collision systems ranging from simple diatomic molecules (CO, SiO) to open-shell species (SO), small asymmetric tops (H$_2$O, SO$_2$), and complex organic molecules (benzonitrile and vinyl cyanide) colliding with H$_2$ molecules or He atoms. Extrapolations were performed toward higher rotational levels, higher temperatures, and combinations of both. For most systems, predictions of rate coefficients involving previously unseen rotational states yielded mean error factors between 1.1 and 1.5, with the dominant rate coefficients generally reproduced more accurately than weaker transitions. Temperature extrapolations proved particularly successful, while combined extrapolations in temperature and rotational levels remained accurate to within a factor of two in most cases. The prediction quality depends on the interplay between dataset size, temperature range, molecular complexity, and the presence of clear collisional propensity rules. Overall, the approach demonstrates that machine learning can provide a practical route to extending collisional datasets for astrophysical applications, especially for large molecules for which additional quantum-scattering calculations are computationally demanding.

### A Parameter-Free Reference Hamiltonian for Tight-Binding Vanadium Oxides
- **arXiv**: [2610.17687](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17687)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17687.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: A Parameter-Free Reference Hamiltonian for Tight-Binding Vanadium Oxides
- **Authors**: Jun-qiang Lu
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, physics.chem-ph, physics.comp-ph
- **TL;DR**: 作者构建了无经验可调参数的钒氧化物紧束缚模型：参考哈密顿量由中性原子叠加、完整PBE交换关联势与冻结芯正交化导出，电荷响应用PBE线性响应的三个屏蔽因子加未屏蔽库仑。该模型重现VO2的PAW能量差（M1相对金红石+2.6 meV/atom，压缩/膨胀下+6.2/-5.2 meV/atom）并拟合大部分PBE响应，同时揭示标准DFTB2的两项近似误差达电子伏级且相互抵消。

Density-functional tight binding has yet to yield a transferable description of vanadium oxides; for the standard second-order construction and the tested cells, we demonstrate that two of its approximations deviate by electron volts and offset each other in the band-index centroid separation between the V 3d and O 2p bands: the neutral two-centre reference omits electron-volt environmental terms, whereas the Mulliken charge model assigns the compact V 3d shell less than half of the self-consistent potential shift observed in PBE. Instead, we build a tight-binding model where the reference Hamiltonian derives from superposed neutral atoms, the full PBE exchange-correlation potential and frozen-core orthogonality, without adjustable terms, and where the orbital-resolved charge response employs three screening factors obtained from PBE linear response and an unscreened Coulomb range. Relative to the primary PAW reference, the plane-wave Harris-Foulkes energy differences of VO2 are matched by the reference energy at fixed geometry and the stated numerical settings, with signed deviations of +2.6 meV/atom for M1 relative to rutile (an energy difference of 92 meV/atom) and +6.2 and -5.2 meV/atom under 3% volume compression and expansion; the charge response reproduces 14 of 22 fitted and 5 of 8 held-out matched PBE responses within one tolerance, and 28 of the 30 within twice the tolerance, and in rutile the fitted first-radial V 3d shift is -4.39 eV, 0.47 eV more negative than PBE's -3.92 eV, whereas the tested DFTB2 charge model gives -1.53 eV. The convexity of the spinless model at fixed geometry prevents it from spontaneously differentiating symmetry-equivalent sites under the symmetries respected by its implemented operators and sampling, so within this model such differentiation in the Magnéli phases must arise from electron-lattice coupling or an explicit spin or correlation term.

### Engineering Ferroelectric Phases in BaTiO$_3$ Films via Buffer-Layer Induced Misfit Strain Reversal
- **arXiv**: [2610.16766](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16766)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16766.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Engineering Ferroelectric Phases in BaTiO$_3$ Films via Buffer-Layer Induced Misfit Strain Reversal
- **Authors**: A. S. Mikheykin, N. V. Lyanguzov, A. S. Konstantinov, V. B. Shirokov, E. D. Linnik, T. Bondar, A. G. Razumnaya
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 在MgO上生长BaTiO₃薄膜时，热膨胀失配主导导致有效压应力；插入50 nm LSMO导电缓冲层可将应变反转为拉应力，从而得到非常规相序（顺电→正交aa相→单斜r相），并产生内建电场与自极化。

Controlling ferroelectric phase symmetry through strain engineering is crucial for tailoring polarization topology and functional properties in oxide-based devices. Here, we show that a thin conductive buffer layer can override substrate-induced strain in BaTiO$_3$ (BTO) films on MgO.
