Although the lattice parameter of MgO(001) would be expected to induce tensile misfit strain, in 500~nm BaTiO$_3$ films the thermal-expansion mismatch dominates, resulting in effective compressive stress upon cooling. Inserting a 50-nm La$_{0.67}$Sr$_{0.33}$MnO$_3$ (LSMO) buffer layer reverses the effective strain state from compressive to tensile. Using temperature-dependent polarized Raman spectroscopy, X-ray diffraction, and piezoresponse force microscopy, we reveal an unconventional phase sequence upon cooling: paraelectric $\rightarrow$ orthorhombic ($\textit{aa}$-phase, $\sim$407 K) $\rightarrow$ monoclinic ($\textit{r}$-phase, $\sim$300-345 K), characterized in-plane and tilted polarization components. The LSMO layer not only serves as a bottom electrode but also modifies the strain state, while interfacial charge redistribution induces a built-in electric field, evidenced by self-polarization and asymmetric PFM hysteresis. These findings highlight the critical role of conductive buffer layers in controlling ferroelectric symmetry and enabling oxide heterostructures with tailored polarization textures for low-power spintronics, tunable microwave devices, and neuromorphic computing.

### WannierSTM: Efficient superconducting scanning-probe spectroscopy from maximally localized Wannier functions
- **arXiv**: [2610.17943](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17943)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17943.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: WannierSTM: Efficient superconducting scanning-probe spectroscopy from maximally localized Wannier functions
- **Authors**: Oscar Moreno Segura, Dorye L. Esteras, Andrei Shumilin, Gonzalo Rivero-Carracedo, Riku Tuovinen, José J. Baldoví
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, physics.chem-ph, physics.comp-ph, quant-ph
- **TL;DR**: 本文提出一个基于Python的程序，将基于最大局域化Wannier函数的第一性原理电子结构与非平衡格林函数方法结合，实现高效的扫描隧道显微镜（STM）模拟；通过Nambu旋量形式纳入超导配对势、宽能带极限处理探针与电极耦合，得到电流-电压与微分电导的闭式解析表达式，避免数值能量积分。

We present a Python-based program that combines maximally localized Wannier functions-based first-principles electronic structure with the nonequilibrium Green's function formalism to enable efficient simulations of scanning tunneling microscopy (STM). The workflow builds upon standard density functional theory packages to construct compact transport Hamiltonians that preserve the properties of realistic materials. Superconductivity is incorporated via an effective pairing potential within the Nambu-spinor formalism, while coupling to the STM tip and substrate electrodes is treated using the wide-band limit approximation. Within this formulation, transport quantities such as current-voltage characteristics and differential conductance are evaluated in a closed analytical form. This avoids explicit numerical energy integrations and enables rapid spectroscopic calculations over broad parameter spaces. The framework provides a unified and computationally efficient approach for modeling superconducting hybrid nanostructures with spin-orbit coupling, magnetic textures, and spin polarization. Representative applications in spin chains, two-dimensional and molecular magnets on a superconductor demonstrate its robust capabilities for both simple model systems and realistic ab initio molecular structures relevant to modern STM experiments.

### Molecular Precursor Chemistry Shapes Early-Stage Reaction Pathways in Nitrogen-Doped Carbon Nanotube Synthesis
- **arXiv**: [2610.16831](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16831)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16831.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Molecular Precursor Chemistry Shapes Early-Stage Reaction Pathways in Nitrogen-Doped Carbon Nanotube Synthesis
- **Authors**: Narayan N. Som, Lukasz Nowicki, Tomasz Gizewski, Karolina Z. Milowska, Anna Wroblewska, Caghan Tuncer, Maciej Trzaskowski, Olga Iwasinska-Kowalska, Tomasz Plocinski, Mariusz Zdrojek, Krzysztof K. Koziol, Malgorzata Jakubowska, Agnieszka Lekawa-Raus
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 本文证明碳纳米管结构演化可追溯到前驱体分解与催化剂形成的最初纳秒阶段：前驱体化学（尤其氮杂环种类）通过改变Fe-碳、Fe-SiO2相互作用与早期催化剂演化影响CNT结构，吡啶与吡嗪表现不同分解路径。

The structural characteristics of carbon nanotubes are usually discussed in terms of processes occurring during nanotube nucleation and subsequent growth from an already formed catalyst nanoparticle. Here, we show that their structural trajectory can begin much earlier, within the first nanoseconds of precursor decomposition and catalyst formation. We combine experiment and reactive molecular dynamics to compare nitrogen-free CNT synthesis with two closely related aromatic nitrogen precursors, pyridine and pyrazine, containing one and two nitrogen atoms per molecule, respectively. Experimentally, both nitrogen-containing precursors promote bamboo-like CNT formation and produce substantially more structurally disordered CNTs than the nitrogen-free reference. Notably, nitrogen incorporation does not scale simply with the amount of nitrogen supplied, demonstrating a strong contribution from precursor chemistry. Reactive molecular dynamics reveals that nitrogen-containing precursors significantly modify local Fe-carbon and Fe-SiO2 interactions during catalyst evolution, while pyridine and pyrazine exhibit distinct decomposition pathways, with pyrazine showing significantly greater decomposition within the simulated timescale. These results demonstrate that precursor chemistry influences the local chemical environment and early catalyst evolution, highlighting its potential role in the initial stages of CNT growth.

### Mutual information across the commensurate--incommensurate crossover and near the Kosterlitz--Thouless point of the spin-$\tfrac12$ $J_1$--$J_2$ Heisenberg chain
- **arXiv**: [2610.16915](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16915)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16915.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Mutual information across the commensurate--incommensurate crossover and near the Kosterlitz--Thouless point of the spin-$\tfrac12$ $J_1$--$J_2$ Heisenberg chain
- **Authors**: Wuttichai Pankeaw, Pruet Kalasuwan
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, quant-ph
- **TL;DR**: 研究自旋1/2 J1-J2海森堡链中量子互信息相对常规关联函数的额外信息（主要来自块互信息），用DMRG和SU(2)通道分解分析二聚化相与无能隙相。

What quantum mutual information adds to conventional correlation functions is often unclear. We study the spin-1/2 $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg chain, using DMRG on infinite chains and periodic rings and an SU(2) decomposition of the mutual information into correlation channels. For singlet ground states the two-site mutual information is an exact function of the spin correlator, so any additional content must come from blocks. In the dimerized phase, blocks resolve bond-energy correlations in the SU(2)-scalar channel without an independent asymptotic length: singlet and triplet correlation lengths agree to about 1%. The scalar channel dominates block mutual information at large distances in the commensurate regime up to $J_2/J_1=0.40$, but falls to a few percent beyond the disorder point, which coincides numerically with the Majumdar-Ghosh point $J_2/J_1=1/2$; the structure-factor peak splits only at the Lifshitz point $J_2/J_1=0.520622(2)$. On the gapless side we compare block mutual information on rings extrapolated to infinite size with the two-interval prediction of SU(2)$_1$ conformal field theory across the Kosterlitz-Thouless point $J_2/J_1=0.241167$. There, for block lengths $l\le6$, the correction decays as $l^{-p}$ with $p=1.96$-$2.07$, and any logarithmic term is at least 60 times smaller than in the Heisenberg chain. Expanding in the marginal coupling (from level spectroscopy) the first-order coefficient is consistent with $l^{-2}$ scaling and the second-order coefficient is positive for $x\le1/4$, suggesting that the mutual information relative to the field-theory value becomes stationary at the KT point for large blocks. The scalar-channel share approaches 1/4 at small cross ratio $x$, as expected from SO(4) symmetry; under two stated assumptions its first-order response to the coupling is $(3/8)\ln x-5/8$ at small $x$, matched by the $l=4$ data within 1-3% at $x\le1/9$.

### Ensuring Complete Consistency between Hops and Wavefunction Propagation in Fewest-Switches Surface Hopping
- **arXiv**: [2610.18743](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18743)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18743.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Ensuring Complete Consistency between Hops and Wavefunction Propagation in Fewest-Switches Surface Hopping
- **Authors**: Jonathan R. Mannouch
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph
- **TL;DR**: 该文提出一种"完全一致"的跃迁概率公式及态追踪算法，解决FSSH在平凡交叉处精度差、需极小时间步的问题，实现大时间步下仍高精度。

Fewest-switches surface hopping (FSSH) is a popular and versatile approach for performing dynamical simulations beyond the Born-Oppenheimer approximation, in which nonadiabatic effects are incorporated through stochastic hops of the active potential energy surface. However, the FSSH algorithm does not ensure that hops are completely consistent with the underlying electronic wavefunction dynamics. This can lead to significant errors as well as requiring small propagation time steps, which can significantly compromise the computational efficiency of the method. These problems are known to be particularly severe at so-called trivial crossings, for which the nonadiabatic coupling is entirely localized at the crossing point. Here, we solve the trivial-crossing problem by developing a fully-consistent hopping-probability formula, which generates attempted hops that match the time-evolution of the wavefunction across any size time step by construction. A state-tracking algorithm is also utilized to ensure that attempted hops are always accepted at trivial crossings. Through application to systems that contain extremely challenging trivial crossings, we demonstrate that this hopping scheme is capable of obtaining highly accurate results, even when large propagation time steps are used.

### Colossal Spin--Lattice--Charge Coupling in Noncollinear Ferrimagnetic NbMnX$_2$ ($X$ = S, Se, Te) Monolayers
- **arXiv**: [2610.17849](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17849)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17849.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Colossal Spin--Lattice--Charge Coupling in Noncollinear Ferrimagnetic NbMnX$_2$ ($X$ = S, Se, Te) Monolayers
- **Authors**: Peng Wang, Nanshu Liu, Hongkuan Yuan, Xue Jiang, Jijun Zhao
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, physics.comp-ph
- **TL;DR**: 本文通过第一性原理、对称性分析、原子自旋模拟与输运建模，预言NbMnX2(X=S,Se,Te)单层为稳定的非共线亚铁磁体，居里温度可达313 K，并展现磁弹耦合（模量变化达36%）、近室温磁热效应及高达10000%的各向异性磁电阻，构成二维多功能平台。

Noncollinear ferrimagnets combine a net magnetic moment with internal spin degrees of freedom, creating opportunities to couple magnetic, mechanical, thermal, and electronic responses. Using first-principles calculations, symmetry analysis, atomistic spin simulations, and transport modeling, we identify stable NbMnX$_2$ ($X$ = S, Se, Te) monolayers with noncollinear ferrimagnetic ground states and Curie temperatures up to 313 K. Competing exchange, Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interactions, and magnetic anisotropy produce field-responsive canted states that couple strongly to the lattice and charge carriers. Magnetization rotation changes the elastic modulus of NbMnTe$_2$ by up to 36\%, while lattice entropy substantially enhances the near-room-temperature magnetocaloric response of NbMnS$_2$ and NbMnSe$_2$. In NbMnSe$_2$, rotation restores a mirror-protected crossing, continuously closes an approximately 0.13 eV gap, and produces a conductivity-based anisotropic magnetoresistance ratio up to 10,000\% at 100 K. The combined results establish noncollinear NbMnX$_2$ monolayers as a multifunctional platform for two-dimensional spintronics, nanomechanics, and thermal control.

### Trust-Limited Machine-Learning-Accelerated Nudged Elastic Band Calculations for Small-Polaron Hopping in Rutile TiO2
- **arXiv**: [2610.16850](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16850)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16850.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Trust-Limited Machine-Learning-Accelerated Nudged Elastic Band Calculations for Small-Polaron Hopping in Rutile TiO2
- **Authors**: Ashish Kumar Singh, Irina V. Lebedeva
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 提出一种高斯过程辅助的NEB工作流，结合DFT+U与SIESTA，稳健计算金红石TiO2中小极化子跳跃势垒，并通过独立DFT全带验证获得高可重复的势垒结果。

Nudged elastic band (NEB) calculations of small-polaron hopping are demanding because nuclear motion is coupled to a self-consistent localized electronic state. Near the charge-transfer region, slow self-consistent-field convergence, localization switching, and competing electronic solutions complicate surrogate-assisted optimization. We develop a Gaussian-process-assisted NEB workflow for this regime and demonstrate electron small-polaron hopping in rutile TiO2 using spin-polarized density functional theory with a Hubbard U correction (DFT+U ) with SIESTA. A joint energy-force Gaussian process (GP) with a Matérn covariance model advances the elastic band, while active-atom root-mean-square and maximum-atom displacement limits constrain surrogate motion relative to evaluated configurations. Reaching these limits triggers first-principles sampling without requiring full surrogate convergence. Three independently DFT-verified 96-atom [001] calculations using 11, 13, and 15 images and different surrogate settings and GP parameterizations yield barriers of 0.1645, 0.1637, and 0.1640 eV, respectively, spanning 0.793 meV. In the 15-image calculation, repeated central-image sampling activates a separately calibrated local GP during surrogate optimization. Final full-band DFT verification gives a maximum projected NEB force of 0.0346 eV/Å. A 324-atom calculation yields a lower barrier of 0.1302 eV, indicating supercell sensitivity and demonstrating feasibility at larger GP observation dimension. The workflow combines controlled surrogate extrapolation, targeted charge-transfer-region interpolation, and independent DFT verification to obtain polaron migration pathways, emphasizing reproducible barriers and full-band force convergence.

### Zeeman spin quantum geometry and intrinsic gyrotropic spin current
- **arXiv**: [2610.18224](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18224)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18224.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Zeeman spin quantum geometry and intrinsic gyrotropic spin current
- **Authors**: Srijan Chatterjee, Tarun Kanti Ghosh
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 本文揭示了一种仅由振荡磁场驱动、源于Zeeman自旋量子几何的内禀回旋自旋流(IGSC)，并用密度矩阵方法推导其表达式，同时预言可与Zeeman Berry曲率驱动的电荷流区分并用于自旋流探测。

We uncover an intrinsic gyrotropic spin current (IGSC) driven solely by an oscillating magnetic field, arising from the spin version of Zeeman quantum geometry. Using the density matrix formalism, we derive the IGSC in the low-frequency clean limit, in terms of the Zeeman spin quantum metric and the Zeeman spin Berry curvature. The effect is studied in a minimal Rashba-Dresselhaus model that also supports intrinsic gyrotropic charge currents from the Zeeman Berry curvature. The spin and charge currents exhibit a $π/2$ phase shift and are found to be perpendicular to each other for certain parameter ranges. This not only enables spin current detection but also opens pure spin current channels for energy-efficient spintronics. We further observe that these currents switch directions between longitudinal and transverse relative to the applied field, depending on the relative strength of Rashba and Dresselhaus interactions.

### Capturing Coordination Core Topology in Metal--Organic Frameworks Using Persistent Mayer Homology
- **arXiv**: [2610.17056](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17056)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17056.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Capturing Coordination Core Topology in Metal--Organic Frameworks Using Persistent Mayer Homology
- **Authors**: Caleb Simiyu Khaemba, Chun-Long Chen, Guo-Wei Wei
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 论文提出持久Mayer同调(PMH),用于定量刻画MOF和POM中金属配位核心的局部几何差异,并将其与元素分辨持久同调(ERPH)结合,以提升对氮氧相关吸附与自扩散性质的预测。

Local coordination cores are critically important in metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) and polyoxometalates (POMs), as they govern the fundamental chemical and structural properties of these materials. Existing methods, including element-resolved persistent homology (ERPH), have limited ability to translate local coordination cores into quantitative, scalable, and interpretable information. Here, we introduce persistent Mayer homology (PMH) to enable the detection of the geometric variability invisible by classical topology. By focusing on coordination cores formed by periodic metal-centered heavy-atom, carbon, and donor channels, PMH distinguishes different arrangements of the local coordination environment, linker framework, and potential coordinating atoms. We examine explainable PMH of orders $N=3$ and $N=5$ and separately combine each with ERPH, which characterizes the multiscale organization of chemically defined element groups across the extended framework, to predict eight nitrogen- and oxygen-related adsorption and self-diffusion properties. The resulting coordination-core PMH representations consistently improve predictive performance over ERPH alone, with the third-order Mayer representation providing the strongest overall performance. These results demonstrate that the inner coordination configurations captured by PMH contain essential structural information that is not fully represented by conventional element-resolved topological descriptors. This finding provides a quantitative interpretable topological framework for understanding local coordination environments and may facilitate the rational design of MOFs and POM-based materials.

### Emergent low-energy thermodynamics and universal $B/T$ scaling in $α$-RuCl$_3$
- **arXiv**: [2610.16961](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16961)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.16961.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Emergent low-energy thermodynamics and universal $B/T$ scaling in $α$-RuCl$_3$
- **Authors**: A. Khansili, B. J. Ramshaw, A. Bangura, H. -Y. Kee, R. D. McDonald, F. Assaad, A. Rydh, A. Shekhter, K. A. Modic
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: α-RuCl₃ 高场比热测量显示低能热力学呈普适的 B/T 标度行为，暗示仅用微扰 Kitaev 极限（Majorana 费米子+通量激发）描述非 c 轴磁场下的低能物理可能并不恰当。

Quantum spin liquids are exotic states of matter in which strongly interacting spins remain disordered down to zero temperature and host fractionalized excitations. This has motivated an extensive search for their realization in materials. Among candidate systems, $α$-RuCl$_3$ has drawn particular attention as a platform for the quantum spin liquid proposed by Kitaev, in which the low-energy excitations in applied magnetic fields are interpreted as itinerant Majorana fermions and local flux excitations. However, conflicting experimental signatures have cast doubt on whether a Kitaev quantum spin liquid-like state is really the right starting point to interpret the low-energy states in $α$-RuCl$_3$, motivating a closer look at the nature of these excitations. Here we report an extensive high field study of specific heat up to 35 T on $α$-RuCl$_3$. We find that the low-energy thermodynamics exhibit universal $B/T$ scaling with a single effective energy scale proportional to the applied field as long as the magnetic field is not close to the $c$-axis. This behavior persists continuously from the antiferromagnetic phase boundary to the highest fields, suggesting that the perturbative Kitaev limit, with its Majorana and flux excitations, may not be an appropriate starting point for describing the low-energy thermodynamics of $α$-RuCl$_3$ for fields away from the $c$-axis.

### Embossing of Tapered, Isometric Micro-Interdigitated Flow Fields for Enhanced Desalination by Faradaic Deionization
- **arXiv**: [2610.18791](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18791)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18791.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Embossing of Tapered, Isometric Micro-Interdigitated Flow Fields for Enhanced Desalination by Faradaic Deionization
- **Authors**: Md Habibur Rahman, Sadia Afrin, Kyle C. Smith
- **Categories**: physics.chem-ph, physics.flu-dyn
- **TL;DR**: 提出一种压印-压延一体化方法，在普鲁士蓝类似物（PBA）多孔电极上构筑嵌入式微叉指流场（eμ-IDFF），实现非减材、可卷对卷的电极图案化，显著提升电化学脱盐性能。

Engineered transport architectures can improve electrolyte distribution and active-material utilization in porous electrodes, yet their implementation remains limited by fabrication methods that compromise precision, throughput, active-material preservation, or scalability. Here, an integrated embossing-calendering method is introduced to pattern Prussian blue analog (PBA) porous electrodes with embedded, micro-interdigitated flow fields (e$μ$-IDFFs) for electrochemical desalination. This non-subtractive, roll-to-roll compatible method forms channels by controlling plastic deformation while simultaneously calendering the electrode, preserving active material and reducing patterning time by 100-fold relative to mechanical milling. Indentation experiments and a closed-form model further show that embossing requires 15-fold less force than stamping, while electrode microstructures show local densification beneath embossed channels. PBA electrodes are embossed with novel e$μ$-IDFFs that use continuous width-tapering with isometric flow paths that promote uniform electrolyte distribution, increase rate capability, and reduce polarization. Compared with e$μ$-IDFFs patterned using less scalable means and non-isometric flow paths, these e$μ$-IDFFs desalinate seawater-salinity feeds near to potable levels with 1.5-fold of its thermodynamic energy efficiency and water recovery. Brackish desalination benefits similarly. Tapered, isometric e$μ$-IDFFs and the embossing method introduced to pattern them are readily translated other electrochemical energy storage and separations systems.

### Local-resolvent formulation and assessment of linear-scaling Kohn-Sham calculations
- **arXiv**: [2610.18137](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18137)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18137.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Local-resolvent formulation and assessment of linear-scaling Kohn-Sham calculations
- **Authors**: Albert Linda
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, physics.comp-ph
- **TL;DR**: 提出局部预解式密度泛函理论（LRes-DFT）：仅用少复能量点上单电子预解式的对角元即可得到密度与能带能量，无需轨道、无需动能泛函；其求和等价于平滑占据函数1/(1+u^M)，化为一种新型分治（DC）方案。

Kohn-Sham density-functional theory is expensive because it needs orbitals that extend over the whole system. For insulators this can be avoided by using only the diagonal of the one-electron resolvent, $g(\mathbf{r},z)=\langle\mathbf{r}|(z-\hat h)^{-1}|\mathbf{r}\rangle$, at a few complex energies $z$. We call the scheme local-resolvent density-functional theory (LRes-DFT). The density and the band energy are linear in $g$, so neither orbitals nor a kinetic-energy functional are needed. The complex energies are $M$ equally spaced points on a circle that encloses the occupied levels, offset by half a spacing so that no point lies on the real axis. Summing the resolvent over them is equivalent to occupying each level with $1/(1+u^M)$, where $u$ is the level's energy measured from the circle's center in units of its radius. This is a smooth step that is near 1 inside the circle and near 0 outside, and it stays between 0 and 1 for every $M$. If the circle moves with the chemical potential, forces at fixed electron number are exact. Gauss-Legendre and Zolotarev rules need fewer points when the gap is small. In three dimensions we use eigenpairs of small windows around each molecule instead of resolvents. This is divide-and-conquer (DC) with a different occupation function, and it is as accurate as DC with Fermi-Dirac occupations. For H$_2$ molecular crystals of up to 1024 electrons it agrees with Kohn-Sham to $2.5\times10^{-7}$ Ha per electron. On one core it is slower than dense diagonalization below about 500 electrons and 3.5 times faster at 1024 electrons. It loses accuracy for disordered or compressed systems and for diffuse basis functions.

### Microscopic Theory of the Acoustic Faraday Effect in Spin-Orbit Coupled Magnets
- **arXiv**: [2610.17010](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17010)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17010.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Microscopic Theory of the Acoustic Faraday Effect in Spin-Orbit Coupled Magnets
- **Authors**: Antoine Matar, Haoting Xu, Hae-Young Kee
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 本文重新审视声学法拉第效应与声子霍尔粘度的关系，指出磁序破坏旋转对称性会产生各向异性声子响应与椭圆偏振本征模，故法拉第旋转不能单独归因于霍尔粘度；并基于J-K-Γ-Γ'模型给出微观自旋-声子耦合框架。

The acoustic Faraday effect probes phonon Hall viscosity through the rotation of transverse acoustic polarization, conventionally interpreted in terms of circular birefringence. We show that magnetic order breaking rotational symmetry generates anisotropic phonon responses and elliptically polarized eigenmodes, so that Faraday rotation cannot be attributed to Hall viscosity alone. To elucidate their microscopic origin and consequences, we derive spin-phonon couplings from a generic $J$-$K$-$Γ$-$Γ'$ model with interlayer coupling, identifying transverse strain couplings for propagation along the $c$-axis arising from the interplay of Kitaev $(K)$ and $Γ$ interactions, along with strain-induced $g$-tensor contributions. Incorporating these couplings into our theory for the Kitaev spin-liquid candidate $α$-RuCl$_3$, we find that symmetry breaking modifies the Faraday response, revealing a Hall viscosity approximately twice the value inferred assuming rotational symmetry. Our results establish a microscopic framework beyond circular birefringence, refining the connection between acoustic Faraday rotation and phonon Hall viscosity in symmetry-broken spin-orbit-coupled magnets.

### Role of dissipation dilution in achieving high mechanical quality factors in van der Waals resonators
- **arXiv**: [2610.18288](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18288)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18288.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Role of dissipation dilution in achieving high mechanical quality factors in van der Waals resonators
- **Authors**: Lukas Schleicher, Leonard Geilen, Alexander Musta, Niklas Walter, Anne Rodriguez, Philipp Bredol, Alexander W. Holleitner, Eva M. Weig
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, quant-ph
- **TL;DR**: 本文在单层MoS₂鼓膜谐振器中实现了高达 372,000±37,000 的品质因子（4 K），比此前任何vdW材料高近一个数量级，并提出圆鼓膜的耗散稀释模型，揭示耗散稀释是决定高Q vdW机械谐振器的核心机制。

Dissipation dilution has driven a revolution in the quality factors ($Q$) of top-down nanomechanical resonators. Yet it has not been leveraged for van der Waals (vdW) materials. Here we report quality factors up to $372{,}000\pm37{,}000$ in monolayer MoS$_2$ drums at 4 K, nearly an order of magnitude beyond the previous best for any vdW material at this temperature, enabled by a clean, polymer-free exfoliation technique. We develop a dissipation-dilution model for circular drums and analyze the contributing intrinsic and extrinsic dissipation mechanisms. The MoS$_2$ monolayer exhibits a dilution factor of order $1,000$, establishing sub-nanometer-thin resonators as ultimate dilution materials. The intrinsic quality factor, however, is limited by surface losses at atomic thickness. Conversely, multilayer drums show a lower dilution factor but a high intrinsic quality factor, on par with established top-down materials of similar dimensions, both resulting from the increased thickness. A two-level-system analysis of the temperature-dependent damping confirms this bulk-versus-surface picture and provides first insight into its microscopic origin. Our results identify dissipation dilution as the governing mechanism for high-$Q$ mechanical vdW resonators. Combined with strategies such as soft clamping or stress engineering, they establish atomically thin resonators as a platform for ultrahigh-$Q$ quantum transduction and sensing.

### Symmetry-constrained coexistence of charge-three and charge-one Weyl points in nonmagnetic crystals
- **arXiv**: [2610.17071](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17071)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17071.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Symmetry-constrained coexistence of charge-three and charge-one Weyl points in nonmagnetic crystals
- **Authors**: Ze-Xin Xue, Ke-Xin Pang, Yun-Yun Bai, Yanfeng Ge, Yan Gao
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该工作系统研究了非磁晶体中三荷与单荷Weyl点共存的八节点构型，通过对称性分类筛选出候选空间群，并预言硼同素异形体TBR-B48与OBD-B48可实现该构型。

High-charge Weyl semimetals (WSMs), particularly those hosting charge-three Weyl point (C-3 WPs), have attracted considerable interest owing to their unconventional topological properties and enhanced boundary phenomena. However, ideal C-3 WSMs remain rare, and the symmetry-constrained coexistence of charge-three and charge-one Weyl point (C-1 WPs) in nonmagnetic crystals remains largely unexplored. Here, we investigate a representative eight-node Weyl configuration, consisting of two C-3 WPs and six C-1 WPs with opposite chirality, corresponding to two identical $\{3,-1,-1,-1\}$ irreducible Weyl molecules. By combining topological charge conservation with space-group symmetry analysis, we systematically classify all 230 space groups and identify 12 and 23 candidate groups without and with spin--orbit coupling (SOC), respectively. First-principles calculations without SOC reveal that the boron allotropes TBR-B$_{48}$ and chiral OBD-B$_{48}$ realize this configuration, hosting hourglass-type and non-hourglass-type C-3 WPs, respectively. Surface-state calculations reveal six Fermi-arc branches at the shared projection of the charge-three pair and three at each individual projection. Reversing the structural chirality of OBD-B$_{48}$ reverses the signs of all Weyl charges, establishing a direct correspondence between crystal and momentum-space chirality. Furthermore, breaking the protecting $C_6$ symmetry in TBR-B$_{48}$ splits each C-3 WP into three C-1 WPs and reconstructs the surface Fermi arcs. Our work establishes symmetry-based guidelines for designing and searching for nonmagnetic WSMs hosting charge-three and charge-one Weyl-point coexistence.

### Berry curvature from plane-wave mixing: A three-band model with emergent symmetries
- **arXiv**: [2610.17209](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17209)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17209.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Berry curvature from plane-wave mixing: A three-band model with emergent symmetries
- **Authors**: Avi Vadali, Tomohiro Soejima
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 提出一个截断到动量线性的三带模型，揭示动量依赖的带投影势如何决定拓扑带的贝里曲率及涌现对称性。

Two-dimensional materials can host topological phases stabilized either by an intrinsic moiré potential or topological crystallization. Crucial to the topology of these phases is the interplay between the (self-consistently generated) potential and the spinor structure, which imbues the band-projected potential with momentum-dependence. Truncating this potential at linear-order in momentum, we obtain a three-band model that captures the low-energy band structure and Berry curvature of such topological bands. We analytically find that the Berry curvature strongly depends on momentum-dependence of the potential. Furthermore, we identify that the model can be tuned to have emergent symmetries, including $U(1)$ and anti-unitary symmetries. Emergent-symmetry breaking perturbations drive the system to regimes hosting topological flat bands, quadratic band touchings, and multiple Dirac cones. We recover many of these features when studying an un-truncated model of a $λ$-jellium spinor inside of a periodic potential. Our results reveal a rich interplay of spinor structure and a potential in determining the topology of the ground state.

### A Transferable Machine-Learned Potential Enabling Large-Scale Copper-Carbon Simulations from Equilibrium to Extreme Conditions
- **arXiv**: [2610.18545](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18545)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18545.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: A Transferable Machine-Learned Potential Enabling Large-Scale Copper-Carbon Simulations from Equilibrium to Extreme Conditions
- **Authors**: Feiyu Zhou, Wei Qiu, Yan Chen, Jiahui Liu, Yilun Liu, Shengying Yue, Penghua Ying
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, physics.app-ph, physics.chem-ph, physics.comp-ph
- **TL;DR**: 该工作开发了NEP-CuC神经演化势函数，统一描述Cu-C体系的金属Cu、多种碳键合、异质Cu-C界面及近距原子作用，并在多场景下验证其可迁移性。

Cu-C materials and interfaces are central to diamond thermal management, metallization, graphene growth and carbon-coating technologies, but atomistic simulations across these applications require a potential that describes metallic Cu, diverse carbon bonding, heterogeneous Cu-C interactions and close atomic encounters. Here we develop NEP-CuC, a transferable neuroevolution potential trained on a dataset of 2,727 uniformly relabelled configurations selected from a heterogeneous Cu-C candidate pool. Disagreement within an ensemble of five independently trained models provides an internal indicator of model sensitivity. A Ziegler-Biersack-Littmark (ZBL) correction describes short-range nuclear repulsion, while a runtime D3(BJ) correction supplements dispersion-sensitive calculations. Tests on source-derived held-out and independent external datasets, together with structural, elastic, defect, interfacial, vibrational and finite-temperature transport benchmarks, establish broad but property-dependent physical fidelity across elemental Cu, diverse carbon environments and heterogeneous Cu-C configurations. Large-scale simulations of amorphous-carbon formation and orientation-dependent shock compression of Cu further test transferability under strongly non-equilibrium conditions. For Cu/diamond interfaces, the same potential describes deposition, annealing and tensile response: preparation history leaves persistent differences in interphase structure, while the mean peak nominal tensile stress at 500 K decreases from $12.35\pm0.71$ to $6.65\pm0.98$ GPa as the incident Cu energy during the initial deposition stage increases from 1 to 50 eV. NEP-CuC provides an atomistic framework for linking processing, interfacial structure and physical response across Cu-C systems, with direct relevance to Cu/diamond thermal-management interfaces, diamond metallization and non-equilibrium Cu-C processing.

### A Topological Spin on Racetrack Memory: Stability and Transport of 360° Domain Walls in Magnetic Nanotubes
- **arXiv**: [2610.18297](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18297)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18297.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: A Topological Spin on Racetrack Memory: Stability and Transport of 360° Domain Walls in Magnetic Nanotubes
- **Authors**: Benjamin Zingsem
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cond-mat.mes-hall, physics.comp-ph
- **TL;DR**: 空心铁磁管中的360°畴壁可作为兼具快速运动、抗扰动和可读出信号的局域载体，用于赛道存储器。

Magnetic racetrack memory requires localized carriers that combine rapid motion, resistance to perturbations and a detectable signal. Here we show how a 360° domain wall in a hollow ferromagnetic tube combines these properties within a single continuous magnetic shell. Two successive magnetization reversals form a bound object whose surrounding domains point in the same direction. Analytical energetics and micromagnetic simulations establish its finite separation, recovery from tested perturbations and current driven transport without microscopic Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction or a maintaining field. The texture's winding resists unwinding while radial rotation carries an energy cost, providing a specific control over stability. Relative rotation of the constituent spins accounts quantitatively for additional displacement during current onset. The wall supports motion at hundreds of metres per second, with transient velocities approaching one kilometre per second at stronger drive. A prepared seven-bit pattern retains its carriers and local readout contrast after pulsed transport and recovery. Curved-track and differential-current tests identify radial stiffness and drive uniformity as controls for intact motion. These results establish a physical basis for fast, locally distinguishable tubular magnetic bits and identify a continuous switching pathway that can be targeted by tailored excitation.

### Spin-orbit torque effects in all-electrical spin-wave spectroscopy experiments
- **arXiv**: [2610.17095](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17095)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17095.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Spin-orbit torque effects in all-electrical spin-wave spectroscopy experiments
- **Authors**: José Solano Córdova, Jone Mencos, Richard Schlitz, Quentin Rossi, Denise Reustlen, Javed Ali, Sebastian Sailler, Michaela Lammel, Matthieu Bailleul, Daniel Stoeffler, Felix Casanova, Sebastian T. B. Goennenwein, Helena Reichlova
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cond-mat.other
- **TL;DR**: 该工作研究Pt天线在105 nm厚外延YIG薄膜中激发相干自旋波的双重机制——射频Oersted场与自旋轨道矩(SOT)，并指出蚀刻及界面不均匀性会显著干扰原始透射谱，去除后剩余信号可用两种机制共同激发解释。

Electrical excitation and detection of propagating spin waves are key elements of magnonic devices. While conventional antennas consisting of non-magnetic metals such as Au or Cu excite spin waves predominantly through radio-frequency (RF) Oersted fields, antennas comprising Pt can additionally generate spin-orbit torques (SOTs) via the spin Hall effect. Recent theoretical work has proposed RF-SOT as an alternative mechanism for coherent spin-wave excitation, predicting distinct characteristics compared with conventional inductive excitation. Here, we investigate Pt antennas for the excitation of coherent spin waves in 105-nm-thick epitaxial $\text{Y}_3\text{Fe}_5\text{O}_{12}$ (YIG) films through two potential mechanisms: an oscillating Oersted field and an oscillating spin current. The raw experimental transmission spectra are strongly affected by fabrication-induced modifications to the YIG film associated with the etching process together with spatially inhomogeneous interface conditions induced by Pt. After removing these contributions, we find the remaining two wave propagation consistent with a combined excitation from Oersted fields and SOTs.

### Plasmon modes in three-dimensional multifold semimetals. I. Triple-point semimetal, its quadratic cousin, and Hopf semimetal
- **arXiv**: [2610.18629](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18629)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18629.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Plasmon modes in three-dimensional multifold semimetals. I. Triple-point semimetal, its quadratic cousin, and Hopf semimetal
- **Authors**: Ipsita Mandal
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, hep-th
- **TL;DR**: 本文用RPA研究了赝自旋-1三重点半金属的集体电荷激发，对比线性平带模型与二次修正模型，揭示能带结构与量子几何如何共同决定等离激元、屏蔽与Friedel振荡等响应。

We study collective charge excitations in pseudospin-1 triple-point semimetals within the random-phase approximation, considering both the linearised model with a flat-band and its quadratic-corrected counterpart. We analyse their density response, Drude weights, screening, plasmon dispersion and damping, particle-hole continua, and Friedel oscillations from the long-wavelength to the arbitrary-momentum regime. The quadratic correction renders the flat-band dispersive and endows it with a Fermi surface, thereby affecting the overall response. Comparisons with Weyl and Hopf semimetals reveal how bandstructure and quantum geometry jointly govern collective charge dynamics. While the Drude weights are determined by the Fermi surface and band-velocities, interband screening, plasmon dispersion, and Friedel oscillations reflect the quantum geometry of the eigenstates.

### Comprehensive Workflow for Uncertainty-Aware Ionic Liquid Solvent Discovery
- **arXiv**: [2610.17171](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17171)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17171.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Comprehensive Workflow for Uncertainty-Aware Ionic Liquid Solvent Discovery
- **Authors**: Alexander Lobo, Urvi Awasthi, Leonid Zhukov
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cs.LG
- **TL;DR**: 提出一种将数据处理、深度学习建模与物理计算耦合的离子液体性能预测与最优候选筛选工作流，实现可靠的分布外排序，并用于筛选用于烟气CO2捕集的帕累托最优离子液体。

Designing synthesizable materials with targeted properties remains one of the central objectives in materials sciences. Ionic liquids (ILs) are well suited to this goal due to their "tunability", where physical and chemical properties can be precisely customized by modifying their molecular structure, specifically by varying the combination of cations and anions. At the same time, current data-driven property models for ionic liquids often degrade in scarce, noisy, and uneven data regimes, where measurements cover only a small fraction of chemical space and a limited range of thermodynamic conditions. This makes out-of-distribution screening brittle: even when point predictions look plausible, it is difficult to compare candidates consistently, quantify downstream implications, or trust rankings enough to guide molecular discovery. We present a property-prediction and optimal candidate selection workflow that treats data processing, deep learning modeling, and physics-based calculations as a coupled system, linking property prediction with uncertainty estimation and physics-based filtering. By tightening the link between predicted properties and the criteria used for down-selection, the workflow supports reliable out-of-distribution ranking and enables prioritization of previously untested ionic liquid candidates with favorable predicted trade-offs. We demonstrate the application of our workflow by designing novel ionic liquids for CO2 capture from flue gas, predicting their properties and selecting Pareto-optimal candidates based on CO2 solubility, IL viscosity and working capacity.

### Phase-Coherent Spin and Lattice Modulations in a Y-type Hexaferrite
- **arXiv**: [2610.17292](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17292)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17292.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Phase-Coherent Spin and Lattice Modulations in a Y-type Hexaferrite
- **Authors**: Oskar Stepančić, Annika Stellhorn, Emma Campillo, Kun Zhai, Niklas Ekström, A. Corani, D. Alba Venero, R. Cubitt, E. Blackburn, L. Shen
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 在Y型六角铁氧体Ba0.4Sr1.6Mg2Fe12O22的饱和高场态中，首次观测到同一波矢上自旋调制与纵向晶格调制相位锁定共存。

Spin-driven multiferroics owe their electric polarization to lattice distortions that follow the magnetic order, yet in incommensurate magnets symmetry normally forbids such a distortion at the fundamental magnetic wavevector, and the distortions themselves are rarely observed. Here we use half-polarized neutron scattering to show that the Y-type hexaferrite Ba$_{0.4}$Sr$_{1.6}$Mg$_2$Fe$_{12}$O$_{22}$ hosts, in its saturated high-field state, a magnetic modulation and a longitudinal lattice modulation at one and the same wavevector, locked in phase. The signature is a large nuclear-magnetic interference asymmetry, $r$ ~ 0.8, of the same sign at the two conjugate satellites and persisting to 275 K. The zone contrast and the field dependence of the satellite indicate an internal, field-induced longitudinal distortion. The modulated state coexists with the transverse-conical magnetoelectric phase and outlives it, revealing a fundamental-wavevector spin-lattice coupling in a magnetic field regime previously regarded as a simple ferrimagnet.

### Quantum skyrmion molecule: two quantum skyrmions bound by coherent magnon exchange
- **arXiv**: [2610.17299](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17299)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17299.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Quantum skyrmion molecule: two quantum skyrmions bound by coherent magnon exchange
- **Authors**: Ricardo L. Silva
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.other
- **TL;DR**: 在易轴各向异性 J1-J2 三角晶格模型中，利用 ED 与 DMRG 研究量子斯格明子两两相互作用，发现自然尺寸的两斯格明子可自发形成束缚分子并保持各自 7+7 翻转自旋身份，其通过磁振子相干交换耦合，相对螺旋度被锁定，可作为单层自组装的二量子比特元件。

Quantum skyrmions in frustrated spin-$1/2$ magnets are bound states of a few flipped spins in a ferromagnetic background. Using exact diagonalization and density-matrix renormalization group calculations for the $J_1$--$J_2$ triangular-lattice model with easy-axis anisotropy $K$, we ask what happens when two such objects are brought together. The binding energy per flipped spin of a single skyrmion has a minimum at $\Nf^{*}=7$ for $K=0.02$--$0.04$, and skyrmions smaller than $\Nf^{*}$ do not exist as pairs: at any distance they coalesce into a single larger object, and pinning wells cannot prevent it. Two skyrmions of natural size, by contrast, form a bound molecule without pinning or external field, with binding energy $U\simeq-0.007J_1$ at $K=0.03$--$0.04$ and an equilibrium separation of $5.5$ lattice spacings that does not depend on $K$ or on the size of the simulation domain; at $K=0.02$, where the single skyrmion is itself marginally bound, the pair is unbound within our resolution. The two constituents keep their identity ($7+7$ flipped spins) and are coupled by a coherent exchange of magnons, visible as number fluctuations between the two halves of the domain in excess of those of two independent skyrmions and as in-plane spin correlations between the two skyrmion walls that reach $0.16$ across the gap. These correlations follow the vortex pattern of the two textures and lock their relative helicity, the quantum counterpart of the helicity-dependent interaction of classical skyrmions. We discuss the molecule as a self-assembled two-qubit element for skyrmion helicity qubits in a single layer.

### Dark state preparation in a zero-field-split molecular triplet
- **arXiv**: [2610.18661](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18661)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18661.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Dark state preparation in a zero-field-split molecular triplet
- **Authors**: Maria Pfahler, Andrea Donarini
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall, quant-ph
- **TL;DR**: 提出基于原子力显微镜单分子电子自旋共振(ESR-AFM)制备与探测暗态的协议，将原有Rabi振荡技术拓展到三能级V型系统的相干操控。

We propose a protocol to prepare and probe dark states in a single-molecule electron spin resonance based on atomic force microscopy (ESR-AFM), thus extending existing techniques developed to address Rabi oscillations. To this end, we simulate in analogy to atomic physics the coherent manipulation of a three-level system in the V-configuration using a multifrequency magnetic field. More specifically, we consider a neutral molecule with a zero-field-split (ZFS) first excited triplet and analyze within a pump-probe scheme the dynamics of the total triplet population. In the triplet subspace, the dark state is a coherent superposition formed by two ZFS states which does not evolve in time despite a continuous resonant driving towards the third one. We characterize the properties of this state comparing it to its fully incoherent counterpart. Within a density-matrix formalism for open systems, we then compute the expected dark state signatures both for ideal scenarios and for the realistic parameterization of ESR-AFM on pentacene.

### Evidence of an anisotropic Griffiths phase in a two-dimensional van der Waals magnet
- **arXiv**: [2610.17315](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17315)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17315.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Evidence of an anisotropic Griffiths phase in a two-dimensional van der Waals magnet
- **Authors**: Anupam K. Singh, Dhananjay Nair, Holger Meyerheim, Stuart S. P. Parkin, Banabir Pal
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该文报道了范德华磁体Fe5GeTe2中由自插层驱动的高度各向异性Griffiths相，为低维磁性中的无序相研究提供了新平台。

Over the last decade, the emerging field of two-dimensional van der Waals magnetism has focused mainly on long-range-ordered magnetic phases. Disordered magnetic phases, however, remain largely unexplored. Here, we report a highly anisotropic Griffiths phase driven by self-intercalation in the van der Waals magnet Fe5GeTe2. We find evidence of short-range-ordered ferromagnetic clusters embedded in a paramagnetic matrix, which dominate the magnetic response between the Curie temperature and a higher characteristic Griffiths temperature. An anomalous power-law divergence in the magnetic susceptibility, universal Lee Yang scaling, and distinct entropy anomalies provide further evidence for this phase. Strikingly, the non-analytic Griffiths response emerges exclusively under in-plane magnetic fields, whereas out-of-plane fields abruptly alter this behavior. This directional dependence indicates that the local magnetic interactions within nanoscale clusters are closely coupled to the layered lattice geometry. Our findings establish a regime of dimensionally constrained, disorder-driven magnetism and identify self-intercalated van der Waals magnets as a platform for investigating the interplay between magnetic disorder, anisotropy, and reduced dimensionality.

### Conformal soft modes at elastic instabilities
- **arXiv**: [2610.17920](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17920)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17920.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Conformal soft modes at elastic instabilities
- **Authors**: Lars Franke, Nick Sander, Markus Garst
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 临近连续弹性相变的晶体中出现临界涨落，其位移场在线性弹性理论层面满足类似共形Killing方程的条件；同构失稳给出四个临界模式（一个膨胀+三个特殊共形变换），对称破缺失稳则给出连续谱模式，部分情形对应二维Minkowski空间共形变换。

In case a crystal is close to a continuous elastic phase transition, pronounced critical fluctuations emerge. We show that on the level of linear elasticity theory the displacement vector for these critical modes obeys a condition reminiscent of the conformal Killing equation. For an isostructural instability, four critical modes arise corresponding to one dilation and three special conformal transformations. At crystal symmetry-breaking instabilities, the critical fluctuations include a continuum of modes even for crystals with finite size. For certain cases, these comprise the conformal transformations associated with two-dimensional Minkowski space. These results have implications for critical elastic phenomena as well as for extremal mechanical metamaterials.

### Collision Cascade Morphology and Defect Nucleation in Pure Tungsten and an Equimolar Refractory W-Ta-Cr-V Alloy
- **arXiv**: [2610.17361](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17361)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17361.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Collision Cascade Morphology and Defect Nucleation in Pure Tungsten and an Equimolar Refractory W-Ta-Cr-V Alloy
- **Authors**: Mark Fedorov, Francisco Javier Dominguez-Gutierrez, Damian Kalita, Łukasz Kurpaska
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 用机器学习势(tabGAP+ZBL)分子动力学模拟0.1–100 keV初级撞出原子级联，比较纯W与WTaCrV高熵合金的辐照损伤：合金中更频繁的级联分裂（子级联）和轻元素(Cr、V)吸收点缺陷，使位错环与孔洞更少，抗辐照性能更优。

Collision cascades and defect-formation mechanisms in pure W and the WTaCrV refractory high-entropy alloy were investigated using molecular dynamics simulations over primary knock-on atom (PKA) energies of 0.1-100 keV. Machine-learning tabulated Gaussian approximation potentials (tabGAP), complemented by short-range Ziegler-Biersack-Littmark (ZBL) interactions, were employed. Analysis of the heat spike lifetime, affected region, atomic relocations and surviving defects reveals an importance of subcascade formation and presence of light elements in the alloy. Cascade splitting into subcascades contributes to the distribution of the PKA energy over a larger region in the material, nucleating less dislocations and smaller voids - latter being present only in W and absent in WTaCrV. Since cascade splitting is more frequently observed in WTaCrV, the alloy shows better resistance to primary irradiation damage compared to pure W. Apart from cascade splitting, analysis of interstitials shows that Cr and to lesser extent V, being lighter and smaller elements, accommodate the damage in form of point defects, contributing to the mitigation of formation of the dislocation loops.

### Spin textures at the Fermi surface of ferromagnets and associated torque
- **arXiv**: [2610.18800](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18800)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18800.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Spin textures at the Fermi surface of ferromagnets and associated torque
- **Authors**: T. Valet, J. Li, H. Jaffrès, V. Cros, M. Chshiev, R. Raimondi
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 该文理论证明中心对称铁磁金属费米面处自旋轨道相互作用普遍诱导横向自旋织构（Fe/Co/Ni 第一性原理证实），该效应在均匀电流下因对称性不产生自旋轨道矩，但在纳米尺度出现新的电流梯度诱导力矩，其Ni厚度依赖与实验定量吻合。

We establish theoretically that transverse spin textures are generically induced by the spin-orbit interaction at the Fermi surface of centrosymmetric ferromagnetic metals. We confirm this prediction with {\em ab initio} computations for Fe, Co and Ni. By symmetry, this cannot be associated with spin-orbit torques under a uniform current. However, a new {\em current-gradient-induced} torque emerges in nanoscale geometries. For a series of Ni-based bilayers, the computed Ni thickness dependence of the current-gradient-induced torque is in quantitative agreement with measurements previously attributed to orbital torques. We discuss how this opens a new avenue for the electrical control of magnetic orders, possibly also including the Néel vector of metallic altermagnets.

### High-Resolution X-ray Spectro-Microscopy of Au Microcrystallites
- **arXiv**: [2610.17417](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17417)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17417.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: High-Resolution X-ray Spectro-Microscopy of Au Microcrystallites
- **Authors**: Chaitali Sow, Martin Seyrich, Johannes Hagemann, Giridhar U. Kulkarni, Thomas F. Keller, Christian G. Schroer, Andreas Schropp
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 利用X射线光谱叠层成像(X-ray spectro-ptychography)在Au L3边(~12 keV)实现45 nm分辨率的振幅与相位对比XANES成像，对含fcc及非立方晶格的Au微晶进行了多模态化学成像。

Understanding the functionalities of heterogeneous catalysts requires high-resolution nanoscale chemical imaging tools. X-ray spectro-ptychography offers this capability by enabling X-ray absorption near edge structure (XANES) based amplitude and phase contrasts imaging with high spatial resolution. However, such demonstrations at energies above 10 keV, such as the Au L-edges, remain limited due to weak X-ray absorption cross-sections and correspondingly, low signal levels. Here, multimodal spectro-microscopy has been performed on Au microcrystallites that host both conventional face-centered cubic (fcc) and unconventional non-cubic lattices, particularly important for catalysis. Amplitude and phase contrasts across the $\mathrm{L}_3$ edge are resolved on thin Au crystallites (150 nm to 1.71 $μ\mathrm{m}$) with 45 nm spatial resolution. The resulting XANES spectra exhibit excellent agreement with standard transmission and fluorescence contrasts, while providing complementary chemical insights with improved spatial resolution. Quantitative retrieval of anomalous phase variations ~12 keV further demonstrates the suitability of this method for high-resolution chemical imaging across a wide range of functional materials.

### Direct view of exciton and carrier dynamics in twisted bilayer graphene
- **arXiv**: [2610.17984](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17984)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17984.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Direct view of exciton and carrier dynamics in twisted bilayer graphene
- **Authors**: Gianmarco Gatti, Anders S. Mortensen, Zhihao Jiang, Alfred J. H. Jones, Gesa-R. Siemann, Thomas S. Nielsen, Søren Ulstrup, Naina Kushwaha, Bruce Weaver, Yu Zhang, Emma Springate, Charlotte E. Sanders, Vibha Reddy, Ulrich Starke, Philip Hofmann
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.mes-hall, cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 利用位置、时间及角分辨光电子能谱研究H插层SiC上扭转角约9°的双层石墨烯，观察到杂化带隙内的"ghost-Fano"激子特征及层间电子温度差异。

Twisted bilayer graphene (tBLG) on H-intercalated SiC (twist angle of $9 \pm 1^{\circ}$) is studied by position\mbox{-}, time\mbox{-}, and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES). Both graphene layers are hole doped, with a Dirac-point offset of 30~meV. Time-resolved ARPES near the van Hove singularity reveals a spectral feature within the hybridization-induced band gap, together with excited electrons in the conduction band. The in-gap feature exhibits distinct dynamics, and its binding energy and $k$-space location are consistent with the proposed non-hydrogenic ``ghost-Fano'' exciton arising from destructive interference between two nearly degenerate excitonic channels. Near the Dirac points, the twist separates the largely layer-polarized Dirac cones in momentum space, providing direct layer selectivity. We find markedly different peak electronic temperatures in the two graphene layers, even though their subsequent cooling dynamics are very similar. We attribute this to an ultrafast interlayer redistribution of electronic energy, driven by an interlayer electric field that arises from the different doping in the layers.

### Crystal growth, magnetic, and transport properties of the candidate altermagnet Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_{2}$
- **arXiv**: [2610.17452](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17452)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17452.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Crystal growth, magnetic, and transport properties of the candidate altermagnet Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_{2}$
- **Authors**: Nashra Pistawala, Martin R. Lees, Geetha Balakrishnan, Daniel A. Mayoh
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 首次成功生长高质量Cr1/4NbS2单晶，确认其六方对称性与约141 K的反铁磁奈尔温度，为交错磁体研究提供实验平台。

Altermagnetism is a rapidly emerging paradigm in condensed matter magnetism, yet the number of experimentally confirmed altermagnetic candidates remains remarkably limited. Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$ has long been theoretically proposed as a promising altermagnet, but there are no reports on experimental investigation of single crystals. Here, we report the successful growth of high-quality single crystals of Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$ and present its structural, thermal, magnetic, and transport properties. Single-crystal X-ray diffraction provides detailed crystallographic information and confirms the hexagonal crystal symmetry ($P6_3/mmc$), one of the criteria for altermagnetic behavior. Resistivity and heat capacity measurements unambiguously identify an antiferromagnetic Néel temperature of $T_N \sim 141$ K. Magnetotransport measurements reveal non-trivial features in the magnetoresistance and Hall response, indicative of quantum diffusive transport phenomena. Cr$_{1/4}$NbS$_2$, synthesized here for the first time in single-crystalline form, therefore serves as an experimental platform for probing the rich spectrum of phenomena predicted for altermagnetic materials.

### Single anyons and anyon binding in a quantum Hall dot
- **arXiv**: [2610.18817](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18817)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18817.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Single anyons and anyon binding in a quantum Hall dot
- **Authors**: Marco Valentini, Will Wang, Amir Shapour Mohammadi, Noah Samuelson, Chengxuan Li, Liam Cohen, Mark Zakharyan, Jiechao Feng, Taige Wang, Takashi Taniguchi, Kenji Watanabe, Michael P. Zaletel, Andrea F. Young
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 该工作在石墨烯量子点接触器件中实现了单个任意子的捕获与调控，观测到e/3分数电荷共振输运、单任意子量子点及任意子束缚能。

Anyons are particles with quantized fractional statistics. In theory, a single anyon can be stabilized as a localized fractional charge within the bulk of a topologically ordered two-dimensional insulator. However, experiments to date have focused on probing anyons in structures in which many anyonic excitations coexist and interact. Here, we demonstrate the trapping of a single anyon in a device consisting of a graphene-based quantum point contact with an embedded tunable quantum Hall dot. Benchmarking our device at v = 1, we demonstrate backscattering between Fermi liquid edge states mediated by the whole-electron Coulomb blockade of the dot. The cleanliness and control available in our device allow depletion of the dot to the last quasihole, and we observe spin blockade for transitions to the opposite-spin Landau level. At v = 1/3 and 2/3, we observe backscattering resonances with three times the frequency, consistent with resonant transmission of e/3 charges via a single anyon transistor. We demonstrate controlled depletion to the last quasiparticle to form a single anyon quantum dot. Tracing the charge addition spectrum for quasiparticle number Nqp = 1-5, we observe highly irregular addition energies, consistent with DMRG modeling showing a complex orbital shell-filling behavior. At higher Nqp = 10-20, the addition energies show a striking pattern: quasiholes at 1/3 (and quasiparticles at 2/3) show a regular addition spectrum while quasiparticles at 1/3 (and quasiholes at 2/3) do not. We trace the origin of this irregularity to an attractive component of the inter-anyon interaction: by experimentally tuning the confining energy, we observe complete suppression of alternating charge sectors, after which the dot charges in increments of twice the fundamental charge. This observation indicates the emergence of an anyon binding energy as large as the charging energy.

### Electric Edelstein Effect in Two Dimensions
- **arXiv**: [2610.17535](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17535)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17535.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Electric Edelstein Effect in Two Dimensions
- **Authors**: Xiaoran Lv, Tianqi Zhang, Chaoxi Cui, Yaling Li, Yi Shen, Weikang Wu, Ziming Zhu, Yugui Yao, Zhi-Ming Yu
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 本文提出"电场Edelstein效应"(EEE)：在二维体系中，交叉的面外与面内电场可共同产生自旋极化，且通过栅压调节自旋方向，为二维自旋电子学提供新的门控手段。

We propose the electric Edelstein effect (EEE) in two-dimensional (2D) systems, where crossed out-of-plane and in-plane electric fields generate a spin polarization. The gate field converts the Edelstein response from a material-fixed spin polarization into a gate-tunable one. A symmetry analysis over all magnetic layer point groups shows that the EEE is allowed in a wide range of magnetic and nonmagnetic systems, including ferromagnets, antiferromagnets, and altermagnets. The EEE plays two distinct roles: it generates spin polarization where the linear Edelstein effect (EE) is symmetry-forbidden, as in centrosymmetric materials; and it adds a new gate-tunable component, generally non-collinear with the conventional one, where the EE is already present. The two fields then act as independent knobs and raise the control dimension of the spin polarization from one to two. Our microscopic theory traces the EEE to band-geometric quantities, which are strongly enhanced near small band gaps. First-principles calculations predict pronounced effects in the centrosymmetric ferromagnet MnSe2, where the linear EE is symmetry-forbidden. In the nonmagnet SnSe where the EE and EEE coexist, the two knobs cover an extended region of the spin sphere. Our findings establish the EEE as a symmetry-guided, gate-tunable route to electrical spin manipulation and open avenues for multistate spintronics in 2D quantum materials.

### Non-Hermitian Statistical Geometry
- **arXiv**: [2610.18175](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18175)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18175.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Non-Hermitian Statistical Geometry
- **Authors**: Xi Hu, Yan-Qiong Ma, Su-Peng Kou
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 本文将二维任意子交换统计推广到非厄米框架，提出"非厄米统计几何"理论：放松厄米性会把僵硬的编织/融合数据软化为复空间中的谱与相对几何自由度，并给出编织一致性锁定、融合缝合与几何曲率判据。

Particle exchange in two dimensions gives rise to quantum statistics beyond the boson-fermion dichotomy, with central roles in fractional quantum Hall physics, topological quantum computation, and strongly correlated quantum matter. In conventional topological phases, microscopic exchange geometry is compressed into rigid braid and fusion data. We develop a theory of non-Hermitian statistical geometry in which relaxing Hermiticity softens these exchange data into a larger complex space of spectral and relative-geometric degrees of freedom. For two adjacent braid generators, a single projective exchange spectrum is insufficient: their relative eigenline geometry supplies an additional gauge-invariant modulus. On the generic irreducible two-channel branch, braid consistency locks these coordinates through an exact spectrum-geometry relation. Using Riesz projectors and Kato transport, we realize this geometry in a five-arm Kitaev-Majorana network and test spectral and relative-geometric responses in an independent two-dimensional four-vortex Kitaev model. Finite-time dynamics tests the exchange operation, microscopic fusion sewing tests its composition across fusion descriptions, and matched-spectrum controls isolate the role of relative geometry. Nonzero projective Kato curvature shows that braid-consistent, fusion-coherent exchange can depend on the control path. Non-Hermiticity thus exposes exchange geometry, braid consistency re-locks it, fusion coherence further constrains it, and projective flatness determines when geometric exchange becomes intrinsically topological.

### Hydrogen-Bond Density Drives the Order-Disorder Phase Transition from Orientational to Vibrational in Polyalcohol Plastic Crystals
- **arXiv**: [2610.17774](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17774)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17774.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Hydrogen-Bond Density Drives the Order-Disorder Phase Transition from Orientational to Vibrational in Polyalcohol Plastic Crystals
- **Authors**: Ares Sanuy, Carlos Escorihuela-Sayalero, Pol Lloveras, María del Barrio, Hassan Allouchi, Josep-Lluís Tamarit, Luis Carlos Pardo, Claudio Cazorla
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 通过NPG、PG、PE的分子动力学模拟，发现随氢键连通性增强，相变熵的来源从取向无序主导完全反转为振动主导，氢键密度可作为调控热响应及其压力稳健性的设计抓手。

Neopentane-derived plastic crystals undergo order-disorder phase transitions accompanied by exceptionally large entropy changes, making them attractive solid-state materials for thermal energy storage and barocaloric cooling. Yet it remains unclear whether the microscopic nature of this entropy change is an intrinsic property of each compound or can be understood generally and tuned systematically through molecular design. Here, we use classical molecular dynamics simulations of neopentyl glycol (NPG), pentaglycerine (PG), and pentaerythritol (PE), validated against experimental transition temperatures, enthalpy changes, and phase diagrams, to show that the character of the phase-transition entropy undergoes a complete inversion as the hydrogen-bond connectivity of the molecule increases: orientational disordering dominates in NPG, whereas lattice vibrations dominate in PE and become remarkably robust against pressure. PG occupies a balanced intermediate regime in which the orientational, conformational, and vibrational channels contribute comparably. This inversion reflects two coupled microscopic mechanisms: a growing low-frequency gap between the phonon densities of states of the ordered and disordered phases as the hydrogen-bond network densifies, and an increase in molecular symmetry that progressively suppresses orientational disordering. Because the vibrational channel is substantially less sensitive to pressure than the orientational one, hydrogen-bond density emerges as a practical, chemically accessible design handle for tailoring not only the magnitude but also the pressure robustness of the caloric response in hydrogen-bonded plastic crystals, with direct implications for the search for new barocaloric and thermal-energy-storage materials.

### Altermagnetic Ground States on the Ammann--Beenker Tiling
- **arXiv**: [2610.18441](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18441)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18441.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Altermagnetic Ground States on the Ammann--Beenker Tiling
- **Authors**: SeungBeom Hong, YunHo Kim, Moon Jip Park, GiBaik Sim
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.dis-nn, cond-mat.mes-hall
- **TL;DR**: 本文在Ammann--Beenker准晶上的经典海森堡模型中给出精确的交变磁(alternagnetic)基态，并通过自旋分辨输运证实其g波自旋劈裂。

We identify exact altermagnetic ground states of a classical Heisenberg model on a quasicrystal. Considering the Ammann--Beenker tiling as an example, we determine the ground states by exploiting the parity structure of its parent four-dimensional hypercubic lattice. The ground state hosts coplanar altermagnetic order characterized by the preserved spin-group symmetry $[C_{2x}\parallel C_{8z}]$. Without translational symmetry, the momentum-space spin splitting that usually identifies altermagnets is not directly accessible. We instead couple the spins to itinerant electrons and show spin-resolved transport with $g$-wave symmetry, confirming the altermagnetic nature in a nonperiodic system. Our results establish a geometric route to altermagnetic order in quasicrystals, connecting exact magnetic ground states to their spin-resolved transport signatures.

### Learning from fracture: Teaching language models to design through physical experience
- **arXiv**: [2610.17804](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17804)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17804.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Learning from fracture: Teaching language models to design through physical experience
- **Authors**: Bo Ni
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cs.AI, cs.LG
- **TL;DR**: FracLearn 是一个"经验—抽象—泛化"框架，让语言模型在类俄罗斯方块断裂游戏中通过模拟实验积累经验、抽象为原理，并迁移到新架构上超越先前纪录。

How can language models learn to optimize physical designs through experience? We introduce FracLearn, an experience-abstraction-generalization framework evaluated through a deliberately unfamiliar, Tetris-inspired fracture game. The model first proposes architectures and examines simulated fracture responses to accumulate experience. Abstraction organizes these observations into conditional principles linking reinforcement placement, crack evolution and load transfer, with supporting evidence and failure conditions. Generalization challenges the model to combine these principles in new architectures and surpass the earlier design record. Campaigns demonstrate progressive refinement and recovery through hypothesis revision, yielding a best composite score 11.0% above the experience-stage record. Fracture analyses connect improved performance to crack deflection and temporary trapping as toughening mechanisms, alongside greater residual load capacity. Learning occurs through external knowledge and decisions, without model-weight updates. This approach offers a route to scientific agents that turn computational experiments into reusable physical knowledge for materials discovery and engineering design.

### Unexpected stabilization of a single-$q$ charge density wave in pristine 1T-TiSe$_2$
- **arXiv**: [2610.18211](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18211)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18211.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Unexpected stabilization of a single-$q$ charge density wave in pristine 1T-TiSe$_2$
- **Authors**: Kimoon Higashihira Han, Dongju Hwang, Dongsin Kim, Yeojin Ahn, Jaehun Cha, Seonggeon Gim, Gyubin Lee, Mingi Jho, Chan-young Lim, Sae Hee Ryu, Chris Jozwiak, Aaron Bostwick, Eli Rotenberg, Jonathan D. Denlinger, Makoto Hashimoto, Donghui Lu, Young Woo Choi, Gil Young Cho, Yeongkwan Kim
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 1T-TiSe₂的2×2三-q电荷密度波并非三个等价分量同时形成，而是先建立单向2×1单-q序，另两个分量在更低温度依次出现，最终构成不等价的三-q相。

Over the past decade, studies revisiting the classical charge-density-wave system 1T-TiSe$_2$, long understood to host the $2 \times 2$ triple-$q$ order with equivalent subcomponents, have suggested that these subcomponents may not be identical, challenging a 50-year-old paradigm. Here, we demonstrate not only that these subcomponents are non-identical but also that they develop sequentially rather than simultaneously. By tracking folded-band features, angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy reveals that a unidirectional $2 \times 1$ single-$q$ order stabilizes first, while the other two components emerge sequentially at lower temperatures, completing the triple-$q$ phase. Our free-energy analysis explains this hierarchy through repulsive coupling among the three subcomponents, as supported by first-principles calculations incorporating phonon anharmonicity. Consequently, the three subcomponents are inequivalent in both phase and amplitude. These results reconcile reported symmetry breaking and differing characteristic temperatures and establish a framework in which ostensibly simple, high-symmetry charge-density order emerges from temperature-dependent competition and cooperation among inequivalent subcomponents.

### Polarons in Lead-Halide Perovskites: What the Structural Evidence Does and Does Not Establish
- **arXiv**: [2610.17809](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17809)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17809.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Polarons in Lead-Halide Perovskites: What the Structural Evidence Does and Does Not Establish
- **Authors**: Inhee Maeng, Young Mi Lee, Jinwoo Park, Seung Jae Oh, Min-Cherl Jung
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 本文质疑"卤化铅钙钛矿中光生载流子以大极化子形式运动并屏蔽缺陷"的主流图像，基于三项新证据指出极化子形成不等于缺陷容忍，并提出以激发密度依赖的晶格畸变测量来区分二者的实验方案。

Photogenerated carriers in lead-halide perovskites are widely believed to move as large polarons, whose accompanying lattice distortion is thought to screen the carrier from charged defects. Three recent findings call this picture into question. First, time-resolved structural probes now measure the photoinduced lattice distortion directly; yet calculations show that thermal motion of the lattice alone can raise the carrier effective mass-an increase previously taken as evidence for polarons-so that it remains unclear whether the lattice distortion around a carrier is really a polaron. Second, the carrier-lattice coupling strengthens as the material is thinned from 3D to 2D. In the two main families of 2D perovskites, Ruddlesden-Popper and Dion-Jacobson, the carrier couples to the lattice through different channels, and their contrasting behavior is set by which coupling channel dominates rather than by lattice stiffness alone. Third, the first direct test of defect tolerance does not point to polaron screening; its authors attribute defect tolerance to the soft, disordered lattice. New transport data show that polaron screening reduces scattering between carriers, but not that it protects carriers from defects. Polaron formation, even if established, therefore does not establish polarons as the cause of defect tolerance. Separating the two requires measuring the lattice distortion as a function of excitation density, over a range spanning the low densities of a solar cell under sunlight and the much higher densities used in most ultrafast experiments. On this basis, we propose experiments that can clarify how photogenerated carriers behave in lead-halide perovskites.

### Quantitative Modelling of Solvation in 2D Materials via Alchemical Decoupling and Potential Energy Surfaces
- **arXiv**: [2610.18490](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18490)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18490.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Quantitative Modelling of Solvation in 2D Materials via Alchemical Decoupling and Potential Energy Surfaces
- **Authors**: Lindsey N. Ioos, Duncan A. Peterson, Scott C. Warren
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cond-mat.mes-hall, cond-mat.soft
- **TL;DR**: 提出一种用溶剂化吉布斯自由能校准"完全脱溶剂"与"完全溶剂化"两个不连通势能面的建模策略，可更准确预测2D材料在溶剂中的稳定性与聚集/剥离行为。

Understanding why 2D materials remain suspended in solutions is essential to understanding the mechanisms of aggregation and exfoliation. These dynamics have been difficult to model because of the long timescales and large activation energies involved in these simulations. Here we describe an improved strategy for modelling the solvation and stability of 2D materials that uses the Gibbs free energy of solvation to accurately calibrate two disconnected potential energy surfaces for the fully desolvated and fully solvated 2D materials. By joining these surfaces using solvation energy, we obtain predictions for 2D material stability in various solvents that are qualitatively different than prior simulations and that have excellent agreement with experimental results. Our approach predicts a much higher barrier to full desolvation of the material, suggesting that materials that appear to be aggregated in fact still retain monolayers of solvent between adjacent layers of 2D materials. This work provides much needed understanding on the interactions between the solvent and 2D material that can guide future experimental work.

### Orthoexciton-to-paraexciton conversion rate in Cu$_2$O at sub-Kelvin temperatures
- **arXiv**: [2610.17812](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17812)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17812.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Orthoexciton-to-paraexciton conversion rate in Cu$_2$O at sub-Kelvin temperatures
- **Authors**: Yusuke Morita, Yukihiro Akiyama, Yuki Haita, Yuga Hirota, Kosuke Yoshioka
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 在1 K以下测量Cu2O中1s正激子到1s仲激子的转换速率，发现其随温度降低持续减小且温度依赖很弱，与先前"低于1.7 K趋于恒定"的预期相悖。

We measured the conversion rate from 1s orthoexcitons to 1s paraexcitons in Cu$_2$O below 1 K using a dilution refrigerator. To determine the conversion rate, we performed time-resolved luminescence spectroscopy of 1s orthoexcitons and 1s paraexcitons confined in a strain-induced trap potential. From the time evolution of the $Γ$$_3$$^{-}$-LO-phonon-assisted luminescence of 1s orthoexcitons, we obtained a conversion rate of $5.9 \times 10^7$ s${^{-1}}$ at a mixing-chamber temperature of 64 mK under an applied stress of 2.2 kbar. Between 64 and 800 mK, the conversion rate depends only weakly on temperature, and it is less than half of the value of $1.6 \times 10^8$ s${^{-1}}$ reported at 2 K under the same applied stress. The conversion rate therefore continues to decrease below 2 K, contrary to the previous expectation that it becomes nearly constant below about 1.7 K. We discuss these results in terms of the momentum distribution of orthoexcitons, which does not reach equilibrium with the lattice during the conversion at these low temperatures. These findings call for a theory that accounts for both the small magnitude of the conversion rate and its weak temperature dependence below 1 K.

### Superexchange coupling between a dilute Gd3+ probe and orphan spins in a valence-bond glass
- **arXiv**: [2610.18722](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18722)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18722.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Superexchange coupling between a dilute Gd3+ probe and orphan spins in a valence-bond glass
- **Authors**: Patrick Colgan, Artur Solodovnyk, Jeremy P. Carlo, Scott Dietrich
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.dis-nn, cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 通过稀释Gd^3+探针的EPR测量，揭示Ba2YMoO6价键玻璃态中"孤儿"自旋的耦合强度(J~0.2 K)与动力学退相干通道。

The valence-bond-glass state of the 4d^1 double perovskite Ba2YMoO6 combines Mo^5+ singlets with residual paramagnetic "orphan" spins whose coupling is hard to reach with bulk probes. We probe them by electron paramagnetic resonance of a dilute Gd^3+ ion on the Y^3+ site via broadband coplanar-waveguide transmission at 5 K. The isostructural nonmagnetic niobate Ba2YNbO6, doped with the same Gd^3+ probe, is a fixed-lattice control. The Gd^3+ linewidth nearly doubles in the magnetic host. Dipolar coupling to the sparse orphans is nearly an order of magnitude too weak; the excess is instead reproduced by a transferred f-d superexchange of J ~ 0.2 K, so the width is a static measure of orphan-spin coupling. The half-field (Delta m_S = +/-2) satellite collapses in the magnetic host, a dynamical dephasing channel, while the unchanged g-factor marks a zero-mean random internal field. Together the two channels probe the coupling and dynamics of valence-bond-glass orphan spins.

### ARES: a plane-wave density-functional-theory code in Rust
- **arXiv**: [2610.17854](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17854)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17854.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: ARES: a plane-wave density-functional-theory code in Rust
- **Authors**: Zehan Liu, Qian Wang, Xiangyu Zheng, Jiaxiang Li, Chuntian Wu, Yongshuo Chen, Xuecheng Shao, Qiang Xu, Yu Xie, Yanming Ma
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: ARES is a Rust-based plane-wave DFT code (22-crate workspace) supporting NC/PAW pseudopotentials, LDA/PBE, k-point sampling, forces/stress/relaxation/bands/DOS, serial or MPI; its key engineering feature is a fixture-based validation harness that continuously re-derives Quantum ESPRESSO reference data, with band-energy median error &lt;0.4 meV and pseudopotential delta factor 0.696 meV/atom over 65 elements.
