ARES (Ab initio atomic mateRial modEling Software at JLU) is a plane-wave density-functional-theory (DFT) code written entirely in Rust as a layered 22-crate workspace. It supports norm-conserving pseudopotentials and the projector augmented-wave (PAW) method with the local-density approximation (LDA) and Perdew-Burke-Ernzerhof (PBE) functionals, Γ-only and explicit k-point sampling, smearing and mixing, forces, stress, relaxation, band structures, densities of states (DOS) and dispersion corrections; execution is serial or MPI-distributed over k-point pools, band groups and plane-wave rows. The code's distinguishing engineering choice is its validation apparatus. Every numerical component is checked against Quantum ESPRESSO driven from the same physical and numerical inputs, and the comparison is organized as numbered fixtures kept in the repository whose reference data is regenerated by a script instead of being captured once. On six materials spanning a semiconductor, three metals and two two-dimensional systems, band energies within one electronvolt of the Fermi level agree with the reference to a median below 0.4 meV. The pseudopotentials the code reads are validated against all-electron reference data as well: the median delta factor is 0.696 meV/atom over all 65 paired elements. Restrictions are stated with the capabilities: two functionals, no spin-polarized or spin-orbit path, a stress implementation with an explicit scope, and no accelerator execution.

### Thermal stability and structural ordering of thick Co$_2$MnAl films grown on MgO and Si substrates
- **arXiv**: [2610.17955](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17955)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17955.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Thermal stability and structural ordering of thick Co$_2$MnAl films grown on MgO and Si substrates
- **Authors**: David Robles-Cuenca, Juan Antonio González, Juan Pedro Andrés, Miguel Ángel Arranz, Mohamed Salaheldeen, Ricardo López Antón
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该文研究750 nm厚Co2MnAl薄膜在MgO(001)和Si衬底上的热集成路线：MgO上600°C实现B2有序，Si上超过500°C发生互扩散导致A2无序与失效。

While most studies on Co$2$MnAl (CMA) focus on the ultrathin regime for spintronic junctions, applications scaling with magnetic volume--such as Anomalous Nernst Effect (ANE) energy harvesters, integrated power inductors, and magnetic MEMS--require significantly thicker films. In this work, 750 nm thick CMA films were grown on MgO (001) and Si substrates to establish a roadmap for their thermal integration. Structural analysis reveals that B2 ordering is optimized on MgO at 600 $^\circ$C, which is important for functional devices. In contrast, samples on Si substrates exhibit a disorder transition toward the A2 phase, closely linked to diffusion-driven degradation processes. Above 500 $^\circ$C, Si interdiffusion into the Heusler layer triggers the formation of interfacial silicides and Kirkendall voids, leading to microcracking, electrical resistivity jumps, and a final fully disordered A2 phase. Despite the Mn-poor stoichiometry (Co$2.25$Mn$0.7$Al$1.05$), the films maintain a robust saturation magnetization of ~600 emu/cm$^3$, consistent with recent theoretical models for off-stoichiometric Heusler alloys. These results define the processing windows for integrating thick CMA layers into multifunctional CMOS-compatible platforms.

### A hierarchy of exact lower bounds on the Laughlin spectral gap
- **arXiv**: [2610.18854](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18854)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18854.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: A hierarchy of exact lower bounds on the Laughlin spectral gap
- **Authors**: Michael G. Scheer, Eslam Khalaf
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 本文提出一种基于"bootstrap"+半定规划(SDP)的方法,给出球面上第一 Haldane 赝势哈密顿量谱隙的严格下界,对任意磁通数 N_Φ 乃至热力学极限均成立。

We develop a bootstrap approach to deriving lower bounds on the spectral gap of the first Haldane pseudopotential Hamiltonian on the sphere with $N_Φ$ flux quanta for arbitrary $N_Φ$, including in the thermodynamic limit $N_Φ\to \infty$. Our approach can in principle be generalized to pseudopotentials describing other model wavefunctions such as the Moore-Read state or Read-Rezayi sequence. Our approach is based on Knabe's observation that if $H$ is a Hamiltonian with ground state energy $0$ then the spectral gap is at least $ε&gt; 0$ if and only if $H^2 \succeq εH$. To show this inequality, we split $H^2$ into two parts, one that can be analytically bounded and another that can be bounded using a finite semidefinite program (SDP). Crucially, the size of this program is determined only by cutoff parameters, and the program depends on $N_Φ$ only parametrically, allowing us to obtain numerically exact (up to solver precision) lower bounds on the gap. In order to turn these numerical bounds into mathematically rigorous bounds, we introduce an algorithm that can produce nearly-optimal exact feasible points for large rational SDPs. We additionally provide an algorithm that establishes the rank stability of the semidefinite program in the limit $N_Φ\to \infty$ which is needed to certify the bound in the thermodynamic limit. Beyond establishing the Laughlin gap and revealing a potential method for deriving spectral gap lower bounds for other pseudopotentials, our work provides a general method for turning numerical semidefinite programming bounds into rigorous mathematical proofs, which may have wide applicability.

### Field-free Magnetoacoustic Nonreciprocity via Exchange-bias Engineering
- **arXiv**: [2610.17979](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17979)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17979.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Field-free Magnetoacoustic Nonreciprocity via Exchange-bias Engineering
- **Authors**: Weiqi Du, Mingyuan Ma, Zheng Sun, Chong Chen, Shixuan Liang, Deqi Tao, Shihai An, Yifan Fu, Sulei Fu, Feng Pan, Cheng Song
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该工作展示了基于交换偏置工程的零外场磁声非互易传播。

Nonreciprocal wave propagation is essential for directional signal processing and isolation. Magnetoacoustic systems are attractive platforms for nonreciprocal devices because of their small footprint and passive operation. However, pronounced nonreciprocity typically requires an external bias field,limiting device miniaturization and integration. Here, we demonstrate field-free magnetoacoustic nonreciprocity in an IrMn/FeGaB/Cu/FeGaB multilayer integrated with a shear-horizontal-wave delay line. Exchange-bias engineering stabilizes a remanent magnetic state with broken time-reversal and spatial-inversion symmetries while tailoring the spin-wave dispersion to achieve resonant magnetoacoustic coupling at zero field. The normalized isolation reaches 21 dB/mm, with its sign programmable by the field-sweep history. Further numerical optimization predicts a zero-field normalized isolation of 91 dB/mm by increasing the dynamical magnetic asymmetry. Our work establishes exchange-bias engineering as a promising route toward compact RF isolators and circulators and as a general strategy for realizing magnetoacoustic devices without external magnetic fields.

### Knowledge-Guided Construction of Machine Learning Potentials for Reactive Behavior in Energetic Materials
- **arXiv**: [2610.17987](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17987)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.17987.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Knowledge-Guided Construction of Machine Learning Potentials for Reactive Behavior in Energetic Materials
- **Authors**: Guanchen Dong, Huanrong Liu, Lin Wang, Yuxuan Chen, Zhengyu Tu, Qing-an Li, Bin Xu, Jian Li, Shan Zhang, Xiaotong Li, Linghua Tan, Guangcheng Yang, Jun Kang, Rui Su, Pengfei Guan
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 提出改进球形谐波基组sphmulti与主动学习策略，仅用13,680构型即构建可靠的神经网络势，成功描述含能材料DAAzF的关键性质与动力学机制。

Energetic materials involve multiple interactions, making reliable machine-learning potentials (MLPs) difficult to construct without extensive datasets. Here, we enhance the ability of the neural network interatomic potential (NNAP) to describe molecular systems using sphmulti, a refined spherical-harmonic basis set that combines extra radial basis with element-specific chemical weighting in each radial channel. We further integrate hyperactive learning, farthest-point sampling, and iterative sufficiency criteria with knowledge-guided key events, enabling the NNAP to retain essential information for describing the material system. For 3,3'-diamino-4,4'-azoxyfurazan, the resulting NNAP is trained on only 13,680 configurations yet captures key properties, including the isomerization barrier, pyrolysis activation energy, and shock Hugoniot behavior. NNAP-driven molecular dynamics simulations further reveal that pyrolysis proceeds through furazan-ring opening followed by chain reactions, whereas shock loading induces anisotropic hydrogen transfer driven by interlayer structural deformations. This work enables efficient and reliable MLPs for energetic materials.

### Twofold Symmetry Observed in Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe Interfacial Superconductor
- **arXiv**: [2307.05904](https://arxiv.org/abs/2307.05904)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2307.05904.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Twofold Symmetry Observed in Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe Interfacial Superconductor
- **Authors**: Xinru Han, Hailang Qin, Tianluo Pan, Bin Guo, Kaige Shi, Zijin Huang, Jie Jiang, Hangyu Yin, Hongtao He, Fei Ye, Wei-Qiang Chen, Jia-Wei Mei, Gan Wang
- **Categories**: cond-mat.supr-con, cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 该文通过扫描隧道显微镜准粒子干涉(QPI)与宏观磁阻测量，在界面超导体Bi2Te3/FeTe中观测到二重超导能隙对称性，并与FeTe的Te-Te方向对齐。

Superconducting pairing symmetry are crucial in understanding the microscopic superconducting mechanism of a superconductor. Here we report the observation of a twofold superconducting gap symmetry in an interfacial superconductor Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe, by employing quasiparticle interference (QPI) technique in scanning tunneling microscopy and macroscopic magnetoresistance measurements. The QPI patterns corresponding to energies inside and outside the gap reveal a clear anisotropic superconducting gap. Furthermore, both the in-plane angle-dependent magnetoresistance and in-plane upper critical field exhibit a clear twofold symmetry. This twofold symmetry align with the Te-Te direction in FeTe, which weakens the possible generation by bi-collinear antiferromagnetism order. Our finding provides key information in further understanding of the topological properties in Bi$_{2}$Te$_{3}$/FeTe superconducting system and propels further theoretical interests in the paring mechanism in the system.

### Hydrogen partial pressure as a key parameter in the reactive sputtering of photochromic YHO thin films
- **arXiv**: [2610.18068](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18068)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18068.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Hydrogen partial pressure as a key parameter in the reactive sputtering of photochromic YHO thin films
- **Authors**: E. Strods, M. Zubkins, E. Letko, V. Vibornijs, A. Sarakovskis, J. Purans
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 通过调控反应磁控溅射中H2/Ar流量比并辅以空气后氧化制备YHO薄膜，发现低H2/Ar比有利于高透明性、高光致变色对比度和快速漂白动力学。

Oxygen-containing yttrium hydride (YHO) thin films were synthesised by reactive magnetron sputtering over a wide range of hydrogen-to-argon gas flow ratios (H2/Ar) during yttrium hydride deposition, followed by post-oxidation in air. The deposition process was monitored in situ by plasma optical emission spectroscopy. X-ray diffraction reveals a strong dependence of crystallographic texture on the H2/Ar ratio: highly &lt;100&gt;-textured films formed at low H2/Ar ratios progressively evolve into more polycrystalline microstructures with slightly reduced crystallite size as the hydrogen content increases. The optical transmittance gradually decreases from approximately 85 % to 70 % as the H2/Ar ratio is raised to 0.6, followed by a sharp drop at higher hydrogen contents. The photochromic contrast of 705-845 nm thick YHO films, measured after 10 min of illumination with a solar simulator, decreases from 14 % to 7 % as the H2/Ar ratio increases from 0.08 to 0.45. Thinner films (365-505 nm) exhibit a similar but less pronounced trend. Bleaching kinetics, analysed using a double-exponential model, indicate faster recovery, with effective bleaching time constants of approximately 1-15 min for films deposited at H2/Ar &lt; 0.25. X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy reveals higher oxygen incorporation in films deposited at lower H2/Ar ratios, deviating from the commonly reported correlation between oxygen content and photochromic response in YHO. These results demonstrate that low hydrogen partial pressure during reactive sputtering is advantageous for achieving higher optical transparency, enhanced photochromic contrast, and faster bleaching kinetics in YHO thin films.

### Tellurium trioxide as an n-type wide-gap semiconductor with ultrahigh work functions from first principles
- **arXiv**: [2610.18076](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18076)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18076.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Tellurium trioxide as an n-type wide-gap semiconductor with ultrahigh work functions from first principles
- **Authors**: Tianwei Wang, Teruya Nagafuji, Kota Hanzawa, Hidenori Hiramatsu, Fumiyasu Oba
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 第一性原理计算表明，β-TeO3（带隙&gt;3 eV）可成为功函数高达~8 eV的n型半导体，适合空穴注入/传输层与透明电极，但需精准控制O化学势以抑制表面氧空位。

The exploration of next-generation wide-gap semiconductors with extreme work functions is highly desirable for the continued advancement of electronic and optoelectronic devices. By using first principles calculations, we show that \b{eta}-TeO3, with a wide band gap exceeding 3 eV, emerges as a promising n-type semiconductor exhibiting ultrahigh work functions of up to ~8 eV. Intentionally or unintentionally incorporated interstitial H, Li, Na, Cu, Mg, and Zn impurities and substitutional F impurities are identified as shallow donors, whereas O vacancies act as minor carrier-compensating defects that locally trap electrons at neighbouring Te sites. The work function is evaluated across a series of low-index nonpolar surfaces. The presence of ordered O vacancies is energetically favoured on these surfaces and tends to reduce the corresponding work functions. These findings underscore the significance of precise control over the O chemical potential to suppress surface O-vacancy formation, thereby preserving the exceptional electronic performance of \b{eta}-TeO3 associated with ultrahigh work functions. Collectively, the results highlight the potential of \b{eta}-TeO3 for hole injection/extraction/transport layers as well as transparent electrodes for applications such as light-emitting devices and photovoltaic cells, while providing useful guidance on carrier doping and surface tuning for these practical applications through elucidating the effects of the point-defect and surface configurations on the electronic properties.

### High-throughput screening of Heusler alloys for thermoelectrics combining machine-learned densities of states and first-principles transport
- **arXiv**: [2610.18179](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18179)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18179.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: High-throughput screening of Heusler alloys for thermoelectrics combining machine-learned densities of states and first-principles transport
- **Authors**: Shivam Randive, Rahul Dixit, Himanshu Pandey
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该文检验了以态密度(DOS)为依据的热电材料筛选策略是否可靠：在DXMag数据库全部106,235个Heusler结构中，用DFT谱筛选51,257个、用神经网络预测谱筛选54,978个，并以3,625次能带与输运计算作为判据。结果显示DOS衍生的Seebeck指标能把大多数通过全部输运与稳定性测试的化合物排进其族群前1%，但预测谱会丢失大部分带隙。

Thermoelectrics turn waste heat into electricity, but finding new ones requires transport calculations too costly for whole databases. Searches therefore choose what to calculate from the electronic density of states (DOS), increasingly predicted by machine learning, yet such choices are rarely tested. We test them on all 106,235 Heusler structures in the DXMag database, screening 51,257 on density-functional spectra and 54,978 on neural-network predictions, and judging both by 3,625 band-structure and transport calculations. A DOS-derived Seebeck indicator ranks most compounds passing every transport and stability test within the top 1% of their population, and ten of the eleven established half-Heusler thermoelectrics calculated pass. Predicted spectra lose most band gaps, unnoticed by spectral-similarity metrics, yet still rank passing compounds near the top. The screen certifies 80 computational candidates, 61 without reported measurements. Computed or predicted, the DOS can choose what to calculate; only a band structure shows whether a gap exists.

### Orientation-Resolved Nanoindentation of Single Crystal Ti-15Mo: Inverted Elastic Anisotropy and Yield-Onset Behavior Beyond Simple CRSS Predictions
- **arXiv**: [2610.18181](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18181)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18181.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Orientation-Resolved Nanoindentation of Single Crystal Ti-15Mo: Inverted Elastic Anisotropy and Yield-Onset Behavior Beyond Simple CRSS Predictions
- **Authors**: Kidus Abebe, David Gaetano, Aurelia Moriyama-Gurish, Jana Šmilauerová, Yanfei Gao, Udo D. Schwarz, Amit Datye
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 通过EBSD联用纳米压痕，量化单晶Ti-15Mo在不同取向下的纳米力学各向异性，发现弹性各向异性出现反常倒置，且屈服起始的取向依赖无法用简单Schmid因子模型解释。

Ti-15Mo (wt%) is a well-established $β$-stabilized alloy whose mechanical response is strongly governed by phase stability and deformation mechanisms that operate at the single-grain scale. Because these intragranular mechanisms are intrinsically anisotropic, understanding the orientation dependence of the elastic and plastic response is critical to the texture-engineered processing of wrought components. Here, we quantify the nanomechanical anisotropy of single-crystal Ti-15Mo by combining EBSD with nanoindentation to correlate hardness, indentation modulus, strain rate sensitivity, and indentation yield response with the crystallographic surface normal near $\langle111\rangle,\langle124\rangle,\langle011\rangle,\langle023\rangle$, and $\langle013\rangle$ directions. While the modulus anisotropy is small (~10%), contrary to the prediction for a cubic crystal with A&gt;1, orientations near $\langle111\rangle$ exhibit the lowest moduli. Yield onset is likewise softest near $\langle111\rangle$ and spans 41%, while hardness spans only 6.6% and follows neither the modulus nor the yield ordering. The alloy also exhibits yield behavior not captured by the simple CRSS/Schmid-factor models evaluated here. Strain-rate-jump tests yield V*=18-31b$^3$, consistent with kink-pair-nucleation-controlled plasticity; based on V*, the orientations separate into two groups suggestive of glide-dominated versus twin/transformation-participating response. We reveal an inversion of the elastic anisotropy expected for a homogeneous cubic $β$-phase and an orientation-dependent yield-onset response that is not reproduced by the simple Schmid-factor models examined here. Retained athermal $ω$ is proposed as the contributor because $β\rightarrowω$ collapse occurs on {111}$_β$ planes and may provide anelastic compliance through the reversible $ω\rightarrowβ$ transformation.

### Coherent Phonon Generation from Prethermal Carrier Populations in a Semiconductor
- **arXiv**: [2610.18279](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18279)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18279.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Coherent Phonon Generation from Prethermal Carrier Populations in a Semiconductor
- **Authors**: Mingyu Shi, Matteo Savoini, Patrick Friebel, Andrea Annunziata, Thomas Metzger, Yunpei Deng, Laura Cattaneo, Steven L. Johnson
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, cond-mat.str-el
- **TL;DR**: 碲中带隙附近激发时，全对称A1相干声子的振幅几乎不随激发光子能量变化，这否定了"热化载流子密度主导"图像；实验与第一性原理表明，由跃迁分辨的预热力（受光学矩阵元与形变势加权）主导相干声子产生，拟合给出约几飞秒的激发跃迁态寿命。

Ultrafast laser excitation of carriers in semiconductors often results in the creation of fully symmetric coherent optical phonons, typically understood in terms of a thermalized transient carrier population. We test this understanding with systematic measurements and first-principles calculations of the photon-energy dependence of ultrafast-laser-driven coherent phonon generation in tellurium, using excitation wavelengths around the electronic band gap. We observe that, contrary to the expectations of models assuming a thermalized carrier population, the coherent amplitude of the fully symmetric $A_1$ mode changes only weakly when tuning wavelength across the band gap. The measured spectral dependence is instead reproduced by a transition-resolved prethermal force weighted by optical matrix elements and band- and momentum-dependent $A_1$ deformation potentials. Fitting this model to the data yields an excited transition state lifetime of a few femtoseconds. These results identify transition selectivity, rather than a thermalized total carrier density, as a key factor controlling coherent-phonon generation in semiconductors excited near a band gap.

### MINFF: A Family of Classical Force Fields for Minerals with Tunable Angle Terms
- **arXiv**: [2610.18220](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18220)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18220.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: MINFF: A Family of Classical Force Fields for Minerals with Tunable Angle Terms
- **Authors**: Michael Holmboe, Nathan Lavauzelle, Rogers E. Swai
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: The paper introduces MINFF, a new force field family for layered and non-layered oxides and hydroxides containing Si, Al, Fe, Mg, Ca, Ti, Li and F. Instead of explicit M-O bond terms, it uses electrostatics and dispersion to set M-O distances plus a single harmonic angle term to control coordination geometry, using DDEC6 metal charges and site-dependent oxygen charges. Two parameter sets (general GMINFF and tailored TMINFF) at four angle force constants provide a tunable transferability-fidelity trade-off, and the force field reproduces unit-cell metrics, distances, angles, elastic constants, vibrational bands and diffraction patterns, while remaining compatible with the OPC3 water model.

Atomistic simulations of minerals are limited by the accuracy and transferability of the force field. We introduce MINFF, a force field family for layered and non-layered oxides and hydroxides of M being Si, Al, Fe, Mg, Ca, Ti, Li and F. MINFF has no M-O bond terms. Instead M-O distances follow from electrostatics and dispersion, while a single harmonic angle term on all M-O triplets controls coordination geometry. Metal charges are DDEC6 charges from reference mineral structures, and oxygen charges are assigned by nearest-neighbour charge smearing, covering basal, apical, hydroxyl and edge sites and sites adjacent to isomorphous substitutions. The oxygen Lennard-Jones parameters are based on the OPC3 water model and change by less than 5% when re-optimised per mineral, so compatibility with OPC3 and related ion-pair potentials is measured rather than assumed. Metal Lennard-Jones parameters were fitted to unit-cell metrics, bond distances and angles, both synchronously across more than 30 reference minerals (general, GMINFF) and for individual minerals (tailored, TMINFF), each at four angle force constants (0, 250, 500 and 1500 kJ/mol/rad^2). With angle terms, MINFF reproduces experimental unit-cells metrics to within 0.5% and atom-type distances and angles to within 0.7-1.4% and 0.1-0.4%, with Global Instability Index values as low as 0.11, below those of CLAYFF (0.34) and INTERFACE (0.21). A moderate angle constant reproduces the stiff in-plane and soft shear elastic constants of phyllosilicates and improves the Kaolinite librational bands, and in hydrated Na-Montmorillonite MINFF gives the lowest residual factor against the 2W 00l pattern of the models tested. The general and tailored sets at four angle stiffnesses give direct control over the transferability-fidelity trade-off. Topologies can be generated with the atom (MATLAB) and atomipy (Python) libraries and at www.atomipy.io.

### Transition-Metal-Induced Uniaxial Anisotropy in YFe$_3$
- **arXiv**: [2610.18303](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18303)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18303.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Transition-Metal-Induced Uniaxial Anisotropy in YFe$_3$
- **Authors**: M. Nur Hasan, Bruno L. Fertig, Santa Pile, Rafael M. Vieira, Alena Vishina, Alexander Kovacs, Gotthard Rieger, Stefan Denneler, Samuel J. R. Holt, Martin Lang, Swapneel Pathak, Andrea Petrocchi, Hans Fangohr, Thomas George Woodcock, Thomas Schrefl, Heike C. Herper
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 通过第一性原理筛选过渡金属（V、Nb、Ta）掺杂实现YFe3从易面到易轴各向异性转变，YFe2.33Ta0.67获得2.3 MJ/m³单轴磁晶各向异性能并保持高居里温度与热稳定性。

Achieving uniaxial magnetocrystalline anisotropy while preserving high magnetization and thermal stability remains a central challenge in the development of Fe-rich permanent magnets. YFe$_3$ is a promising candidate owing to its saturation magnetization and Curie temperature, but its intrinsic easy-plane anisotropy limits its potential for permanent-magnet applications. Here, we show that selective transition-metal substitution drives an easy-plane-to-easy-axis transition in YFe$_3$. Systematic first-principles screening identifies V, Nb, and Ta as effective dopants, with YFe$_{2.33}$Ta$_{0.67}$ exhibiting a large uniaxial magnetocrystalline anisotropy energy of 2.3~MJ/m$^{3}$. Calculated exchange interactions and atomistic Monte Carlo simulations show that the enhanced anisotropy is achieved while preserving robust ferromagnetic order and high thermal stability. Micromagnetic simulations further reveal that YFe$_{2.33}$Ta$_{0.67}$ exhibits the highest coercivity and strongest resistance to irreversible demagnetization, whereas YFe$_{2.33}$V$_{0.67}$ achieves a comparable maximum energy product at 300~K. Experimental characterization of V-alloyed YFe$_3$ shows that the YFe$_3$ phase can be retained at low nominal V concentrations (1-3 at.\%) under the employed synthesis and heat-treatment conditions, whereas higher V contents lead to increasingly multiphase samples. Concentration-dependent MAE calculations reveal a sign reversal between approximately 11 and 14~at.\% V, marking the transition from easy-plane to uniaxial easy-axis anisotropy. Consistent with this concentration dependence, electron backscatter diffraction and Kerr microscopy reveal in-plane magnetic domain structures in the experimentally accessible low-V regime. Therefore, our work offers a promising approach to tuning the magnetocrystalline anisotropy of YFe$_3$ while retaining high Curie temperatures.

### Accurately Generalizing Deep-Learning Hamiltonian Methods to Plane-Wave Density Functional Theory
- **arXiv**: [2610.18317](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18317)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18317.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Accurately Generalizing Deep-Learning Hamiltonian Methods to Plane-Wave Density Functional Theory
- **Authors**: Chuntian Wu, Jiaxiang Li, Qiang Xu, Xuecheng Shao, Congwei Xie, Yu Xie, Yanming Ma
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 提出一种"可投影性引导"的方法，从少量精选的平面波(PW)本征态与本征值构造非正交原子轨道(AO)哈密顿量，使含非局域算符的杂化泛函PW计算也能用于AO哈密顿量学习。

Deep neural networks that predict density functional theory (DFT) Hamiltonians from material structures offer a route to efficient electronic-structure calculations. However, extending localized atomic-orbital (AO) Hamiltonian learning to plane-wave (PW) calculations with hybrid functionals requires a compact AO representation that preserves the effects of nonlocal operators. Here we propose a projectability-guided construction of nonorthogonal AO Hamiltonians from a small, selected set of PW eigenstates and eigenvalues. High-projectability states are retained, and selected higher-energy states complete the AO subspace, avoiding an empirical shift and its arbitrary contribution to the learning targets. Because the construction uses PW eigenstates and eigenvalues, it incorporates nonlocal contributions present in the reference calculation without explicitly reconstructing their AO matrix elements. Benchmarks for MoS2 and sodium phases show accurate reproduction of PW bands with smaller AO bases than real-space reconstruction. The resulting PBE and HSE Hamiltonians can be learned by equivariant neural networks, connecting PW reference calculations with electronic-structure prediction. This approach broadens the range of PW DFT calculations available for AO Hamiltonian learning and enables compact, accurate training targets for developing more widely applicable electronic-structure models.

### Reversible Magneto-ionic Modification of Metallic Magnetic Thin Films
- **arXiv**: [2610.18396](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18396)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18396.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Reversible Magneto-ionic Modification of Metallic Magnetic Thin Films
- **Authors**: Md Golam Hafiz, Hari Babu Vasili, Philippa Shepley, Mannan Ali, Andrew J. Britton, Oscar Cespedes, Weibin Li, Manuel Valvidares, Rohit Pachat, Wilfried Fotso, Mourad Cherif, Shimpei Ono, Yves Roussigne, Mohamed Belmeguenai, Gavin Burnell
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 利用离子液体门控实现氧离子在金属多层膜中的电驱动迁移，从而非易失、可逆地调控HM/FM界面氧化态，改变磁各向异性、矫顽场、畴壁钉扎与DMI。

Magnetic multilayers with structural inversion asymmetry, perpendicular magnetic anisotropy (PMA), and large Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI) are widely studied for magnetic domain wall- and skyrmion-based data processing applications. In this work, we report a controlled, nonvolatile, but reversible local modification of such a system by electrically driven oxygen migration through the metallic film structure by using ionic liquid gating. Our findings provide direct evidence for the modification of the oxidation state at the heavy metal (HM)/ferromagnet (FM) interface. As a result, we observed changes in fundamental magnetic properties, such as an increase in the effective anisotropy constant (Keff) and coercive field (Hc) with oxidation, and a decrease with the reduction of oxygen ions. Positive gate voltages relative to the thin film extract oxygen ions from the magnetic layer, significantly reducing the domain nucleation field. In contrast, negative voltages drive oxygen ions into the magnetic layer, leading to a reversible increase in the extent of domain wall pinning near saturation. We observe a corresponding decrease in the magnetic moment and DMI, with negative voltage reducing both. This magneto-ionic modulation in fully metallic structures is beneficial for spintronic device applications, particularly in field-programmable domain wall and skyrmion devices.

### Substrate-controlled epitaxial-like growth of photochromic yttrium oxyhydride thin films
- **arXiv**: [2610.18448](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18448)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18448.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Substrate-controlled epitaxial-like growth of photochromic yttrium oxyhydride thin films
- **Authors**: M. Zubkins, E. Strods, E. Letko, H. Mändar, D. Moldarev, K. Mizohata, K. Sarakinos, V. Vibornijs, K. Kundzins, J. Purans
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 采用反应磁控溅射在YSZ(111)/(100)单晶衬底上200°C沉积β-YHx并空气中氧化制备YHO薄膜，系统研究衬底取向对结构、成分与光致变色性能的影响，发现光响应主要受成分控制而非外延结构。

Yttrium oxyhydride (YHO) thin films are synthesised by reactive magnetron sputtering of \b{eta}-YHx precursor films onto single-crystalline yttria-stabilised zirconia (YSZ) (111) and (100) substrates at 200 °C, followed by post-deposition oxidation in air. For comparison, reference films are also prepared on amorphous SiO2 and soda-lime glass substrates at both 200 °C and room temperature (RT). The structural (X-ray diffraction and scanning electron microscopy), compositional (elastic recoil detection analysis), and optical properties, including photochromic behaviour, of the films are systematically investigated. The results reveal a strong influence of substrate orientation on the film microstructure and crystallographic texture, leading to epitaxial-like growth with a mosaic spread of ~3-4°. YHO deposited on YSZ(100) exhibits larger crystallite sizes (10-20 nm) than YHO grown on YSZ(111) (~8 nm) and exhibits residual in-plane compressive strain while remaining largely relaxed with respect to the substrate. Compositional analysis revealed that the O/Y and O/H atomic ratios ranged from 0.71 to 0.82 and from 0.33 to 0.44, respectively, for films deposited under optimised conditions. These films exhibit photochromic behaviour under solar simulator illumination, with relative transmittance changes ranging from 7 to 22 % after 10 min of illumination. Despite the pronounced structural differences, the epitaxial-like films show no systematic improvement in photochromic response, indicating that the effect is governed primarily by composition. The study shows that the bleaching time constant increases exponentially with increasing contrast reached during illumination, rather than representing an intrinsic material constant of YHO.

### Flexoelectricity and Flexomagnetism
- **arXiv**: [2610.18525](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18525)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18525.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Flexoelectricity and Flexomagnetism
- **Authors**: Alexander Edström, Massimiliano Stengel
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 综述应变梯度（在弯曲低维体系中即曲率）作为内禀对称破缺场，所引发电极化（挠曲电）与磁性（挠曲磁）耦合。

Strain gradients, unlike homogeneous strain, are odd under space inversion and unlock couplings that are strictly forbidden in the parent crystal. At the nanoscale, and in curved low-dimensional systems in particular, such gradients are readily attainable and often unavoidable, so that curvature acts as a symmetry-breaking field written into the lattice itself. This chapter reviews the resulting couplings to electric polarization and magnetism. After establishing the geometric relation between strain gradients and curvature, we review flexoelectricity as the better-established reference case: the bulk, surface and dynamic contributions that complicate its definition, its microscopic mechanisms, its manifestation in two-dimensional materials, and the first-principles methodology now available for its calculation. Using the magnetoelectric effect as a conceptual bridge, we then develop flexomagnetism in a broad sense, covering any coupling phenomena between magnetism and strain gradients, and specifically discuss the linear magnetization response to curvature and curvature-induced Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions, beyond the established curvilinear treatment. Finally, we briefly describe some additional effects fitting under this broad umbrella of flexomagnetism, including nonlinear ones, inhomogeneous magnetoelectric and Rashba-type phenomena, and connections to altermagnetism.

### Imaging Gate-Tunable Single-Layer Molecular Kagome Lattices
- **arXiv**: [2610.18539](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18539)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18539.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Imaging Gate-Tunable Single-Layer Molecular Kagome Lattices
- **Authors**: Ziyi Wang, Rafal Zuzak, Weichen Tang, Haowen Pang, Yutang Qiao, Dhanvanth Balakrishnan, Yuqiao Yan, Aining Hu, Andrew Sun, Takashi Taniguchi, Kenji Watanabe, Feng Wang, Steven G. Louie, Michael F. Crommie
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 首次在石墨烯等范德华衬底上外延生长单层Ni₃(HITP)₂金属有机框架kagome晶格，观测到平带与van Hove奇异性特征，并通过栅压实现对这些电子态的能量调控。

Kagome lattices provide a simple geometry for engineering electronic states that exhibit novel quantum behavior. In conventional kagome materials, however, the relevant states are often embedded in chemically complex three-dimensional crystals and are difficult to isolate or tune. Molecule-based kagome networks offer structural precision and tunability, but have largely been confined to metallic substrates incompatible with 2D devices. Here we report growth of single-layer Ni$_3$(HITP)$_2$ metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) on van der Waals substrates including graphene-based field-effect devices using metal-salt molecular beam epitaxy. Low-temperature scanning tunneling spectroscopy resolves kagome-derived flat-band and van Hove singularity-based spectral features consistent with first-principles calculations. Gating hybrid 2D MOF devices allows us to energetically shift kagome spectral features, thus establishing electrostatic control of chemically programmable 2D kagome electronic lattices.

### Direct growth of crystalline spinel GaON films: A wide-bandgap mixed-anion semiconductor
- **arXiv**: [2610.18550](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18550)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18550.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Direct growth of crystalline spinel GaON films: A wide-bandgap mixed-anion semiconductor
- **Authors**: Baikui Li, Zecheng Yang, Wenhao Yin, Zijing Wu, Haitao Zhao, Yunwei Sheng, Honglei Wu, Jiannong Wang
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci, physics.app-ph
- **TL;DR**: 通过氮等离子体辅助脉冲激光沉积直接生长出晶态尖晶石型γ-GaON薄膜，并测定其带边跃迁约为4.3 eV。

Mixed-anion semiconductors offer opportunities for bandgap engineering beyond conventional multication alloys, but competing phases and disorder complicate their synthesis and the identification of their electronic properties. Gallium oxynitride (GaON) could connect the technologically important GaN and Ga2O3 systems, yet establishing a clear relation between its crystal structure, composition and bandgap remains challenging. Here we report the direct growth of crystalline spinel gamma-GaON films by nitrogen-plasma-assisted pulsed-laser deposition. Varying the nitrogen and oxygen supply yields monoclinic beta-Ga2O3, predominantly disordered films and crystalline spinel GaON. Complementary diffraction, atomic-resolution microscopy and elemental analysis establish the spinel structure and nitrogen incorporation. Temperature-dependent photocurrent spectroscopy reveals a clear redshift of the crystalline gamma-GaON response, supporting assignment of a band-edge transition near 4.3 eV at room temperature. These results open opportunities to investigate mixed-anion wide-bandgap semiconductor physics and develop heterostructures.

### Time-Resolved Phonon Dispersions of Thermally Excited Graphite at the Nanoscale
- **arXiv**: [2610.18561](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18561)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18561.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Time-Resolved Phonon Dispersions of Thermally Excited Graphite at the Nanoscale
- **Authors**: Mairi McCauley, Christoph T. Koch, Benedikt Haas
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 本文结合动量分辨振动EELS、脉冲激光加热与同步门控电子探测，首次实现微秒尺度时间分辨的声子色散测量，观测到石墨光学声子支随升温能量下降、峰宽增大，归因于非谐相互作用导致的声子重整化与散射。

Lattice vibrations govern the propagation of heat in materials, and investigating their dynamics through time-resolved phonon dispersion measurements would provide insight into these excitations and their interactions. However, time-resolved measurements that directly resolve the phonon dispersion at the nanoscale have not been previously demonstrated. Momentum-resolved vibrational electron energy loss spectroscopy (EELS) provides access to phonon energies and momenta with nanoscale spatial resolution. Here, we combine momentum-resolved vibrational EELS with pulsed laser heating and synchronised gated electron detection to observe the phonon dispersion of graphite during controlled heating and cooling on the microsecond timescale. Recording both the gain and loss spectra at defined times relative to laser excitation, we determine the temperature at each time delay and follow changes to the phonon branches as a function of temperature. The momentum-resolved spectra reveal a decrease in energy of the optical phonon branch with increasing temperature accompanied by an increase in peak width. These changes are consistent with temperature-dependent phonon renormalisation and scattering associated with anharmonic interactions. The approach is compatible with detector gating on timescales down to 50 ns, providing a route towards extending these measurements to faster dynamics. This work demonstrates time-resolved phonon dispersion spectroscopy using vibrational EELS and provides a route towards probing temperature-dependent phonon dynamics at the nanoscale with simultaneous energy and momentum resolution.

### Composition-controlled optical fingerprints and anisotropy of self-trapped excitons in double perovskites
- **arXiv**: [2610.18665](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18665)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18665.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Composition-controlled optical fingerprints and anisotropy of self-trapped excitons in double perovskites
- **Authors**: Simone Grillo, Offir Zachs Maishlos, Liya Koren, Rotem Strassberg, Rachel Lifer, Edi Maizenberg, Umberto De Giovannini, Angel Rubio, Yehonadav Bekenstein, Ofer Neufeld
- **Categories**: cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 该研究结合光谱与第一性原理计算，考察Na至K的B位合金化对无铅Sb掺杂Cs2BInCl6纳米晶的影响，揭示自陷激子（STE）主导的红移发光及其组分调控机制，并以偏振分辨光谱探测STE波函数。

Double perovskites (DPs) offer a compositionally versatile platform for photocatalysis and optoelectronics, but their soft lattices favor strong exciton-phonon coupling and self-trapped excitons (STEs), which produce broad, red-shifted photoluminescence (PL). Here, we combine optical spectroscopy and ab initio calculations to study lead-free, Sb-doped Cs2BInCl6 nanocrystals with B-site alloying from Na to K. Alloying tunes the PL energy from about 2.48 to 2.70 eV, while absorption features near 3.8 eV remain nearly unchanged. Calculations reveal that Sb doping enables bright radiative recombination channels and delocalizes the excitonic wavefunction. We identify STEs responsible for the large PL red shift, associated with an upward shift of dopant-hybridized in-gap states. Na-to-K substitution selectively changes the STE lattice distortion, explaining why PL varies strongly with composition while absorption does not. Theory further predicts pronounced PL polarization anisotropy that reflects the STE wavefunction's spatial structure and depends on B-site composition. Time-resolved anisotropy measurements closely agree with these predictions, providing experimental fingerprints of the excitonic wavefunction and its evolution with alloying. Together, these results disentangle the roles of doping and alloying in lead-free DPs and establish polarization-resolved spectroscopy as a probe of STE wavefunctions.

### Revisiting magnetoelectric response in collinear antiferromagnetic zigzag chains: A downfolding approach beyond conventional low-energy models
- **arXiv**: [2605.07118](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07118)  ·  [PDF](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2605.07118.pdf)
- **Title (EN)**: Revisiting magnetoelectric response in collinear antiferromagnetic zigzag chains: A downfolding approach beyond conventional low-energy models
- **Authors**: Shuhei Kanda, Satoru Hayami
- **Categories**: cond-mat.str-el, cond-mat.mtrl-sci
- **TL;DR**: 本文用含s、p轨道的多轨道紧束缚模型研究共线反铁磁锯齿链的磁电(ME)效应微观机制，指出ME响应由s-p杂化激活的轨道自由度主导（自旋贡献因自旋守恒为零），并提出通过引入轨道杂化顶点修正与准粒子重整化的重整化Kubo公式以正确描述低能有效模型。

Magnetoelectric (ME) effects in antiferromagnets provide a fertile platform for exploring symmetry-driven cross-correlated responses. However, their microscopic origin remains elusive and is often obscured in simplified low-energy descriptions. In this study, we revisit the microscopic mechanism of the ME effect in a collinear antiferromagnetic zigzag chain by employing a multi-orbital tight-binding model that explicitly includes both $s$- and $p$-orbital degrees of freedom. Using analytical and numerical calculations based on the Kubo formula, we demonstrate that the ME response is governed by orbital degrees of freedom activated through $s$--$p$ hybridization, while the spin contribution vanishes due to spin conservation. To elucidate the low-energy description, we derive an effective Hamiltonian projected onto the $s$-orbital subspace using the Schur complement. We show that a naive application of the Kubo formula within this effective model fails to capture the ME response. This issue is resolved by systematically incorporating vertex corrections in terms of orbital hybridization into the response functions. Furthermore, by introducing a quasiparticle renormalization scheme, we formulate a renormalized Kubo formula that preserves conservation laws and accurately reproduces the full multi-orbital results. Our analysis revisits the conventional low-energy perspective and reveals that the ME effect originates from virtual interorbital processes encoded in vertex corrections, rather than from the bare low-energy Hamiltonian. The effective framework developed here provides a unified microscopic understanding of orbital-driven ME responses and offers a systematic route to incorporate hybridization effects beyond simple low-energy models.
